# Zoe-System — rules for Claude sessions

## Start here

- **Architecture:** ZoeW renders React state; features call domain operations through the `fb` adapter. Device Config selects Firebase **or** Supabase. There is no automatic dual-write. ZoeKeyGen is a separate administration app. The production action boundary is `ACTION_REGISTRY`; `core/runtime.ts` supplies the audit mirror.
- **Recovery:** retryable SDK updater callbacks are speculative, never commit proof. Capture operation identity and Reset epoch before effects; preserve unknown outcomes as durable pending evidence. Pickup additions require a proven committed history transition or proven prior membership. Never infer money reversal from missing history or release a registry claim before proven compensation.
- **Authority:** committed backend records and operation receipts decide outcomes. Browser journals preserve unfinished intent; listener snapshots supply views. A local queue entry cannot prove completion. Supabase uses tenant-scoped RPC/RLS; Firebase isolates customers by project. Recovery must retain the original session and inspect current server evidence before replay or compensation.
- **Business invariants:** preserve barcode identity across history, trash and the registry. Delete retains value; Remove reverses the counted barcode once; Restore reverses the proven deduction once. Local Close does not establish upstream Sign. Unknown evidence stays unresolved. Consult the scenario table and mode-specific restore policy below before changing either.
- **Working order:** verify branch, SHA and dirty files; inspect related current work; reproduce the failure; add a failing behavioral regression; make the smallest correction; rerun affected checks and document limits. Never replace a user's uncommitted work.
- **Commands:** install with `npm ci --prefix ZoeW`; use `npm --prefix ZoeW run typecheck`, `npm --prefix ZoeW run lint` and focused Vitest paths. Generate the audit measurement tree before extraction/browser checkers; [Runbook](#-runbook) defines environment, strict flags and publication conditions.
- **Boundaries:** this document supplies no authority to publish, deploy, change production data, disclose credentials or invoke real ZTO mutations. Resolve authorization from the active request. Preserve the locked gesture and ZTO constraints below.
- **Evidence:** label unrun, simulated and blocked checks accurately. A successful mock or an older CI revision cannot establish current backend/device behavior. Keep findings, rollout prerequisites and unresolved business decisions in [HISTORY](docs/HISTORY.md).

The sections below retain detailed operational constraints; the map identifies the material relevant to each change.

> Written for **Claude**. English by owner request; **everything else stays Khmer** (rule 7).
> ⛔ **This file holds only rules and prohibitions.** Bug history, measured numbers, versions and dates live in
> [`docs/HISTORY.md`](docs/HISTORY.md) (React era · pending user verification at the top) and
> [`docs/HISTORY-ARCHIVE.md`](docs/HISTORY-ARCHIVE.md) (older releases, including archived React rounds). Want the *why* of a rule? `rg` its tool name
> there (index at the end of `docs/HISTORY.md`). `doc-scope-test` fails on any version number or date outside the header
> table below. A rule states a required constraint; its tool column names related guards, not proof of complete coverage.
> Open violations and unmeasured cases belong in the pending section of `docs/HISTORY.md`; documentation alone never marks them fixed.

## ⛔ The one lesson

**Writing a lesson down is not enforcing it.** Bug found ➜ **build the tool first** ➜ prove it fails on the pre-fix tree
➜ only then fix the code. Rules with a tool are short because **the tool is the memory**; rules marked 📝 have no tool —
only this text protects them.

## 🗺️ Map

> Sections in file order. One rule lives in one place; other places reference it (rule 12).

| Section | Read when |
|---|---|
| The one lesson · Warnings for every audit round | **every round, before touching code** |
| What the system is | first round of a session · before touching an app, backend or tool you have not read |
| Non-negotiable rules · Forbidden zone · Locked zone (ZTO) · Accepted by design | **every round, before touching code** |
| Core table · Rules carried by specific checkers | **every round** — find the tool that locks the area you touch |
| ZTO status · auto-close · sign-list sweep | before touching ZTO verdicts · auto-close · the signed-list sweep (locked zone) |
| Core business rule | before touching money · trash · pickup stats · cleanup |
| Architecture | before touching UI · gestures · CSS · CSP · scanner · clock |
| Network · Service Worker · License | before touching connection · listeners · Service Worker · ZTO · License |
| Checker discipline | before writing or trusting any checker |
| Runbook | start of round and before commit |
| Expected error patterns | when you see an error in Sentry or console |
| References | where history, usage docs and the checker catalog live |
| Pending user verification | «📌» at the top of [`docs/HISTORY.md`](docs/HISTORY.md) — when the user asks "what should I test?" |

Start with the current request, verified checkout and pending items. Session-specific permissions and restrictions in old history
are evidence about that session; resolve today's authorization from the active conversation before publishing or contacting live services.

## 🛑 Warnings for every audit round

1. **`run-all.sh` green ≠ no bugs.** Green proves only that *measured* bugs didn't return. The same bug returning through
   several doors = broken **structure**: count **identities** (sets) instead of **numbers**. Always ask "does this number
   have an identity?"
2. **Bugs live in gaps tools don't watch**: stubs accepting everything (fake SDK never rejects like real rules) · states
   never entered (ledger smaller than the delta · stale local view · a `fetch` that **hangs** · a promise resolving
   **after** the ceiling) · seams between two files (writer locked, reader locked, nobody checks they agree).
3. **Ask "what is *not yet* measured?"**, not "which checker is red?". Methods: fake SDK in **real-rules reject mode** ·
   dependencies in new failure modes (hang · slow success · absent) · fuzz outside the default seed range:
   `FUZZ_RUN0=100 FUZZ_RUNS=14 FUZZ_OPS=50 node audit-tools/revenue-fuzz-test.js` (deeper runs find **new** classes;
   they don't protect old ones) · compare sibling paths (two apps · two backends) on their **failure modes**, not their
   shape · `grep` every caller of a shared resource · surfaces added *after* a rule (re-derive its scope from code) · a
   queue walked in the same order every round starves its tail · a test ceiling must be tighter than the backup door's
   ceiling, or a mutation of the main door survives.
4. **Review deleted lines in every fix**: `git diff "$BASE_REF" -- ZoeW/src ZoeW/public ZoeKeyGen | grep '^-'` — **an
   unexplained deletion is a regression** (step 1 of 6 of the impact verification, Runbook step 3).
5. **Don't change verified areas on suspicion** (Forbidden zone). **Measurement is evidence, reading code is not.**

## What the system is

**Two PWAs**, separate Netlify sites: **ZoeW** = **React + TypeScript + Vite** (build step); **ZoeKeyGen** = vanilla JS.

| App | Role | Current version | Sentry tag |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ZoeW** | Business app — parcels, COD/DOD, Locker positions, stats, Export, Excel import · also an **Android app** (Capacitor) · backend **Firebase or Supabase** per Config | `2.50.176` (`zoew-v440`) | `zoew` |
| **ZoeKeyGen** | Seller tool — create/Revoke/Extend Activation Keys, Setup Link/QR · card "🏪 ហាង Supabase" (shops · invite codes · password-reset codes · devices per shop) · **separate Firebase project** | `2.24.31` (`zoekeygen-v142`) | `zoekeygen` |

- ZoeW code lives in `ZoeW/src/**` (the single hand-edited source; same function names and storage keys as vanilla ZoeW)
  and builds to `ZoeW/dist/`. Where this file says `ZoeW/app.js` · `index.html` · `sw.js`, the code is `src/**` · JSX ·
  `src/sw/sw.ts`. Docs: [`ZoeW/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`](ZoeW/docs/ARCHITECTURE.md) ·
  [`ZoeW/docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`](ZoeW/docs/DEVELOPMENT.md).
- 🔬 **`audit-tools/` measure the audit build** (`ZoeW/scripts/build-audit.mjs` ➜ `ZoeW/dist-audit/ZoeW`: text view
  `app.js` from TS · rendered `index.html` · `sw.js` · bundle exposing functions on `window` only there).
  ⛔ `bash audit-tools/run-all.sh` builds it and runs checkers on `ZoeW/dist-audit/measure-root`; ⛔ extraction/browser checkers that expect those generated files
  fail **directly on the source checkout** with "code not found" (not an app bug). Repository-only guards use their documented source-root contract; do not invent generated files to make a check pass.
- ZoeW's own guards (tsc with `noUnusedLocals` · eslint · vitest incl. the forbidden-zone lock · purity · native · android · rules) run via
  `zoew-suite-test.js`; none of them needs git history.
- 💰 `check-money.cmd` (`money-reality-check.js`) reads **`audit-tools/money-core.js`** generated by
  `npm --prefix ZoeW run money:core` ⛔ regenerate when money code changes (freshness guard fails otherwise).
- ⛔ React is in production (`git cat-file -e origin/main:ZoeW/src/main.tsx`). Real iPhone + Android testing
  ([`ZoeW/docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`](ZoeW/docs/DEVELOPMENT.md) «Deploy preview · ឧបករណ៍ពិត · ការថយក្រោយ») can't be done on this machine.
- 📱 **Android (Capacitor)**: `ZoeW/android/` · [`ZoeW/docs/ANDROID.md`](ZoeW/docs/ANDROID.md). ⛔ Web **fails closed**:
  `src/platform/native.ts` is the single decider · plugins load by dynamic import only on native · web/iOS unchanged.
  ⛔ PTR on Android native goes through `setupIOSPullToRefresh()` (rules: «Pull-to-refresh»).
  Guards: `npm run android:check` (APK version = `APP_VERSION` · appId · permissions · logo · ⛔ `MainActivity` requests
  the highest-Hz mode — don't remove · ⛔ phones (`smallestScreenWidthDp` < `TABLET_MIN_WIDTH_DP`) stay portrait and tablets rotate (`MainActivity.orientationFor` in `onCreate` before `super` and in `onConfigurationChanged` · ⛔ no `screenOrientation` in `AndroidManifest` — it would lock tablets too · installed Android PWA: manifest `portrait-primary` + `applyOrientationLock()` asks `any` on tablets, the same 600 bound · iPhone: WebKit gives web apps no orientation lock · `ZoeW/tests/orientation-lock.test.ts`) · splash: ⛔ no `android:background` · no title/ActionBar · `postSplashScreenTheme` =
  `BridgeActivity` theme · icon = vector `drawable/splash_icon.xml` from `icon.svg` ⛔ not `@mipmap` · ⛔ permission
  `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` (without it `navigator.onLine` is always `true` and `online`/`offline` never fire) · web never
  loads native code · Gradle/AGP/SDK ↔ Capacitor template · ⛔ APKs signed by **one keystore forever**, cert pinned in
  `ZoeW/android/release-cert.sha256`, no debug-key path, ⛔ keystore **never in the repo**) · release workflow: push builds
  `main` only (Release `zoew-android-v<APP_VERSION>`); another branch only by manual Run workflow ➜ **Pre-release**
  `…-test.<7-char commit>`, never a plain Release; a test APK builds the web bundle exactly like a Release (no test-only flags · `repository-contract-test` runs the workflow's real scripts) · `npm run native:check`
  (fake bridge: Back · history · pause/resume · Share/Print · biometrics · PTR/latch) · `npm run rules:check`
  (delete/remove · 2h · 7d · 2d · 30d on web · Android; Android's final DB = web's). ⛔ Back never returns into "remove" mode
  (`safeScreen()`). Back on a root screen (`ROOT_SCREEN_MODALS`: login · activation, both `noDismiss`) minimizes the app; other
  `noDismiss` modals still eat Back (`tests/native/native-back.test.tsx`).
- ⚛️ **React 100%**: React is the single DOM owner ➜ feature code (`src/core` · `domain` · `features` · `services` · `ui` ·
  `platform`) writes **state/refs only**; `src/app/**` writes DOM only through exits (`refs.ts` · `DocumentEffects`).
- Scan beep uses `playBeep()` for every scan path. Android uses the local `ZoeScanBeep` plugin: prepare/reuse the tone engine on load/resume, play a continuous bounded tone, coalesce calls while it plays, and release on pause/destruction. Maximum media volume applies only during the tone; restore afterward while preserving a user's intervening volume change. Never change call volume, fixed-volume devices or DND policy. Web/older-APK fallback uses a full-scale ramped Web Audio tone with node cleanup, one pending resume per context, and no overlapping tones or replay after context replacement; PWA cannot override the device volume or mute. Guard: `ZoeW/tests/native/scan-beep.test.ts` runs the Java lifecycle against audio/handler stubs plus the real JS dispatch.
- **No roles in the business app** — every signed-in user (`auth != null`) has the same rights. ZoeKeyGen's `admin` role
  lives in its own License Project.
- 🏪 **Two backends per device Config**: Firebase (`databaseURL`) ➜ one Firebase project per customer · Supabase
  (`supabaseUrl` · `supabaseKey` · `loginDomain?`) ➜ **one project, many shops** (`tenant_id` + RLS · sign-up by **invite
  code** bound to a ZTO branch). `initFirebase()` loads chunk `supabase-backend` by dynamic import only when Config has
  `supabaseUrl`; `firebase-loader.js` is the single decider for the Firebase SDK ⛔ no static `modulepreload` of it in
  `index.html` (`npm run smoke`: Supabase Config ➜ 0 SDK requests · Firebase ➜ 3); the adapter (`src/services/supabase-*.ts`) exposes the Firebase `fb` surface ➜ ⛔ money/listener code
  **never branches on backend**. ⛔ **RTDB rules are the single source**: `firebase-database.rules.json` ➜
  `node supabase/scripts/generate-rules-sql.mjs` writes a **new** `<timestamp>_zoe_rules.sql` ➜ Publish on Firebase **and**
  merge to `main` (Supabase GitHub integration applies new migrations) or paste the new file in the SQL Editor.
  ⛔ Migrations in `main` are append-only. Supabase has no Activation Key (shop status · `ensureAppActivated()`);
  `owner`/`member` are labels only. Setup: [`supabase/README.md`](supabase/README.md).
  Both backends are supported customer choices. Keep Firebase support when developing Supabase; migration is optional per customer.
- Other dirs: `audit-tools/` (catalog: [`audit-tools/README.md`](audit-tools/README.md) section 6 ⛔ don't copy counts) ·
  `zto-import/` (Apps Script) · `tools/zto-cookie-sync-windows/` · `tools/firebase-provision/` (new customer project ·
  rules to all customers · [`README-KH.md`](tools/firebase-provision/README-KH.md)) · `firebase-backup/` · `supabase/`
  (migrations · Edge Functions · rules generator; not Netlify) · `tools/money-check-windows/` (money check on Windows) ·
  `tools/supabase-migrate/` (optional Firebase ➜ Supabase move per customer) · `tools/actions-runners/` (self-hosted runner kit ·
  [`docs/SELF-HOSTED-RUNNERS.md`](docs/SELF-HOSTED-RUNNERS.md)) · `.github/workflows/` (`audit.yml` · `backup.yml` · `android-release.yml` ·
  `android-shell-check.yml` · `opencode.yml`; repo variable `ZOE_RUNNER_MODE` = `github` ➜ GitHub-hosted runners; anything else ➜ self-hosted labels
  `wsl-zoe-audit` · `windows-zoe-android` while the repo is private, skipped on a public repo (no CI evidence) · `backup.yml`
  and `opencode.yml` always run on `ubuntu-latest`).

## ⛔ Non-negotiable rules

1. **Real customers and real money (COD/DOD).** Never merge to `main` without an explicit user request.
2. **"Delete" vs "Remove" is a business rule, not a bug** — read "Core business rule" before touching money code.
3. **Shipped app source has no explanatory comments**: app JS/CSS, `ZoeW/src/**`, Netlify code, build configs,
   hand-maintained Gradle and shipped HTML. The enforcement scope is derived by `comments.js`/`ts-comments.js`;
   a file outside that scan is not an exemption for new shipped code. Tests (`ZoeW/tests/**` · `*/test.js`),
   `audit-tools/`, development-only `ZoeW/scripts/**`, third-party `vendor/`/`qrcode.js`, declaration files,
   Capacitor-generated files marked "DO NOT EDIT" and the Capacitor template text of Android resource XML and
   `proguard-rules.pro` retain their necessary comments. Keep TypeScript triple-slash
   `reference`/`amd-module`/`amd-dependency` directives. For source changes finish with
   `node audit-tools/strip-comments.js` (token/declaration/compiled-output equivalence); for documentation-only work use
   its `--check` mode, which writes nothing (including in the Runbook commands). `comments.js` checks the result. Empty `catch` is intentional
   (`allowEmptyCatch`); rules and change history stay in their designated documents.
4. **`license-verify.js` and `error-reporting.js` are byte-identical in both apps** (`cp` + `md5sum`).
5. **Never claim git/branch/merge state without verifying** (`git rev-list --count origin/main..origin/<branch>`). Git is
   evidence, this file is not.
6. **Version only apps whose shipped behavior changed.** `APP_VERSION` identifies the app release; `CACHE_VERSION`
   identifies its matching Service Worker shell. Keep the app/manifest/HTML values aligned through their real source
   and build paths. `version-bump-scope.js` enforces both versions against the verified base; neither a cache-only
   bump nor a version-only app edit substitutes for a real change. See rule 8 for the release obligation.
7. **Language.** `CLAUDE.md` is English. **Everything else is Khmer**: chat replies · commit messages · PR titles/bodies ·
   other docs · user-facing app text. Code identifiers English. ⛔ **No Thai script (U+0E00–U+0E7F)** anywhere in repo
   text, not even examples (third-party `vendor/` and `*.min.js` excepted). ⛔ Number ranges in app text never mix Khmer and Latin digits. ⛔ In-app text (`index.html` ·
   `guide.html` · messages · release notes in `announcements.json`) describes **current behavior in present tense** —
   never compares with old versions and never mentions removed things (real data status like "this parcel is no longer in
   the system" is fine). Guard: `doc-scope-test`.
8. **A shipped app change bumps `APP_VERSION`** (PATCH for fixes) **and its `CACHE_VERSION`** (which apps: rule 6)
   and adds that release to `docs/HISTORY.md` part 1 in the same commit with the required manual actions. The core-table
   Versions row defines shipped scope, including native Android and build dependencies. Documentation, tests,
   `audit-tools/` and server-only changes do not force a client release; record such work in history part 2.
9. **README files describe **usage only**.** Every README has **5** sections in order:
   **កំណែ · មុខងារ · របៀបប្រើប្រាស់ · ប្រព័ន្ធសុវត្ថិភាព · អាជ្ញាប័ណ្ណ**. ⛔ No bug history or per-version notes. Never copy
   assertion counts into a README. Checked every round: root · `ZoeW/` · `ZoeKeyGen/` · `audit-tools/` ·
   `tools/zto-cookie-sync-windows/` (and `README-ANDROID-KH.md`) · `tools/firebase-provision/` · `tools/money-check-windows/` ·
   `tools/supabase-migrate/` · `firebase-backup/` · `zto-import/` (and `google-sheets-api/`) · `supabase/` ·
   plus two usage docs without the 5 sections (content rules apply): [`ZoeW/ZTO-SETUP-KH.md`](ZoeW/ZTO-SETUP-KH.md) ·
   [`docs/SELF-HOSTED-RUNNERS.md`](docs/SELF-HOSTED-RUNNERS.md) (in root `docs/`; the root `docs/` exception covers only the two
   history files) — the README list is derived from real dirs. A stale README is a wrong
   document. ⛔⛔ **Scope is every `*.md` file in the repo**: bug history lives only in `docs/HISTORY.md` and
   `docs/HISTORY-ARCHIVE.md` — `HISTORY.md` = React era (all new entries) · `HISTORY-ARCHIVE.md` = vanilla era + React
   migration + history moved out of `HISTORY.md` (read; new entries never go there) · no third history file. Exceptions:
   root `docs/` (history files) and `CLAUDE.md`
   (rules only — rule 12). ⛔ `ZoeW/docs/` is not an exception. Usage docs describe current operation, not bug-version
   narratives or dated requests/measurements. Current-version tables and installation requirements must match their real sources.
   File lists are derived from real dirs. Guard: `doc-scope-test.js`.
10. **Firebase rules don't deploy automatically.** Every new path gets its rule in the same commit, and **tell the user to
    paste it in Firebase Console ➜ Publish**. Files: `firebase-database.rules.json` (Business) ·
    `ZoeKeyGen/firebase-database.rules.json` (License). Business alternative: `tools/firebase-provision/deploy-rules.cmd`
    (still a manual action to report). License: paste. For app writes needing new rules, prepare each affected backend
    **before** merge: publish Business rules on its Firebase projects, or apply the newest Supabase `*_zoe_rules.sql`
    (merging deploys the app at once). Measure the old client's whole operation: a rejected pickup write does not prove
    that history or money writes stopped. If compatibility fails, require an owner-approved coordinated upgrade with no
    mixed-version operations; preserve unresolved journals, drain confirmed writes, and verify every affected tab/device
    before resuming business. Never force reload or clear storage to finish an upgrade. ⛔ Never pause shops through
    ZoeKeyGen for a rollout: a Key Revoke stops no database write and deletes the Activation record of every device that
    checks (staff re-enter the Key after «សង្គ្រោះ»); closing a Supabase shop signs every device out and refuses its writes
    for good.
11. **⛔ Don't touch PTR · history-panel motion · scroll smoothness without an explicit request.**
12. **`CLAUDE.md` holds only rules and prohibitions.** ⛔ No bug stories, "measured in x.y.z" numbers, versions or dates
    here (except the header table) — they go to `docs/HISTORY.md` (part 2 for bugs and measurements); history that is no
    longer useful moves to `docs/HISTORY-ARCHIVE.md` or is deleted. ⛔ `rg` before adding anything to `CLAUDE.md` or
    `docs/HISTORY.md`; extend existing rules — duplicates become contradictory rules; delete duplication when you see it.
    ⛔ Other docs **reference** these rules, never copy them. A new core-table row only when the rule gets a **new guard**.
    `doc-scope-test` fails on versions/dates in this file outside the header table and on the same ```` ``` ```` block in 2
    files (`docs/` exempt). ⛔ No duplicate writing (owner request): no 10-word phrase repeats on two lines of this file ·
    `docs/HISTORY.md` «📌» holds only open items (≤ 80 lines; a confirmed or done item is deleted ⛔ never a chain of old
    branches/PRs — git is the evidence) · one `### [X.Y.Z]` heading per version and one `A<n>` id per finding across both
    history files · part 1 newest version first · part 2 newest date first · the 🔎 index is one sorted table.

## ⛔ Forbidden zone — PTR · history-panel motion · scrolling

- Verified on **real iPhone and Android**. ⛔ Never "fix" on suspicion, code reading or WebKit theory. The right fix for
  snap fighting motion is **pausing snap**, never removing motion.
- Green `run-all.sh` proves only the scenarios run. Guards: `gesture-test.js` · `panel-motion-test.js` ·
  `ios-panel-glide-test.js` · `panel-snap-ownership-test.js` · `phone-search-swipe-test.js`.
- ⛔ Locked text: `ZoeW/tests/forbidden-zone-lock.test.ts` pins the sha256 (LF-normalised) of `src/app/behaviors/pull-to-refresh.ts` ·
  `panel-motion.ts` · `panels.ts` · `chrome-autohide.ts` · `phone-search.ts` · `src/ui/chrome-autohide.ts` · `src/ui/page-nav.ts` ·
  `src/styles/app.css` ➜ any byte changed = red. After a change allowed above (behavior guards green, both real devices), put the new
  sha256 in `LOCK` and record the request in `docs/HISTORY.md` ⛔ never weaken, skip or delete the lock.
- Change only on a **real user report** (video or precise description). If you must: read the 5-point table below,
  **measure** iOS vs Android directly, and test on **real devices of both** before merge.

## ⛔ Locked zone — ZTO list · sign sync · status

- Share only pending reads within the exact credential, deployment and tenant boundary; keep each waiter’s deadline and evict settled/failed work. Fresh credential rotation must bypass pending older reads. Overlap independent reads, but preserve primary-auth renewal budget and gate sibling renewal on primary success. Status prefetch is bounded; apply verdicts and business writes in order, recheck session/network ownership before scheduling and after awaiting, and settle pending reads on exit.

- Locked at the owner's request after two verification rounds (races · gaps · duplicates): `ZoeW/netlify/functions/zto-order-detail.mjs` · `ZoeW/netlify/functions/_shared/zto-order-detail-core.cjs` ·
  `src/features/zto-list-sync.ts` · `src/features/zto-status.ts` · `src/services/zto-shop-sweep.ts` · `src/services/zto-sweep-lane.ts` · `src/features/auto-lookup.ts` · `src/features/lookup-api.ts` ·
  `src/app/components/zto/model.ts` · `zto/ZtoListSyncBody.tsx` · `modals/ZtoListSyncModal.tsx` · `src/features/zto-palm-login.ts` ·
  `ZoeW/netlify/functions/_shared/zto-palm.js` (the `LOCK` keys are the list). `ZoeW/tests/zto-lock.test.ts` pins each file's sha256 (LF-normalised) ➜ any byte changed = red.
- ⛔ Change only on an **explicit owner request** or a real owner report — never on suspicion, code reading, refactors, renames or "cleanup"
  (a shared-helper change that would touch these files needs the same request). Then: a guard that fails on the pre-change tree ➜ fix ➜
  every ZTO guard green ➜ put the new sha256 in `LOCK` and record the request and reason in `docs/HISTORY.md`.
- ⛔ Never weaken, skip or delete a ZTO guard (`zto-list-sync-test` · `zto-sync-banner-test` · `tests/zto-signed-sync.test.tsx` ·
  `tests/zto-lock.test.ts`) to get past the lock. Netlify env (`ZTO_LIST_SIGNED_SCAN_TYPE` · `ZTO_LIST_SCAN_TYPE` …) is configuration, not code.

## Accepted by design — don't report as bugs

- **Users can write revenue/pickup numbers directly** (these paths do not recompute and validate deltas or aggregates on a trusted server).
- **"Inspect element" protection can't be hardened** (`devtools-guard` is an obstacle, not protection) ⛔ don't try.
- **`zto-import/google-sheets-api/Code.gs` is a template** — repo edits don't change the deployed script (paste into
  script.google.com and deploy a new version by hand).
- **Netlify Base directory lives in the UI**: `zoew` ➜ **`ZoeW`** · `zoekeygen` ➜ **`ZoeKeyGen`** (**case-sensitive**).
- **Clearing site data loses License `seenMax`** — grants nothing without the server (`activate()` needs
  `checkOnline().ok === true`).

⛔ The first four are intentional acceptances (three of them carry a 📝 row) — don't build tools for them unasked.

---

## 🔒 Core table: rule ➜ tool that locks it

> **📝 = no tool** ➜ build a tool before touching that area. **3** 📝 rows remain in this table — all intentional
> acceptances (derived from the table; `doc-scope-test` part 6 locks the count). Existing guard names do not establish
> that every operation, retry or session transition has a scenario; inspect those scenarios before claiming coverage.

Jump to [checker/release](#checker-release-and-repository-contracts), [money/recovery](#money-barcode-identity-and-recovery),
[network/listeners/SW](#network-listeners-and-service-worker), [backend/auth/secrets](#backend-authentication-and-secrets),
[ZTO](#zto-authentication-status-and-sign), or [UI/scanning/notifications](#interface-scanning-imports-and-notifications).

### Checker, release and repository contracts

| Area | Short rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|
| **Tools** | A checker must **be able to fail** (empty dir ➜ none green); ⛔ an `ok()` that takes only a label must never be called conditionally | `checker-coverage.js` |
| **Tools** | ⛔ A green set is not evidence — every money class needs a guard that goes *really red* | `money-guardian-test.js` |
| **Tools** | A checker must fail **within a time limit** (hang ≠ fail). Meta-checkers run children **in parallel under a ceiling**; a child that exhausts its budget while poisoned = FAIL · ⛔ never fix overruns by raising `CHECKER_TIMEOUT` | `hang-guard.js` |
| **Tools: `run-all.sh` runner** | Bounded parallel lanes, output in list order · `emu/*` + `money-guardian` one at a time · checkers touching `.tmp-poison-*` (`checker-coverage` · `exit-code-integrity`) run alone · lanes derived from source both directions (new emulator/shadow checker ➜ `runall_lane()`) · `RUNALL_STATE` written as each checker ends · `RUNALL_RESUME=1` refuses another tree (hash + `*_STRICT`) · unknown `RUNALL_ONLY` ➜ refuse; skipped/partial checks and selected runs never print "all passed" · TERM leaves no orphans · `RUNALL_SHARD=k/n` depends only on list + `RUNALL_HINTS` (no overlap/miss) · `audit.yml` matrix = 1..n · CI STRICT flags ⊇ Runbook step 0 | `runall-runner-test` · `hang-guard` |
| **Tools** | Every checker runs normally and as baseline; dump-needing CLIs have a fixture checker; CI and runner agree | `checker-coverage.js` |
| **Every repo file** | New files need a mapping in `repository-file-coverage.json`; stale entries/missing guards fail; integrity/manual kinds are not behavioral coverage | `repository-file-coverage.js` · `repository-contract-test.js` |
| **Reports to send** | The real CLI finds money bugs identically before/after redaction; never claims "sent" when redaction fails | `money-reality-test.js` |
| **Tools** | Every sandbox dependency exists in real scope; smoke runs the real collected path before the emulator gate | `emu/crud-rules-flow.js` |
| **Tools** | Failure must reach the **exit code**. ⛔ Assertions that never run also exit 0 (awaiting a promise nobody resolves drains the event loop ➜ exit 0) ➜ structural fix: `process.exitCode = 1` at the top, only the summary lowers it | `exit-code-integrity.js` |
| **Tools** | `pageerror` doesn't see promise rejections — watch `unhandledrejection` | `checker-coverage.js` part 8 |
| **Tools** | Poisoning happens on shadow files · no fixed shared resources: `listen(0, '127.0.0.1')` · `emu/*` namespace unique per run | `checker-coverage` |
| Shared helpers ×2 apps | Byte-identical except a reasoned list | `shared-fns` |
| **Same logic twice in a file** | One formula lives in one place. Before unifying, measure free variables and keep real differences as parameters. Detection must be structural and keep property names (`.cod` ≠ `.dod`) | `code-duplication-test` |
| `fb.X` not exported by the loader | Silent `undefined` in production | `sdk-surface` |
| Versions | `app.js` ↔ `manifest.json` ↔ `index.html` per app · bump only changed apps · server code (`netlify/` · `tools/`) is not shipped code · Android native code (`ZoeW/android/app/src/main/**` · `capacitor.config.ts`) ships in the APK ➜ a change there bumps `APP_VERSION` (a new Release needs a new version) and `CACHE_VERSION` (the PWA delivers the matching `version.ts` only through a new shell) · ZoeW `package.json` counts only beyond `version` and scripts outside the release closure (Netlify `command` · workflow `npm run … --prefix ZoeW` · install lifecycle · `pre`/`post` hooks; no entry found ➜ every script counts) | `version-check` · `version-bump-scope` |
| **Toolchain ↔ shipped output** | CSS minifier is esbuild (`cssMinify`; Lightning CSS reorders declarations covering PTR/motion) · built JS parses in `build.target` · chunks split by `codeSplitting` + `priority` (`__vitePreload` must stay out of the native chunk) · Android config stays on the installed Capacitor's template line | `npm run smoke` · `npm run android:check` · `npm run native:check` |
| **New Firebase project tool** | One project per customer · sign-up disabled **and measured** by a real sign-up attempt · Auth settings and rules read back · ⛔ never silently adopt (`--adopt`) or reset passwords (`--reset`) · `pendingProject` recorded before creation · step ceilings · passwords never in files · email ↔ `siteCodeFromEmail()` · Setup Link ↔ `decodeSetupPayload()` · `firebase-tools` pinned · measured with real `firebase-tools` over HTTPS | `firebase-provision-test` |
| **CLI braces backport** | Firebase CLI pin unchanged · local `braces` backport (upstream source + depth guard; versions/provenance in vendor metadata) · parser braces/parentheses ≤100 · compile/expand/stringify validate AST depth/cycles before recursion · preserve literals and glob semantics · packed file dependency installed by npm ci even with ignore-scripts · actual chokidar-resolved bytes match vendor · retain MIT license/provenance · audit zero alone is no evidence for vendored code | `firebase-provision-test` |
| **Netlify config** | ⛔ No root `netlify.toml` (read by both sites) · CSP · `functions` · headers match what the app ships | `netlify-config-scope-test` |
| **Document scope** | READMEs and `ZTO-SETUP-KH.md` usage only · history only in `docs/HISTORY*.md` · `CLAUDE.md` rules only (no versions/dates outside the header table) · no duplicate writing (rule 12: repeated phrases · «📌» ≤ 80 lines · unique version headings and `A<n>` ids · history order · sorted index) · current-version claims derived from code (incl. `CACHE_VERSION`) · checker lists derived (guide words · `listReadmeFiles()` · UI surfaces · 🩺 rows · `collectedValueOf()` screens · 📝 count · every `audit-tools/*.js` in its README · `money-reality-check.js` section counts) · removed surfaces never live in docs (storage keys exist in shipped code) · new surfaces appear in `guide.html` (`licenseFailureMessage()` · `LICENSE_SEAT_SLOTS`) · every `run "emu/…"` named in the Runbook degradation paragraph | `doc-scope-test` · `user-guide-test` |
| **Netlify Base directory** | «Accepted by design» (Netlify UI only) | 📝 |

### Money, barcode identity and recovery

| Area | Short rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|
| Delete vs Remove | `isDeducted` is the settled money flag; interrupted operations also require ledger-event evidence (see Core business rule) | `policy-test` · `revenue-fuzz` |
| **Duplicate barcode ↔ money** | Save only with a real `'claimed'` verdict. The registry writes constant `true` ➜ after `disconnect` a server `true` can't prove we claimed ➜ `claimBarcodeInRegistry()` returns `unknown` on `txOutcome: 'applied'` unless the result carries `txProven` (Supabase: an `ok` reply to the resent `op_id` proves this device's CAS) | `duplicate-money-test` · `tx-outcome-test` part 5 · `emu/tx-disconnect-emu-test` part គ · `ZoeW/tests/registry-claim-proven.test.ts` · `emu/supabase-adapter-parity` |
| **Merge into a gone item** | `mergeBarcodeIntoHistoryItem()` seeing a `null` server item writes a **fresh item** (`freshHistoryItemFrom()`: `id` · `phone` + the merged barcode only · `createdAt` = that barcode's) ⛔ never the stale local item — `null` is also what the SDK hands back on a hash mismatch after another device removed the last barcode or deleted the item (the old barcodes would return open while their trash copy is deducted ➜ deducted twice · counted twice in «ចំណូល (យករួច)»); the cold-cache first run proposes the same fresh item and the SDK reruns with the server value ➜ normal merge · the Supabase adapter (`res.value ?? null`) is the same door · the ZTO import lanes go through it too · the fresh item reuses an id whose trash slot may still hold the old lifecycle ➜ cleanup, Delete and Delete all of it never drop or overwrite (slot rules in «Hang ≠ fail») · a whole claim whose trash write failed every retry goes back to history: absent ➜ the claimed item · recreated meanwhile ➜ its barcodes merge into that item (`restoreClaimedItemToScanHistory()`: dedupe by code · `isDeducted` stripped) ⛔ never keep the current item without them | `ZoeW/tests/merge-into-deleted-item.test.ts` · `revenue-fuzz-test` (`other:remove` on the last barcode) · `concurrent-scan-test` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-foreign-slot.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/clear-history-foreign-slot.test.ts` |
| **Row money totals** | `barcode` = 1 parcel · `item` = row = one customer on one day (`addOrUpdateEntry` merges `phone`+`scanDate`: an open row into the open item · a born-closed row (`closedAtMs`) into an all-closed item · `isClosed` derived from every barcode, item `closedAt` = latest barcode stamp · «គ្មានលេខ» never merges) ➜ `item.count` = parcels. `item.cod/.dod/.price` = sum over barcodes (when non-empty) — **on the server too** (`initDatabaseListeners` recomputes `price` on read and hides server errors that reach Excel) | `item-money-integrity-test` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-born-closed-merge.test.ts` |
| **Values rules reject** | Clamp negatives **before writing** (memory = server) | `revenue-rules-clamp-test` |
| **Clamp ↔ revert** | "apply ➜ revert" is a true inverse: revert the delta the **server applied**, never the requested one | `ledger-clamp-symmetry-test` · `emu/ledger-revert-emu-test` · `revenue-rules-clamp-test` |
| **Revert after a failed apply** | Verdict `null` = not applied ➜ nothing to revert (never fall back to the memory delta); in-memory revert is idempotent | `ledger-failed-apply-revert-test` |
| **Pickup stats** | Count barcode keys and distinct phone owners, never increment derived mirrors blindly. Every daily writer advances `pickupRevision`; Reset advances `pickupResetEpoch` and clears keys/phones/packages. Metadata promotion preserves legacy counts. Capture the original epoch before effects, read revision before history/trash truth and CAS that revision at the write boundary. Rules reject deletion/downgrade/old writers after promotion. A newer Reset cancels only the pickup suboperation; repair the independent collected mirror before retiring evidence. Per-entry `zoew_pickup_journal_v2:` storage is admitted before effects and holds the creator Web Lock before becoming visible; unknown, foreign unlocked and legacy v1 evidence stays HOLD without expiry/eviction. Start and confirmed history transition are separate durable phases; speculative updater callbacks never authorize new additions. A live authoritative committed history ACK admits its known transition, including the compatible legacy path without a receipt. After a crash or missing local permission, require an exact committed `pickupTransition` bound to intent, actor, scope, date, epoch, barcode lifecycle, result and changed-key count in the same history CAS, or exact close-plan replay followed by a real commit ACK under the rules below. When proven closed keys expand `allowedAddKeys`, clear prior `pickupDone` atomically with proof admission; an acknowledged empty set cannot complete those new keys. For unresolved recovery, absent own proof permits only exact-plan replay or fully proven foreign completion under the rules below; unknown legacy or unproven conflicts stay pending. Restore clears the marker and Reset still blocks old pickup additions. Do not count a pending queue as completion or delete journals to upgrade/rollback. | `pickup-barcode-identity-test` · `pickup-ledger-test` · `revenue-rules-clamp-test` · `money-guardian-test` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-protocol.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-pending-ui.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-interrupted-close.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-mirror-durable.test.ts` · `supabase-money-multishop-test` · `emu/money-multidevice-emu-test` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-repair-truth-contract.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/remove-pickup-key.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-transition-proof.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-transition-rules.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-close-plan.test.ts` |
| **Orphan registry keys** | A failed release is queued and retried; deferral has a second exit (view arrival) · a flush decides every pending key against one owned-key set built once from history + trash (`ownedRegistryKeys()`) ⛔ never one history + trash walk per key · the set is rebuilt on every flush (never cached across flushes) | `registry-release-test` · `ZoeW/tests/registry-flush-scale.test.ts` |
| Trash · `trashReason` | Label ≠ money decision. Group totals derive from `TRASH_REASON_META[r].deducted` and keep cents (`renderTrashSummary()` run, user-visible number read) · owner request: a tap on a summary card («➖ ដក + ត្រឡប់» · «✅ យករួច + លុប», real buttons) lists only that bucket (`toggleTrashReasonFilter()` · `trashGroupInFilter()` on the same `deducted` basis · tap again ➜ all · `uiState.trashReasonFilter` cleared on close and logout) — display only: both cards keep the search totals, the total line counts the shown bucket, 🔄/✖️ still act on one `id` | `trash-modal-test` · `restore-marker-hygiene-test` · `ZoeW/tests/trash-reason-filter.test.tsx` |
| **ZTO final ↔ reopen · restore** | `syncZtoFinalMarks()` writes real signed/returned evidence per item, separately from money, skipping active history/trash claims; rejected schema writes back off per database. `ztoSignedAt`/`ztoSignedExact` use the timestamp policy below. Closed signed barcodes cannot reopen. Trash uses the mode-specific policy under "Trash and `trashReason`": ZTO Login always blocks; any signed barcode blocks all reasons, including delete/remove and forwarded/misplaced. Cached live Sign evidence must be checked before the left-branch exception and inside the claim transaction; the durable flag fences both backends. Unsigned non-Palm age-cleanup/forwarded exceptions remain eligible. Newest trash first. | `ZoeW/tests/zto-final-marks.test.tsx` · `emu/supabase-rules-parity-test` · `money-cookie-blob.js` · `ZoeW/tests/lookup-modes-money.test.ts` |
| **Shop-wide trash mode** | A ZTO device keeps its own Lookup mode; a device without ZTO Lookup follows the strictest mode stamped in `zoew_settings/zto_trash_mode` within `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_ACTIVE_MS` (`shopZtoTrashMode()` · `effectiveZtoTrashMode()`): Palm over Cookie · each install stamps only its own key `palm/<device>` · `cookie/<device>` (server basis · `ztoTrashModeDeviceKey()`: a random key per shop in `zoew_trash_mode_device_v1`, never a License or device identifier; storage refused ➜ session store ➜ page memory) and the mode is the newest per-device stamp of any device ⛔ never one shared stamp a leaving device can delete · no marker or `permission_denied` ➜ local · listener pending/failed ➜ `''`, so automatic cleanup and restore wait · a stamp more than `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_FUTURE_MS` ahead counts as active until pruned (fail closed) · manual Delete · Remove · Delete all use the last mode resolved for the same `shopScope()` (`getManualZtoTrashMode()`), else local · a ZTO device stamps its key once its own server stamp is older than `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_MARK_GAP_MS`, sending ≤ 1 per `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_RETRY_MS` (send gaps minted only by `ztoTrashModeGapStamp()`: `Date.now()` + `elapsedSince()`; the stamp value is `getServerNow()`) · a Lookup change withdraws this install's own key of the mode it leaves, unconditionally (`storeLookupApiConfig()` ➜ `withdrawZtoTrashMode()`; modes read by `ztoTrashModeOf()` ⛔ never through `getLookupApiConfig()`, which binds an unbound setting to the current shop) ⛔ never another device's key; a withdrawal lost to the network stays an intent (`zoew_trash_mode_withdraw_v1`, per shop, ≤ `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_WITHDRAW_MAX`) resent from the refresh ≤ 1 per retry gap, never while one is in flight, dropped once its key is gone or the install uses that mode again · the in-flight withdrawal guard belongs to one session (database + `authGeneration`): a withdrawal hung across a backend teardown or a re-login never blocks the next session, and a late answer of the old session never releases the new one · stale stamps of any device (older than `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_ACTIVE_MS` or beyond `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_FUTURE_MS`) are pruned with a CAS on the value seen, ≤ `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_PRUNE_MAX` per pass, rotating, only on a trusted server clock (`pruneZtoTrashMode()`) · every write needs a marker snapshot of the database that serves the stored shop now (`ztoTrashModeWritable()`: not initializing · SDK available · connected, never queued offline · the noted database · the active backend `auth.app.options` names `shopScope()`) · every trash-mode write is an advisory transaction (`txAdvisory: true` · `txOutcomeWitness` ➜ `not-applied`), honoured on both backends: never the money signal and never journaled, so never a blocked or replayed row (Firebase: its journal row carries no CAS and is dropped on any failure, a failure is `not-applied` at once; Supabase: no journal row) · a `permission_denied` (rules not yet published) pauses trash-mode writes to that database for `ZTO_TRASH_MODE_REFUSED_RETRY_MS` with one Sentry `zone: 'data'` per session ⛔ never a `palmAt`/`cookieAt` write (the rules reject both keys; a leftover in the database is never read) ⛔ a refusal that lands in another session (database · `authGeneration` · signed-in account) neither pauses nor reports; one that lands after the device left that mode still pauses and reports once (the rules refused that database) · the listener is generation-gated and re-attached from the 60s cycle and `visibilitychange` (`retryZtoTrashModeListener()`) · rules: per-device keys 8–32 characters, stamps > 0 and ≤ now + 600000, `$other` (`palmAt` · `cookieAt` included) rejected | `ZoeW/tests/zto-trash-mode-shop.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-trash-mode-devices.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/session-switch-fuzz.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-journal-recovery.test.ts` · `emu/app-writes-rules-test` · `ZoeW/tests/setup-lookup.test.tsx` · `clock-hygiene` |
| **Restore CLI intent** | A fresh apply invocation plans against current server state and gets a fresh intent identity. Batch IDs bind intent, tenant, index and content; network retries reuse the exact RPC arguments. Always verify final contents and concurrent writes; cached acknowledgements alone are not restoration proof. | `supabase-data-tools-test` |
| **Reset pickup stats** | Node remains with `0` · honors filter · never touches money · waits while the pickup view is stale (`pickupStatsAreMeasurable()` ➜ «⏳ … មិនទាន់ Sync», nothing written: its days come from that view), and the pickup counts it resets (data page summary · monthly report picked · customers · rate, screen and export) show `—` meanwhile · capture SDK/database/auth generation and an operation owner; check before each day write, in transaction retries, after awaits and before releasing busy/UI state. Phone edits transfer only existing counted identities in the transaction, including late success and rollback; never recreate keys cleared by Reset. | `pickup-reset-test` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-reset-session.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/phone-pickup-stale-view.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-stats-measurable.test.ts` |
| 2h/8d cleanup | Walks **barcodes**, not parcels · the app's doors (`runScheduledCleanup()` · `debouncedRenderAfterHistorySync`) start ≤ `CLEANUP_SWEEP_BATCH` cleanups per call and continue after `CLEANUP_SWEEP_YIELD_MS` until one lap (`cleanupSweepVisited`) has visited every item; only a cleanup that really started counts (in-flight · held · journal-full items cost nothing) ➜ a stuck or failing head never starves the tail ⛔ never one synchronous pass over every ripe item from those doors · a call without a limit keeps the full pass | `partial-pickup-cleanup-test` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-sweep-batch.test.ts` |
| **Cleanup labels ↔ constants** | User-facing text is read from the constants | `trash-modal-test` |
| **Locks ↔ hung trash writes** | `settleLockWithin()` on the **lock** — ⛔ not `dbOp()` on the write (that reverses money wrongly on late landing). A stuck lock kills the 2h/7d rules for that parcel | `stall-lock-release-test` |
| **Locker assignment ↔ claim** | `assignLockerToEntry()` has the `clearClaim` gate like the other write paths (rules only protect deletes). ⛔ No gate for restore markers (restore paths keep the Locker) · a parcel without a prior position is assigned at once, even into a position holding another customer's open parcel (owner decision: a confirm there slows the work); only a parcel that already has a position asks before moving (`locationWarningModal`, occupant noted) · the same position ➜ «រួចហើយ» toast · a result (late commit or failure) arriving after an auth/database switch belongs to the old session ➜ never patches the new view, never ✅/❌ | `locker-claim-guard-test` · `ZoeW/tests/locker-scan-direct.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/locker-assign-session.test.ts` |
| **Dead `clearClaim`** | `releaseStaleClearHistoryClaim()` is the single releaser: decides on the server view inside a transaction · never touches live claims or this device's (`activeClearHistoryClaims`) | `stale-clear-claim-test` |
| **Late commit** | Hang ≠ didn't happen — post-commit work runs when the commit arrives, **in the session that started it**: `claimAndCleanupItem()` and `deleteSingleItem()` capture `db` + `authGeneration` and stop (Sentry) before every later write once either changed; `txResolveOutcome()` re-checks the session after its REST read (a switch ➜ unread `unknown`, never `applied`) · a retry is a new write: the trash-write retries of cleanup · Delete · Remove and the retries of `restoreClaimedItemToScanHistory()` refuse (`noRetry`) once the session changed, and Remove's restore-after-failure runs only in its session · journal resume (`resumeCleanupJournalEntry()` · `resolveCleanupSlot()` · `settleCleanupDeduction()`) captures the session at its start and stops after every `await` that returns into another session (journal kept for its shop) · so do Remove (from its start: a late commit after a switch stops with Sentry money) · Restore (its finalize retries too) · permanent delete · Delete all (its loop and finalize retries) · the trash purge (incl. its late registry/key release) · the pickup repair · **every write operation must join the session-switch fuzz scenarios**: the reply to its k-th Firebase call arrives after a macrotask that switches shops (k = 1…N), also with its w-th write failing (w = 1…W, so every retry loop runs), then zero writes may follow ⛔ a new write operation joins the fuzz's scenario list (a baseline run must write) ⛔ capture the database, SDK, account and generation before a multi-step operation; re-check ownership before each iteration, transaction updater, retry and post-await state change (Reset loops included), never read live `firebaseState.db` to continue an old operation (Supabase: another account in the same project = another shop) · journal entries of another shop/account retain their exact evidence; release stale captured in-memory owners, do not replay or erase foreign entries and do not emit a false dropped-entry alarm · a session-switch alarm (`Remove stopped…` · `Cleanup stopped…` · `Delete stopped…`) stamps its journal entry `switchReported: true` (device storage) ➜ another shop's resume preserves it without a second alarm, and a trash-write failure after a switch reports only the switch ⛔ never two alarms for one operation · Remove's own `stale` ledger stays silent (`ZoeW/tests/remove-stale-session.test.ts`) | `late-commit-test` · `ZoeW/tests/late-commit-stale-session.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/trash-retry-session.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-resume-session.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/session-switch-fuzz.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-mirror-durable.test.ts` |
| **Durable writes · receipts · journal** | Every `fb` write on Firebase goes through `withConnectionOwnership()` ➜ `createNativeWriteRecovery()`: `set`/`update` become one atomic root update carrying `zoew_write_receipts/<opId>` = `{ uid, at, hash }` (rules: created only by its `uid`, never overwritten; a device deletes only its own receipts older than `NATIVE_RECEIPT_RETENTION_MS`, and `purgeReceipts()` does so once per session after a clean recovery, ≤ `NATIVE_RECEIPT_PURGE_MAX` ⛔ never a receipt that lives forever) · every transaction journals its CAS (`zoew_connection_writes_v1:*`, `queuedAt` on the server clock) before the send and drops it after the commit · on the next boot `recoverRow()` decides each row: a receipt row ➜ receipt with our hash = applied · absent = resend (inside the window, past it ➜ blocked) · another hash or uid = blocked; a ledger intent row ➜ witness or replay (`ledgerJournalWitness()`); a plain CAS row (no `op` token) is decided by the value alone — equal to the sent value = applied, anything else = not applied ➜ dropped either way (the pre-journal semantics; a stuck or foreign value never blocks the device) · a plain CAS row whose recovery read the rules refuse (`txReadWasRefused()`) is dropped like a receipt row the rules refuse to read (nothing to decide, and a value-only row never blocks); a ledger-intent row whose read is refused still blocks · an advisory transaction's row carries no CAS and is dropped on any failure (`mutate()`), never `blocked` · a plain CAS transaction cut in session is dropped the same way (owner decision: the boot rule above, never `blocked`; the wrapper still reports its `unknown` as Sentry money, and cleanup · pickup journals keep deciding the money), while a receipt-bearing write or ledger intent still blocks · a read that fails on the network ➜ `deferred`: writes wait and the read is retried every `NATIVE_RECOVERY_RETRY_GAP_MS` while online ⛔ never `blocked` · `permission_denied` on a receipt-bearing write ➜ read its receipt first: ours (uid + hash) = applied (the SDK resends a write whose acknowledgement was lost and the create-once rule refuses the copy) · absent or refused to read ➜ the write fails and its row is dropped · unreadable on the network ➜ `write_outcome_unknown` (`txOutcome: 'unknown'`, row kept for a recovery pass and marked `refused`: ours = applied · absent = dropped ⛔ never resent, the server refused it while the scan settle may already have reverted its money) ⛔ never resent through the plain SDK without its receipt · a scan save answered `txOutcome: 'unknown'` is inspected on the server (`settleRejectedSave()`): a matching code and captured `createdAt` in its row or a moved history/trash copy can prove saved. A competing or unproven lifecycle latches HOLD; its later absence never becomes proof authorizing compensation. Failed/refused/unreadable evidence remains pending across restart. `settleScanLater()` may retry only in the captured live session with bounded read gaps; a proven original failure can compensate only the applied delta and release the registry after the compensation ACK, never on a clamped, failed or unknown inverse. Durable per-operation scan evidence is admitted before effects, retained without expiry and shown as unfinished until terminal proof is safely stored. A transport journal draining does not prove the full business operation reconciled · a receipt the rules refuse to read ➜ row dropped · blocked storage ➜ connection recovery may proceed without its own journal; destructive cleanup still requires the separate persisted before-image barrier · a malformed row is skipped, never poisons its scope · a journaled transaction failing in session: an SDK abort reason (`maxretry` · `set` · a server status) is definitive ➜ row dropped; a `disconnect` keeps the row and a recovery pass decides it once the operation lock is released (`reverify()`) ⛔ never a bare `blocked` for a live failure (`ZoeW/tests/native-live-failure.test.ts`) · a write refused before the send carries `txOutcome: 'not-applied'` ⛔ never `unknown` (Supabase journal full ➜ `write_journal_unavailable` (upper-case in `err.code`) on transactions and set/update alike, nothing sent · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-journal-full.test.ts`) · `blocked` (contradicting receipt · undecidable money intent · row past the window) stops every write on that db (a write refused by it answers `write_recovery_pending` ⛔ never `CONNECTION_SCOPE_CHANGED`) until a recovery pass decides it (reload · the next pass after a live failure; 🩺 is read-only and tells the owner to Refresh) or the owner's exit: ☰ ឧបករណ៍ ➜ «លុបការសរសេររង់ចាំ» (`drawerDiscardPendingWritesFlow()` · shown only while `blocked` · PIN `discardPendingWrites` · a confirm) ➜ `fb.discardPendingWrites(db)` drops every journal row of the current backend/user/shop (Supabase: rows of this account + shop only) except a row a live write of any tab holds (Web Lock `zoew-write:<opId>` · `zoew-sb-write:<opId>`, taken `ifAvailable`), records Sentry money with the paths, re-runs recovery and never replays what it dropped ⛔ never an automatic drop of an undecidable row. Supabase: a `sent`/`cancelled` row left by a sign-out is replayed under the same account + shop with its `op_id` inside `SB_OP_REPLAY_SAFE_MS` ⛔ never left blocking the next session · a plain CAS row (`sbPlainCasRow()`: CAS ops only · no ledger `op` token · no companion intent) follows the Firebase plain CAS rule: a definitive error after a lost reply, a replay past the window or a replay conflict drops it (the transaction still answers `unknown` + Sentry money) ⛔ never `unresolved`/blocked · a write refused by a blocked recovery removes its `queued` row (no phantom replay). Scope leases (`captureConnectionOperation()` · `capture()`): discovering the shop (`''` ➜ tenant) ends nothing, only a change between two shops does · `fetchWithTimeout(…, { scope: 'page' })` (version feed · seller notices) is never bound to the session · ⛔ the companion protocol below covers retained ledger intents, not every acknowledged-money/unknown-history crash: durable scan evidence preserves that unresolved business outcome for reconciliation; never infer complete automatic repair from a drained transport journal. For a retained intent: a `revenue-delta` intent whose data write is a separate transaction (scan save · price edit, both issue the ledger delta **before** the data write) carries a `companion` (`ledgerDeltaCompanion()`: item path · barcode · new price · prior price; the real price edit sends both and DOD decides like COD · `ZoeW/tests/price-edit-unknown.test.ts`) ➜ on boot (Firebase `runRecovery` · Supabase `recoverWrites`: companion rows are decided **after every other row**, so a resent item `update` lands first) the delta is applied **only** when the server barcode holds the new value (`ledgerCompanionState()` = `holds`, via a fresh read ➜ unreadable = deferred, never guessed) · applied already while the barcode still holds the prior price ➜ undone once (token `op_r…`, idempotent) · a barcode `absent` from its row is read from its own copy (the same code + `createdAt`, carried by the companion of a scan and of a price edit) in history or trash (`ledgerCompanionCopyState()` · moved by pickup cleanup · Remove · Delete · the 8-day rule; both roots read once per recovery pass, on Firebase within `TX_ROOT_READ_TIMEOUT_MS` ⛔ never once per row or within `TX_OUTCOME_READ_TIMEOUT_MS`: writes wait on the pass) and decided the same way ⛔ never «found nowhere» as proof the write never landed (a hand purge leaves nothing) · otherwise `absent`/`unknown` ➜ dropped without a write; Supabase never resends the original CAS of a companion row (a stale base would conflict while its sibling lands) ➜ a new transaction from the intent after recovery · Remove · cleanup · manual stats edits carry no companion (their delta follows a landed data write) and replay as before | `ZoeW/tests/ledger-replay-companion.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-ledger-replay-companion.test.ts` · `emu/money-multidevice-emu-test` · `supabase-money-multishop-test` · `ZoeW/tests/native-durable-write.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/native-recovery-plain-cas.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/native-lost-ack-resend.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/scan-save-unknown.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-trash-mode-devices.test.ts` (P1 controls) · `ZoeW/tests/native-receipt-retention.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/durable-connection-write.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-journal-recovery.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/connection-scope-discovery.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/connection-flow-contract.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/ledger-native-retry-identity.test.ts` · `emu/app-network-e2e-test` · `emu/supabase-rules-parity-test` (receipt rule probe) |
