Close goes through the history transaction first, then reconciles from server values · reopening deducts from the entry's day · day moves = one multipath update · price edits decided in server transactions on `<day>/<barcodeKey>` leaves only (`reconcileCollectedHistory(…, true)`: every day of the view + the retention window + the closing day, an absent leaf answers `null` so the server decides, older duplicates deleted after) ⛔ never a transaction on the whole root or a whole day (it re-sends and re-downloads every kept day on every device) · keep the newest day · never recreate cleaned keys: a reconcile writes no mark on a day before `collectedRetentionCutoffKey(getServerNow())` (a resumed or kept-stamp close of an old barcode only deletes) · Native SDK rejections retry within a ceiling. The memory mirror reads one listener only · never touches, decides or blocks `isDeducted`/ledger/`packagesPickedUp` · 7-day cleanup needs server clock + fresh view · drift is measurable only on a real dump (`money-reality-check.js` part 5ខ) · a hung mirror write (`dbOp` ceiling) makes `reconcileCollectedHistory()` return `'pending'` with «⏳ … Sync ដោយស្វ័យប្រវត្តិ» and the late write re-reconciles ➜ «✅ … បាន Sync» (callers show their own ✅ only on `true`, never on `'pending'`) · a close on this device holds the pickup cleanup of its row until its reconcile ends (`holdCollectedSettle()` · `collectedSettleHeld()` in `claimAndCleanupItem('close')`, ≤ `COLLECTED_SETTLE_HOLD_MS`, Map kept on `collectedSettleHolds` itself) · a barcode another device already moved out of `history/<id>` is read from its pickup trash copy (`trashReason: 'pickup'` from the deleted view, re-read on the server) ⛔ never from a `remove`/`returned` copy · a close or reopen rejected with any `txOutcome` except `'not-applied'` (at once or late) reconciles the mirror from the server (`healCollectedAfterFailure`) ⛔ never leaves a landed close without its record · crash-durable: the pickup journal entry carries its item (`item`) and stays until the pickup commit **and** the mirror reconcile answer `true` (`pickupIntentTracker(isCurrent, itemId)` · `finish(collected)`); `resumePickupIntents()` reconciles with `quiet` (no mirror toast on any path, `commitCollectedMarks(…, quiet)` included) · a Remove's mirror delete never gates money: the cleanup journal deducts and flips `isDeducted` first, then moves to stage `collected` (`finishCleanupCollected()`, pending outcome stored) and clears only when `dropCleanupCollected()` answers `true` — until then it is not counted as finished; a definitive refusal retries ≤ `CLEANUP_LEDGER_RETRY_MAX` rounds (`collectedTries`), then the entry is dropped with Sentry `zone: 'data'` ⛔ never a mirror-stuck entry filling `CLEANUP_JOURNAL_MAX` · the live Remove shows ✅ at once and keeps its journal entry (stage `collected`, live marker held) until its own mirror delete answers `true` · `dropCleanupCollected()` keeps the mark of a barcode found in fresh history or closed and not deducted in a `pickup`/`delete` trash copy (view candidates re-read on the server; stale views ➜ wait) ⛔ never deletes the mark of another lifecycle · the journal records revenue only for barcodes the live path deducts (Remove skips `isDeducted`) ⛔ never a resume that deducts what the live path skipped; its mirror drop still covers every closed barcode it moved (`cleanupDropsMirror()`: revenue · reason `remove` · `abandon`) · a Remove whose mirror delete lands after the live phase re-runs the keep check (`cleanupCollectedKept()`, shared with `dropCleanupCollected()`) and re-marks kept barcodes through `reconcileCollectedHistory(…, quiet)` (`remarkCleanupCollectedKept()`) before clearing the entry · mirror recovery re-marks a kept, closed, not-deducted barcode only when its leaf is missing and its day is inside retention; a history holder with restore markers skips that restore's added codes (every code without valid provenance) ⛔ never re-mark or keep what an unfinished restore inserted · read failures retry uncounted, refusals count toward `CLEANUP_LEDGER_RETRY_MAX` · a mirror step that returns into another session writes nothing and answers `false` · the pickup journal `item` reaches a path only through `pickupJournalItemId()` | `daily-collected-test` · `emu/restore-mutation-emu-test` · `money-reality-check` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-sync-pending.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-price-day-scope.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/pickup-cleanup-collected-race.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-mirror-durable.test.ts` |
| **Collected mirror ↔ ledger** | Remove (row 4) deletes the mirror entry · Restore (row 9) rebuilds it on the new `closedAt` day (`reconcileCollectedHistory()`, no second formula) · ⛔ Delete (row 5) never touches the mirror | `collected-mirror-lifecycle-test` · `collected-mirror-fuzz-test` |
| **Interrupted cleanup** | `claimAndCleanupItem()` writes 4 times ➜ journal `zoew_cleanup_journal_v1` written **before** network writes · ⛔ the journal never evicts an entry for room: a cleanup starts only while `cleanupInFlight` + journal entries < `CLEANUP_JOURNAL_MAX` (the rest waits for the next round; the check is a journal call ➜ fail-open) · `resumeInterruptedCleanups()` finishes later · ⛔ no server journal (an ownerless server marker is a permanent trap; the ledger key below only records a landed deduction and never blocks; the per-barcode `trashClaim` in the history item expires and its owner's next start releases it — «Hang ≠ fail») · ledger step after trash, as a **keyed event** (`deductCleanupLedgerKeyed()`): month then day transactions through `runLedgerTransaction()` carrying the deterministic token `ledgerEventToken(trashId, deletedAt, 'd')` as `op`/ring entry; the day writes `ded/<trashId>` = `{ at, cod, dod, count }` (what the server subtracted) ➜ a day key with the same `at` = both buckets deducted · a step that sees its key or token answers «already» and writes nothing · a month key marked back by a restore (`restored`) settles the deduction (`deductCleanupLedgerKeyed()` ➜ `deducted`) ⛔ never left open (the journal stayed and warned forever after another device restored an interrupted Remove) · every day-record writer carries `ded` (`ledgerCarryDed()`) ⛔ never rebuild a day record without it (the month writer too · `ZoeW/tests/ledger-writer-contract.test.ts`) · the journal stores `ledger: 'keyed'` + `prior` (tokens in the day/month records before the first send): `moved` ➜ deductible · `ledger` ➜ rerun the event: key/token ➜ already · a `prior` token still present ➜ never landed ➜ deduct · no proof (ring churned) ➜ don't touch money, tell the user ⛔ never rerun a deduction without proof · every keyed step carries `ded` + ring + token; rules refusing them ➜ the step fails (journal kept) ⛔ never a keyless or ringless resend · **the trash flag follows the money**: a `returned` trash item is written with `isDeducted: false` and flipped to `true` only after the ledger deduction landed (stage `flip` ➜ `markCleanupTrashDeducted()`, a transaction that waits while a live `restoreClaim` holds the item) ⛔ never write `isDeducted: true` before the deduction (a restore elsewhere would add back money never deducted) · stage `flip` with the trash gone ➜ the barcodes back in history (fresh views only, `cleanupBarcodesBackInHistory()`) = restored without add-back ➜ add the deduction back: keyed entries through stage `undo` (`undoCleanupRevenue()`: day first — key `back: true, undo: true` — then month with token `u` and `uprior`); a restore tombstone (`back: true` without `undo`) = the restore already added both back ⛔ never add back again · not back (purged) ➜ keep it · stage `moved` with the trash gone and the barcodes back in history ➜ no rewrite, no deduction · journal helpers tolerate storage errors, but a destructive claim must verify its persisted before-image and abort when unavailable (Network section) · key = trash id · `cleanupJournalScope()` uses the current shop identity · a **live** cleanup is not an interruption: `withCleanupEntryOwnership()` (`cleanupJournalLive` + Web Locks `zoew-cleanup-live-<id>`, fail-open) then re-read fresh · restore adds back by the day key when no barcode is flagged and writes the tombstone whenever it adds back with a live key (`restoreLedgerKeyFor()`) · a `returned` trash item with no flagged barcode is never restored while the daily ledger view is stale (`restoreAwaitsLedgerView()` ➜ toast, nothing written: the key cannot be read, and restoring without it leaves the deduction in place) · a modern `returned`/`remove` item whose barcodes are listed, none flagged, and that has neither receipt is refused as pending (`RESTORE_LEDGER_PENDING` ➜ «⏳» the deduction has not finished on the device that removed or cleaned it) ⛔ never the maintainer text for it; a flagged barcode or a single missing receipt stays `RESTORE_LEDGER_PROOF_REQUIRED` (`ZoeW/tests/trash-retry-session.test.ts` M7) · `runCleanupLedgerKeySweep()` flips unflipped `returned` and `remove` trash whose day key matches its `deletedAt` (older than `CLEANUP_KEY_SWEEP_GRACE_MS`, not journaled on this device) ⛔ never deducts · purge deletes the purged items' keys (`releaseCleanupLedgerKeys()`) · after `flip` a Remove or abandon entry moves to stage `collected`: no money work, cleared only by its mirror drop (row «Daily collected»), whose refusal cap drops the entry only after the money settled | `cleanup-interrupt-atomicity-test` · `money-guardian-test` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-deduct-order.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-journal-cap.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-ledger-key.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/collected-mirror-durable.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-remove-flip-sweep.test.ts` |
| **Destructive cleanup** | Needs the real server clock **and** a live connection **and** a known shop: a Supabase session whose shop is not known yet (`shopScopePending()`: restored session, account not arrived) runs no automatic cleanup and resumes no journal — shop-bound settings (the ZTO hold) are unusable and a journal scope can't be compared until then · a tab whose stored Config names another database than its live backend (another tab saved a Config) waits the same way, and cleanup and pickup journals and restore intents carry the database they write (`liveShopScope()`) ⛔ never the stored Config | `cleanup-clock-guard-test` · `ZoeW/tests/shop-pending-cleanup.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/cross-tab-shop-scope.test.ts` |

### Network, listeners and Service Worker

| Area | Short rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|
| **Accumulating listeners** | N real cycles ➜ listener and node counts never grow | `listener-leak-test.js` |
| **Transactions: `disconnect`** | `disconnect` = *not yet known* ➜ resolve it, never guess: `runTransactionResolved()` (wrapper on `fb`) reads the server via REST until it answers (gap 2 ➜ 30 s · offline resets the gap · stops only on an auth/database switch or `TX_OUTCOME_MAX_REFUSALS` HTTP 4xx refusals); Supabase CAS transactions resend the same `op_id` until definitive (stops only on `close`; a non-network error after a lost reply ➜ `unknown`); `zoe_ops` keeps a reply only 2 days (`private.zoe_housekeeping()`) ➜ a `conflict` on a resend more than `SB_OP_REPLAY_SAFE_MS` after the lost reply is no proof (a purged op re-runs as a CAS against its own landed write); the age is measured on the server clock — the conflict's `now` minus the last server `now` seen before the first send (`sentAfter`, a lower bound of the op's `created_at`) ⛔ never the device clock (a partial backward correction reads a purged op as fresh) · no `now` ➜ the witness path: decided by `updateFn.txOutcomeWitness` (ledger ring) else `unknown` ⛔ never `not-applied` (the ledger resend would deduct twice); an error raised before the POST (no token yet · the token-step ceiling · a failed refresh after a 401) carries `SbNetworkError.unsent` ➜ retried without marking a lost reply (close ➜ plain `disconnect` · conflict ➜ normal CAS retry, never `unknown`/`not-applied`) ⛔ never give up on a time ceiling (a guess deducts twice or loses a parcel). Live non-CAS set/update/increment replay after a lost ACK first reads `zoe_now`: server age since the original first-send bound must be within `SB_OP_REPLAY_SAFE_MS`, including zero. Invalid, backward or expired evidence retains a blocked journal and reports unknown; device time never authorizes replay. Known-unsent work may wait offline beyond that window and refreshes its bound before the first send (`tests/supabase-live-op-retention.test.ts`); every first send (write or transaction attempt) reads `zoe_now` first when the last server time seen is older than `SB_SERVER_NOW_FRESH_MS` on device elapsed time, so a frozen tab never sends with a day-old bound, and a conflict on a first send (row not `uncertain`) is definitive ➜ its journal row is removed (`tests/supabase-stale-server-time.test.ts`). Per-path gate (`txResolvingPaths`): a new wrapper transaction on a path whose outcome is resolving waits, released after `TX_OUTCOME_GATE_RELEASE_FAILS` failed reads while online. `applied` ➜ success · `unknown` ➜ Sentry money · a late cleanup (whole or partial) never duplicates trash (`claimCleanupTrashSlot()` create-if-absent · both backends). Every `unknown` (unread `txServerUnread`: refusals · switch · close · or read but undecided) ➜ ledger reconcile never reports `ok` (`ledgerRejectionVerdict()`). "Equals the sent value" is valid only for values unique to one writer ➜ ledger writes carry an `op` token and a ring `ops/<op> = seq` of the last `LEDGER_OP_RING_MAX` writers (`runLedgerTransaction()` choke point ⛔ never `fb.runTransaction` on the ledger directly; `permission_denied` ➜ the write fails as sent (never a resend without the ring or `op`; a keyed event keeps `ded` + ring + its token); a definitive `not-applied` ➜ resend, ≤ 3 times (`notAppliedRetries`) · ⛔ never resend on `unknown`). A read server that equals neither the sent nor the prior value is decided only by the ring (`ledgerOpWitness()` via `updater.txOutcomeWitness`): our token in the ring or `op` ➜ `applied` (snapshot = the sent value) · a token of the prior ring present without ours ➜ `not-applied` (eviction drops the lowest seq first) · absent prior + a seq-1 entry ➜ `not-applied` (seq 1 only on an absent record) · else `unknown` ⛔ never decide another writer's value by guessing. An advisory transaction (`updater.txAdvisory === true`: a write its own listener reports and a refresh repairs — the shop trash-mode marker · the branch sweep lane) is never resolved or reported and opens no path gate: on Firebase every failure but a rules refusal is `not-applied` at once (no REST read · no money signal; a session ending mid-flight may still reject with `CONNECTION_SCOPE_CHANGED`), a refusal stays a plain refusal; Supabase never journals it and never calls `onTxOutcomeUnknown` (a lost reply may still read `unknown`; nothing waits on it) ⛔ never `txAdvisory` on a money or data path (a write that landed would read not-applied). The wrapper delays `disconnect` ➜ paths queuing on it (`patchHistoryItemFields` · call marking) check `transactionDisconnectPending()` when `dbOp` times out | `tx-outcome-test` · `emu/tx-disconnect-emu-test` · `emu/supabase-adapter-parity` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-tx-outcome.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/tx-outcome-timeout.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/ledger-not-applied-retry.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/ledger-op-ring.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-op-retention.test.ts` · `revenue-fuzz-test` (`ledgerBlip`) · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-unsent-tx.test.ts` · `money-guardian-test` · `history-patch-retry-test` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-applied-ownership.test.ts` |
| **Aborted transactions** | `committed: false` ➜ the money reversal still runs (a throw in the success handler doesn't reach the failure handler) | `price-edit-abort-test` |
| Clock | Section «Clock» | `clock-hygiene` |
| **Rate ceilings ↔ clock** | Elapsed time via `elapsedSince()` (backwards ➜ fail-open). Stamps it measures are minted with `Date.now()` (a `getServerNow()` stamp ➜ `Infinity` ➜ ceilings dissolve), as variables or properties. Retention stamps (`deletedAt`) keep their minting and are measured as `getServerNow() - mark`. One property never holds two bases | `monotonic-gate-test` · `clock-basis-test` · `zto-sync-banner-test` |
| Connection · recovery | Failed listeners come back; the SDK recovers. A switch between Firebase and Supabase tears the old backend down before awaiting the other SDK (`tearDownBackend()`) ➜ a late or failed SDK leaves no live old session, and the SDK retry doors (`isDatabaseInitialized` gates) complete the switch when it arrives · phase `unavailable` is never shown as online. Reloading to recover the SDK first measures reachability of the SDK host (`navigator.onLine` lies) · CSP `connect-src` allows it · Supabase shops: the real app + real adapter leave green within the liveness ceiling when the server drops or hangs (idle cycle · background resume), reconnect alone and receive changes made meanwhile; realtime down ➜ the poll fallback still delivers · a channel that isn't `SUBSCRIBED` (`CLOSED` · `CHANNEL_ERROR` · `TIMED_OUT` · `subscribe()` throws) is re-created on `SB_REALTIME_RETRY_STEPS_MS` (one timer · reset on `SUBSCRIBED` · a channel that rejoins by itself is kept · statuses from torn-down channels are ignored) · the fallback poll timer is re-armed only when realtime health changes or none is pending ⛔ never on every status (a failing channel reports on each retry and would postpone the poll for its whole first minute) | `connection-recovery-test` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-poll-starvation.test.ts` · `netlify-config-scope-test` · `supabase-app-network-e2e-test` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-unsent-tx.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/supabase-realtime-ws.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/backend-switch-late-sdk.test.ts` |
| **Zombie socket** | `.info/connected` = `true` is no proof. `probeDatabaseLiveness()` decides: real round trip (`get()` on `DB_LIVENESS_PROBE_PATH`; any reply incl. `permission_denied` = alive); only a 10s timeout disconnects (`forceDatabaseReconnect()`). Doors: hung `dbOp`/scan claim · wake from background ≥ 30s · 60s cycle (visible, no round trip in 55s). Never probe while a listener is pending · ≤ 1 disconnect per 30s · slow but alive ➜ no disconnect | `emu/app-network-e2e-test` |
| **Listeners dying alone** | Siblings never announce recovery for a dead listener — `.info/connected` and `.info/serverTimeOffset` too | `connection-recovery-test` |
| **Stale callbacks** | Every `onValue` callback has a generation gate (`listenerGeneration !== dbListenerGeneration`) — old callbacks after a database/auth switch never write old data or call `noteDbListenerAlive()`. The ownership proxy drops a data row's callbacks once its uid · auth generation · stored Config change ➜ every session change re-attaches: a uid change without a signed-out snapshot (Firebase cross-tab sync) runs the signed-out view reset (`clearSignedInView()` + `clearSensitiveModalFields()`) before `proceedAfterLogin()` · `proceedAfterLogin()` and Activation attach unless the rows belong to the current generation (`dbListenersBelongToSession()`; a re-login after a sign-out the SDK refused keeps `isDatabaseInitialized` with dead rows), and a sign-in that attaches shows its live sign-in toast (the same check, never `isDatabaseInitialized`) · a Config saved in another tab (`storage` on `zoew_firebase_config`) re-runs `initFirebase()` (`followStoredFirebaseConfig()`) ⛔ never leave rows attached across a session change (the view freezes while the phase says `ready`). Measured over real `DB_LISTENER_KEYS`. A backend switch (`initFirebase()` teardown, both apps) unsubscribes the old auth listener, clears its 8s recovery timer and bumps `authGeneration` **before** `deleteApp()` — Firebase delivers a pending `onAuthStateChanged` after `deleteApp()` — and detaches the old listeners through the old SDK (`firebaseState.fb` takes the new SDK only after teardown) · `setupAuthListener()` keeps one recovery timer (`authRecoveryTimeout`) ➜ a second call never leaves the old one armed · its stuck-auth recovery (`attemptAuthStorageRecovery()` deletes the Firebase IndexedDB every tab of the origin shares) runs only while the tab is visible ⛔ never from a hidden tab (it waits for `visibilitychange`) | `connection-recovery-test` · `ZoeW/tests/registry-session-race.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/backend-switch-detach.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/session-listener-scope.test.ts` |

unsynced ➜ not granted, record kept: `Date` is no CORS-safelisted header, so the REST reply often cannot sync) · the "synced" flag comes only from the real server · post-login verification over 20s retries per `ACTIVATION_RETRY_STEPS_MS` (session switch aborts; exhausted ➜ guidance) · a non-2xx `license_keys` reply (5xx · 429) and a seat `PUT` lost to the network are `ok: null`, never `ok: false`/`seat-taken` (a lost reply after commit reads back as `mine`) · a seat read the server refuses (permission · HTTP refusal) is `seat: 'seat-unavailable'`, kept like `'unknown'` ⛔ never `seat-taken` (only a fresh read naming another device deletes); an offline ceiling that runs out while every seat read is refused answers `reason: 'seat-unavailable'` (the seller publishes the License rules) ⛔ never «connect to the internet» to an online device · `lastOnlineCheck` advances only on a known seat with `serverTimeSynced` (an unsynced device clock never extends the offline grace) · the signed Key payload carries no seller note · an Extended Key Activates on new or reset devices up to the server `expiresAt` and ZoeKeyGen's Extend dialog says so (never "only activated devices") · Extend is a transaction per app path on `expiresAt` (CAS on the value the list showed): the later of `expiresAt` and the server clock, plus the days ⛔ never an absolute date that overwrites a later expiry (`changed` · `missing` ➜ told, the list refreshes) · a reply lost after the send (`disconnect`) is decided by reading `expiresAt` back from the operation's database (`readExpiresAtViaRest()` · `databaseUrlOf()` ⛔ never the stored Config another tab may have changed: the value the updater writes = applied · the value shown = not applied · else or unreadable ➜ `ADMIN_WRITE_PENDING_TEXT`) ⛔ never «failed» for a write that landed (a retry adds the days twice) · one Extend at a time (`confirmExtendKey.busy`) · a Key listing no device but lacking `releasedAt` on an app is released stamp-only (an old-rules release deleted its seats) | `license-grace-test` · `license-clock-trust-test` · `ZoeW/tests/activation-retry.test.ts` · `license-clock-rollback-test` · `keygen-session-security-test` |
| **Key ➜ device ceiling** | **Rules decide**: seat `license_seats/<appCode>/<keyId>` writable only if absent and the Key exists, or same `device` · no client delete · no listing. `getDeviceId()` reads back · "can't identify" ➜ `ok: null` (only a real `seat-taken` deletes) · `activate()` needs `seat === 'mine'` · a seat read lost to the network (`seat: 'unknown'`) never refreshes `lastOnlineCheck` (the offline grace keeps running) · `getStatus()` checks the signed payload's `id` and falls back to its `exp` ⛔ never the editable `record.id`/`record.exp` · automatic reclaim carries the observed `releasedAt` as `releaseEpoch`; the slot write must match the Key's current epoch. A later release invalidates the activation record's acknowledged epoch even when a stale seat reads `mine`; legacy activation records use their `activatedAt` (absent = 0). A refused claim triggers a bounded Key reread: a confirmed release clears activation, an unavailable answer preserves the existing grace. Explicit activation acknowledges the current epoch, including server headers rounded to seconds. Seat `at` retains its binding-time meaning. Existing pre-epoch seats may edit metadata with device and `at` unchanged; an empty slot on a released Key needs the current epoch. Deploy the compatible License rules before either client; older apps need an update before reactivating released Keys · `checkOnline()` without `claimSeat` never writes · releasing devices is the admin's job: one root update deletes the seat and stamps `license_keys/<app>/<keyId>/releasedAt` (server timestamp; rules: number > 0 and ≤ now + 600000 on an existing Key) ⛔ never a seat delete alone while the rules accept the stamp (the released device would claim its empty seat again); rules not yet published ➜ the seat alone is released and the admin is told · ⛔ never loosen seats. Device **meta** (`license_seats/<app>/<keyId>/<slot>/meta` = `model` 1–80 · `platform` 1–40 · `serial` 1–64 · `at`; rules: only on an existing slot, `device` unchanged, `$other` rejected) is display only ⛔ never a seat decision: `checkOnline()` writes it only on the `claimSeat` path, for its own seat, when it differs (`noteSeatMeta()` · not awaited · inside `try` · a refusal stops it for the page) · ZoeW feeds `setDeviceMeta()` from `loadDeviceInfo()` (APK: plugin `ZoeDevice` ➜ maker + model · Android ID; web: `userAgentData` ➜ model · iOS version from `Version/` once it reaches the unfrozen major (Safari freezes the UA OS token) · a frozen OS token without `Version/` + a Safari 26 engine (`webKitAtLeast26()`) ➜ `iOS 26+` ⛔ never the frozen token as is, `appSerialOf()` = SHA-256 of the License device ID ⛔ never the ID itself, in DOM or meta) and shows it in ☰ and 🩺 (`DeviceInfoLine`, not a 🩺 row) · ZoeKeyGen shows one escaped line per seat. Ceiling `maxDevices` (admin-only · absent = 1) via fixed slots `d1..d5`; slot names agree across `license-verify.js` · `ZoeKeyGen/app.js` · rules; races move to a free slot (`LICENSE_SEAT_CLAIM_TRIES`). iOS: install the PWA **before** Activating | `license-seat-test` · `emu/license-seat-rules-test` · `license-app-code-test` · `keylist-consistency-test` · `ZoeW/tests/device-info.test.tsx` · `npm run android:check` 3គ · `npm run native:check` 4ច |

| **Supabase adapter ↔ Firebase SDK** | Differences live only in `src/services/supabase-*.ts`, which equals the real SDK (val · key order · deep update · increment · transaction · listeners · errors). Surface ⊇ every `fb.X` used · adapter-only names (`accountOf` · `tenantScope` · `registerAccount` · `resetPassword` · `noteDevice`) behind gates · lost replies reuse `op_id` · `txOutcome` `applied`/`unknown` · `document` via `platform/document-io.ts` · a multi-page full sync continues with `p_full_head` (a reset page never restarts forever; ≤ `SB_PULL_MAX_RESTARTS`) and fills a separate stage (`pullStage`) swapped in only on the last page — an interrupted full pull never shows its first pages as the fresh view; the swap keeps this device's writes newer than the snapshot head; a reset whose head is below the cursor before it (the seq timeline went backwards: a project restore) keeps only documents the server answered this device directly since the last complete pull (`answeredSeqs`: write and read answers) ⛔ never an old-view document above the new head (`ZoeW/tests/supabase-head-regress.test.ts`); no docs cache save while staged; `resetForSignOut()` drops the stage · a pull answering another `tenant` than the session's restarts from 0 (view · stage · cursor cleared, bounded by `SB_PULL_MAX_RESTARTS`) · the `zoe_docs` IndexedDB cache is egress-only: listeners fire only after a server answer · a cached delta applies only when the server's `tenant` equals the cache's · logout deletes it (`clearSensitiveModalFields()`) · a session is an epoch: a pull reply or write result arriving after `resetForSignOut()` belongs to the old session ➜ discarded, never applied to the new one (`sessionEpoch`) · queued writes and transactions belong to the account that made them (`authScope`): the same account after re-login ➜ sent; another account ➜ rejected, never sent, never shown · every tab of one account + shop shares the write journal ➜ a live write holds the Web Lock `zoew-sb-write:<opId>` from its journal row until it settles, and recovery and discard act only on rows whose lock they take `ifAvailable` (re-read inside the lock) ⛔ never send, undo or drop another tab's live row; a row nobody holds (its tab closed) is recovered · boot restore settles within `SB_RESTORE_CEILING_MS` (under the app's 8s `attemptAuthStorageRecovery`): a stored session whose refresh is slow or fails transiently restores the user (Firebase persistence parity — offline + expired token never lands on the login dialog) · a `SIGNED_OUT` from supabase-js itself (refresh rejected · another tab) ➜ `SB_SESSION_ENDED_TEXT` via `onSessionEnded`, never on the adapter's own sign-outs (`ownSignOut()`) · an account check skipped offline (`_accountUnverified`) reruns on `TOKEN_REFRESHED` or the next sync · sign-out never depends on the network (Firebase parity): best-effort server revoke under `SB_SIGN_OUT_CEILING_MS`, then the transport removes `SB_AUTH_KEY_SUFFIXES` itself; rejects only while storage still holds a session ⛔ never `client.auth.signOut()` (it waits on the refresh lock and can run after the next sign-in) · a session belongs to the tab that made it (`bindTo()` on sign-in · restore · own sign-out): a `storage` event on `zoew-sb-auth` from another tab removing it or naming another account, or a `getSession()` at `rpc()`/`accessToken()` that is gone or names another account ➜ the tab ends its session (`onForeignSession` ➜ `resetForSignOut()` + `SB_SESSION_ENDED_TEXT`) and sends nothing until it signs in again ⛔ never another account's token · never an anonymous RPC · never BroadcastChannel as the signal (any script on the origin can post) · `update()` above `SB_OPS_PER_WRITE` (the `zoe_write` cap ⛔ never raised in a migration) becomes separate atomic writes ➜ allowed only for idempotent payloads (`null` · absolute values); with `increment()` ➜ refused before anything is written · every `fb.update(` caller is classified BOUNDED or IDEMPOTENT (increments only inside BOUNDED) · `notify()` recomputes only listeners whose root changed: every change marks it where it happens (a server doc written · an overlay write queued or settled → its op roots; | **Supabase rules in Postgres** | The latest `*_zoe_rules.sql` equals `rulesSql(compileRules(…))` · verdicts equal the RTDB emulator · no cross-tenant access (RLS · `zoe_read`/`zoe_pull` · broadcast topics) · `zoe_write` locks the tenant before reading seq · one shop never slows or fills the shared project: write and read cost is linear in size — rules see the write's pre/post documents passed once (`p_ctx` · per-node scope `{vars, loc, nv}` ⛔ never a context rebuilt or a document re-read per node), root-level `exists`/`hasChildren` never materialize the root, documents merge in one pass (`zoe_merge()`), path overlap is a sorted adjacent check, ⛔ never `jsonb_agg`/`jsonb_object_agg` over document values (Postgres builds them in time quadratic in the size of each value) — objects are built from text (`zoe_json_object()` · `string_agg`) · a write touches ≤ 8 MiB of documents (`invalid_data` size) · a `zoe_pull` page holds ≤ 2 MiB of stored values, never splitting a seq group · per shop `zoe_tenant_state.bytes` (documents + tombstones + `zoe_ops`, kept exact by every write, housekeeping and the backfill) ≤ `quota_bytes` (default 1 GiB, raised by SQL): a write that grows the shop past it ➜ `quota_exceeded` (final); shrinking writes and `zoe_admin_write` pass · big-shop shapes (reconcile `update()` · whole-root `runTransaction` · 20 000-barcode documents · a full pull) finish under Supabase's 8 s `statement_timeout` · datastore mutations land on the live definition (lastIndexOf + a function/policy/grant digest that must change) · a `zoe_docs` row edited outside `zoe_apply()` (Dashboard Table Editor · SQL Editor) syncs like a write: `zoe_docs` triggers give an inserted or value-changed row the next shop `seq`, turn a deleted live row into a tombstone with the next `seq` and keep `bytes` exact ➜ the `seq` broadcast makes devices pull it ⛔ never a hard delete or a value change that keeps its `seq` (devices would keep the old row until logout) · internal writers stamp `head + 1` and are left alone · an update that changes no value (aging `updated_at`) keeps its `seq` · changing `tenant_id`/`root`/`key` is refused · a shop deletion's cascade and the tombstone purge are untouched · a fixture or repair that must keep its own `seq` sets `zoe.raw_docs = on` for its transaction | `emu/supabase-rules-parity` · `supabase-datastore-test` · `supabase-rls-test` · `supabase-app-network-e2e-test` (ញ) |

| Area | Short rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|
| **Locked zone — ZTO** | The locked ZTO files equal their pinned sha256 · change only on an explicit owner request, then update `LOCK` + `docs/HISTORY.md` | `ZoeW/tests/zto-lock.test.ts` |
| **7-day cleanup ↔ ZTO signed list** | ZTO auto-close on (`ztoAutoCloseEnabled()` + ZTO Lookup) ➜ `abandon` waits for a **fresh complete** signed read taken **after the parcel became ripe** (`ztoAbandonCleanupIsHeld(itemAbandonRipeAt(…))` · `ztoSignedCompleteAt`: measured · not partial · not truncated · every match closed · fresh `ZTO_SIGNED_FRESH_MS` · `ztoSignedCompleteServerAt` (server basis, stamped before the read) ≥ the latest ripe moment of the barcodes being abandoned (`barcodeAbandonBasis()` + `ABANDON_AGE_MS`)) so signed parcels close as pickup instead of expiring (deduct); the device holding a live `true` verdict of a barcode's lifecycle leaves that barcode out of its abandon claim (`abandonZtoSignedHolds()` ➜ claim option `hold`; unsigned ripe siblings leave at once; auto-close off ➜ nothing on the device closes it ➜ no hold). A close that fails while the barcode is still open keeps the round incomplete (retry: section «Sign-list sweep»). A wait episode starts at session start, after `ZTO_ABANDON_RESUME_GAP_MS` without checks (resume from background) or after a complete read, and ends after `ZTO_ABANDON_HOLD_MAX_MS` (never stuck) · signed list off on the server or a definitive not-configured reason (`ztoListReasonIsDefinitive()`) ➜ no wait · a device that cannot read because its secret is locked (`ztoStatusSecretIsLocked()`) holds without running the episode clock until it can read · Data Saver/2g never skips the sign read while a hold is active · every other failure (transient identity reasons included) backs off ≤ `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_FAIL_MAX_MS` < `ZTO_ABANDON_HOLD_MAX_MS` so a retry lands inside the wait · logout/Config change resets · the 2-hour rule never waits · ⛔ constants and `>` unchanged. The read after a gap covers back to the oldest open parcel (`ztoOldestOpenStamp()`, ≤ `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_MAX_DAYS`); the range narrows to 1 day (`ztoSignedSweepOkAt`) only after a **complete** round — a round that leaves matches (`more`) keeps the wide range. **Shop-wide**: settings are per device but cleanup acts on shared data ➜ ZTO devices write the shop marker `zoew_settings/zto_signed_sweep` `{ activeAt, completeAt }` (server basis) after each measured read (`src/services/zto-shop-sweep.ts` — the status module never touches Firebase · `markZtoShopSweep()` · ≤ 1 write per `ZTO_SHOP_SWEEP_MARK_GAP_MS` · `completeAt` = start of a complete read); a device without local ZTO auto-close holds `abandon` while `activeAt` is within `ZTO_SHOP_SWEEP_ACTIVE_MS` and `completeAt` < the ripe moment, or while the marker listener is pending/failed — same episode bound · no marker · stale marker (`ZTO_SHOP_SWEEP_ACTIVE_MS` ≥ hold ceiling + write gap, ≤ 2h: a hold helps only while a ZTO device reads) · `permission_denied` (rules not published) · `ztoSignedOff` ➜ no wait · a denied/failed marker listener re-attaches from the 60s cycle and `visibilitychange` (`retryZtoShopSweepListener()`, ≤ 1 per `ZTO_SHOP_SWEEP_MARK_GAP_MS`, stays non-holding until a snapshot) ➜ Publish works without a reload · stamps later than server now + `ZTO_SHOP_SWEEP_MARK_GAP_MS` are read as 0 (a ZTO device rewrites them) and rules reject stamps > `now + 600000` · only an advance seen from an `ok` state starts a new episode (a listener re-attach does not) · the marker listener is generation-gated but is not a data listener (never in `DB_LISTENER_KEYS`, never announces an outage) | `ZoeW/tests/zto-abandon-signed-gate.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-shop-sweep-marker.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-abandon-blocked-sweep.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-palm-multibranch-hold.test.tsx` · `emu/app-writes-rules-test` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-signed-open-abandon.test.tsx` |

the Argus host is learned from the request, other hosts are never counted; subscribers never throw) + `scans.argus` · `scans.palm` = scan time (request ➜ response headers) split by a new or a reused connection (a socket counts as new only for its first request · Argus scan = the `/detail` endpoint path · Palm scan = `BILL_INFO_PATH`) + the keep-alive ZTO announces (`keepAliveSec` · `closes`) · `runtime.region` = the Function's `AWS_REGION` (shape-checked, else `null`) · 🩺 prints the scan meter of its mode in seconds (`ztoUpstreamTimingText()`, Palm diag carries it too) and never changes the verdict | `zto-budget-test` · `zto-negative-cache-test` · `zto-proxy-test` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-upstream-timing.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-sweep-page-shapes.test.ts` |
| **ZTO returned ➜ trash «ត្រឡប់»** | Owner request: a barcode open in ZoeW that ZTO shows returned (own-site `-710` «ត្រឡប់ការស្កេន» at or after it entered ZoeW: `createdAt`, or `restoredAt` when the owner restored it later ⛔ never remove a restored barcode again for the same return) leaves at once the way the 8-day rule does: `removeReturnedZtoBarcodes()` ➜ `claimAndCleanupItem(id, 'abandon', { codes, returnedAt })` (`returnedAt` = return time per code, re-checked against the **server** barcode inside the transaction with `barcodeReturnCounts()` — a device whose view predates a restore never removes the restored barcode) (the same trash class `returned` · money and parcel count deducted through the same journal, keyed ledger event and flag-follows-money flow · only the named open barcodes, no age wait ⛔ never a closed barcode) · owner decision: the 8-day rule and the ZTO return are **one** category, shown as «ត្រឡប់» (`TRASH_REASON_META.returned.label`) ⛔ never a «ផុតកំណត់» label for parcels and never a second trash value (owner: «ដាក់ returned ទៅ» · «កែអោយស្អាតទៅ» — no `expired` in code, rules or tests) · an own-site send-out `2` at or after the latest own-site arrival, with no own return and not closed, leaves the same way (`ztoReturnedAt` = the send) as one of two kinds: misplaced (owner request «ខុសទីតាំង») only when an own-site problem scan whose `problemReason` is a wrong-location text (`MISPLACED_REASONS`: Khmer · Chinese · English, invisible format characters stripped) lies between that arrival and the send ➜ misplaced, else a plain send-out (owner: staff send returns with «ផ្ញើចេញ») ➜ `ztoSentOutAt`, evidence kind `sent`: label «បញ្ជូនចេញ» (owner request), restorable only under the mode/sign policy below ⛔ never «ខុសទីតាំង» from a send alone · on the wire a send carries exactly one label stamp beside `ztoReturnedAt`: `ztoSentOutAt` (plain send) or `ztoMisplacedAt` (misplaced) ⛔ never both (Palm `status=1` · `tracks=`; Cookie, read only (owner: «cookie … ដូច palm … តែមិនអាច write»): `sent=1` = the `ZTO_LIST_SEND_SCAN_TYPE` (`02`, `off` disables) list and `problem=1` = the `ZTO_LIST_PROBLEM_SCAN_TYPE` (`30`, `off` disables) rows whose reason is in `ZTO_LIST_MISPLACED_REASONS`, both over the companion's range, latest per barcode, read beside `returned=1` in the pull and the hourly sweep (`fetchZtoReturnedPages()`: a return in the window wins · `problem=1` only when the window holds a send-out (Netlify calls) · a problem scan at or before the send ➜ misplaced with `problemAt`, unless it predates the row's latest arrival (pull) or the barcode's entry/restore (sweep, `barcodeReturnCounts()`) ➜ an earlier cycle ➜ sent · the sweep reads `problem=1` from the day of the oldest open parcel (`ztoOpenSinceDay()`), not from its narrow return window ⛔ never «បញ្ជូនចេញ» for a problem scan that fell before the window · a problem list not `ok` (failed · partial · `off`) ➜ every send of that read carries `sentUnverified` ➜ evidence kind `left`: removed and restorable only under the mode/sign policy below like `sent`, label «ត្រឡប់», no stamp ⛔ never «បញ្ជូនចេញ» without a complete problem list · a return list (`-710`) failed or partial ➜ no send of that read is used (a send may follow a return the list missed ➜ it waits for a read whose return list is complete) ⛔ never classify a send without a complete return list · `sentState` failed/partial, a problem list failed/partial or a return list failed/partial ➜ ⚠️ in the note)) ➜ the same removal · `syncZtoFinalMarks()` stamps `ztoMisplacedAt` beside `ztoReturnedAt` for `misplaced` and `ztoSentOutAt` alone for `sent` (owner request «ដាក់ត្រា») ⛔ never `ztoReturnedAt` for a send (every App would 🔒 it) (rules accept both label stamps; a refused write fails as a whole and is retried after `ZTO_FINAL_FAIL_RETRY_MS` ⛔ never rewritten without its label stamp; device evidence keeps the label meanwhile) ➜ display labels «ខុសទីតាំង» · «បញ្ជូនចេញ» (`trashDisplayReasonOf()` · own trash groups · 🔔 📤 «📍» · «📤»; per barcode `barcodeZtoLeftKind()`: this device's evidence `sent` · `misplaced` · `left` first, then `ztoSentOutAt` before `ztoMisplacedAt`) · misplaced and sent-out items are restorable only under the mode/sign policy below (🔄 adds the money back; `ztoReturnedSweepAt` resets in `clearZtoPickupStatusStore()` ➜ a new session reads at once) · return evidence lives `ZTO_FINAL_RETURNED_TTL_MS` from when ZoeW learned it (`noteZtoReturnedEvidence()` · `noted`) ⛔ never from the scan time (a return seen after a gap still gets its 🔒 stamp) · a return of a barcode the 8-day rule already moved is noted for its `returned` trash item too and schedules `scheduleZtoFinalMarks()` ⛔ never only for open barcodes (the trash copy never got `ztoReturnedAt`, so no other device locked it) · quiet like the 8-day rule (🔔 removed list) · the App reads only `zto…` fields the Function or its Palm module emits | `ZoeW/tests/zto-returned-cleanup.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-palm-server.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-multibranch-signed-hold.test.tsx` · `revenue-fuzz-test` (`returned`) · `emu/app-writes-rules-test` · `zto-sync-banner-test` part ១០ · `trash-modal-test` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-returned-evidence-age.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-returned.test.tsx` |
| **ZTO Palm lookup** | `?palm=login` (POST) · `?palm=1` in the same Function, after the proxy key and `readConfig()` (`_shared/zto-palm.js`, deps injected by `palmDeps()`) · never reads or writes the Argus Cookie (`cookie` · `cookie-life`), arrival candidates use the fixed Argus `/scan/page/scan` endpoint with `scanTypeCode: "03"` and only the SSO inside the sealed Palm token (`PDA_USER_COOKIE_01`/`PDA_SSO_COOKIE_01`, never the Blob Cookie). Verify fixed-host `/getUserInfo` matches the Palm branch before every arrival page; reject a mismatched returned scan site/page, malformed total or truncated page. No redirects, configurable destination, credential echo, or money writes. SSO stays as the list authentication; temporary diagnostics must not become a second list implementation. Native track `action: "3"` and Argus list `scanTypeCode: "03"` are distinct API fields for arrival · a Palm preflight (`isPalmPreflight()`: query `palm` = `1` · `status` · `login` · `refresh`, or header `x-zoe-palm-token`) skips `prewarmCookieCredential()` · ⛔ ZTO protocol keys only in env (`ZTO_PALM_SIGN_KEY` · `ZTO_PALM_PASSWORD_KEY` 16 bytes · `ZTO_PALM_SESSION_SECRET` ≥ 32), never in the a scan (camera · scanner · photo) takes the photo, beeps and vibrates (`scanSuccessFeedback()`: `playBeep()` + `hapticScan()`; the audio context is primed in the tap that opens the dialog; iPhone web has no vibration API) and sends `04` for that barcode at once (`dispatchPalmSignCode()`, only once its status is known: not dispatched · not closed; unknown ➜ its status is read again) ➜ name ➜ «✍️ Sign (N)» commits ready receipts and their photos to the durable queue below with ≤ `PALM_SIGN_SEND_CONCURRENCY` local workers, without waiting for a receipt preflight · ✕ drops a scan (`removePalmSignScan()`) · problem scans and ZTO warnings show as collapsed `<details>` under the barcode) (`queryPreOrderByBillCode` · `newBusinessCheck` · `sign/log/v1/add` · `saveBillPic` `picType: 1` · `saveScanInfo` · `deleteByBillCode`) · the dispatch request carries no photo (the APK attaches none; the frame stays on the device as proof of the scan) · camera, manual input and hardware scanner all await the same photo capture; pending hardware captures are single-flight per barcode and fenced to the complete Sign operation identity · live photos freeze the barcode frame and Asia/Phnom_Penh server time before awaiting a fresh location fix (`capturePalmSignPhoto()`); never accept a timestamp-only frame while GPS is still pending. A fix must be ≤ `PALM_SIGN_FIX_MAX_AGE_MS` old and ≤ `PALM_SIGN_FIX_MAX_ACCURACY_M`; Android approximate permission is valid. Stamp with the bundled offline Roboto face (`loadPalmSignStampFont()`), pure red, bold, bottom-left and width-fitted from `PALM_SIGN_STAMP_SIZE` of image height like the reference Palm APK; no outline (`stampPalmSignPhoto()` is fail-open, including font loading). Gallery uses its original EXIF time plus GPS when both exist (`readPhotoExif()` reads up to `PALM_SIGN_EXIF_READ_BYTES`, offset converts to app time, absent offset keeps the camera clock, `0,0` means absent); otherwise stamp selection time/current location and explicitly label that source in the form. A fresh camera file preserves complete EXIF GPS/time; an older camera-picker file retains its original time and available GPS. Never fabricate coordinates when denied/unavailable: show the location state and retry guidance. Location has one shared watch with reference-counted owners and an epoch fence; closing the last owner clears it and cancels waiters. Already-granted GPS starts early; prompt/unknown waits for the camera request to settle (`palmSignCameraStart`) or the location ceiling, with a bounded permissions query and `noteAppLockExcuse()` before a prompt. Capacitor has one permission listener, so never request camera/location permission concurrently. High-accuracy timeout leaves room for one coarse fallback within the capture ceiling; stale callbacks from a closed form cannot change the next session · `Permissions-Policy` `geolocation=(self)` · APK `ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION` + `ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION` ⛔ never background location · receipt requires the dispatch proof sealed to session token · barcode · `requestId` (1 h) ⛔ never a client flag · `newBusinessCheck` text with `whetherForce: false` — at the dispatch `04` too, which carries no photo and no name (owner: real ZTO Palm asks there as well) — ➜ 409 `ZTO_PALM_SIGN_CONFIRM_REQUIRED` + `stage` + a token sealed to session · barcode · request · name · photo · proof · text hash (5 min) ➜ automatically replay the exact payload once with that barcode’s token and recheck ZTO; never infer approval from tracking text, another barcode, or an unknown response. | **Durable Sign queue** | User-visible labels say ZTO; Palm identifiers remain protocol-compatible. Scan/auto-dispatch → press Sign → encrypted local commit of every ready barcode, photo, name, request and proof → close the form and continue work. New jobs opt into automatic confirmation of a strictly validated `ZTO_PALM_SIGN_CONFIRM_REQUIRED`; the worker performs the authoritative preorder/business checks and persists the returned token with a renewed lease before replaying the exact payload once. A token already supplied cannot silently approve a changed warning. A job whose automatic confirmation is spent (`autoConfirm` not `true`) needs explicit approval. Hard rejection, malformed responses and uncertain writes never qualify for automatic confirmation. Client and server must use matching reservation and proof contracts: every receipt stage (status included) requires the reservation protocol (`ZTO_PALM_SIGN_PROTOCOL_REQUIRED` before any read) · a stored job without its reservation fields (`reservationProtocol` · `originalFingerprint` · `originalSHA256`) is unreadable like a foreign-key row · a server journal outside the reservation phases answers 503, never a guessed state. IndexedDB resolves only on transaction completion, uses strict durability, owner scope, AES-GCM, bounded capacity and revision leases; closing the app preserves committed jobs, but execution resumes only while open, unlocked and online. Unreadable encrypted rows stay stored and show a count; they never block other jobs, and a new Sign of the same barcode replaces a row the current key cannot open (`replaceSignJob()` on its revision; the form read ZTO status first). Implausibly future lease/retry deadlines recover after clock rollback, while valid deadlines still hold. Two workers maximum; each barcode keeps its own photo and request ID. A sealed server journal uses Blobs ETag CAS before photo/05 transitions; ordinary edge reads can be stale, so every mutating transition must win CAS, and a missing ETag fails closed. Never request unsupported strong reads in an edge-only Lambda context. Once 05 may have been sent, poll `receipt-status`; never blindly resend after a timeout. Photo verification requires this request ID and exact-original proof; never label a missing or different photo verified. The owner-requested local lifecycle permanently removes signed jobs after a trusted-server-clock retention deadline, based on authoritative matching signed evidence even without photo proof. An explicit confirmed manual delete removes the selected local job/photo/history, not parcel/accounting records or server audit evidence. Persist confirmed before the idempotent local close and remove the job only after DB commit; optimistic closed is insufficient. Local and transaction snapshots must match the queued parcel creation/restore identity. Lock, shop/account/database/key change and lifecycle disposal fence callbacks. Durable local persistence is mandatory before any Sign write; unsupported storage or a failed queue commit retains the form and photo without a foreground write fallback. Mandatory server reservation precedes external writes; safe pre-scan continuation requires its one-shot intent. Unknown image or 05 outcomes never permit blind replay. Physical automatic cleanup requires authoritative signed confirmation, a trusted server clock, the configured retention deadline, no active lease and revision CAS. Unconfirmed work moves to recovery after its queue deadline; explicit user continuation can start a new wait window without changing the logical request. Fresh successful matching-barcode signed status may hide a task from attention without photo proof; cached, returned, local-only or mismatched status cannot. Keep the original and request/history encrypted until the confirmed lifecycle deletion, accessible for export and counted toward capacity. Manual permanent deletion requires confirmation and never marks unsigned work successful; safe cancellation can retire only a server-proven unsent reservation. Ambiguous or in-flight server journals retain duplicate protection. Hidden retained jobs do not schedule receipt/photo writes; ambiguous work needs existing authoritative continuation proof. Explicit cancel/discard writes the server tombstone; manual probes are read-only and photo verification retries use bounded backoff. Unknown, recompressed, unsupported or reused-barcode photo evidence remains held. | `ZoeW/tests/palm-sign-journal.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/palm-sign-outbox.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/palm-sign-outbox-idb.test.ts` |
| **ZTO Cookie store** | Blobs down/hung ➜ env fallback without overwriting memory (the blob Cookie lives until a real 401, `mustRevalidate`) · background refresh never locked behind the request budget · memory value ➜ answer then refresh in background; empty/after 401 ➜ blocking read · store failure reason survives the 60s env cache · renewals: never write a session-less value · ETag writes · late attempts never overwrite newer renewals · auth rejection in `code/errorCode/statusCode` beats a success envelope · auxiliary Cookies 60s ceiling; a new core session writes at once and stays pending until confirmed · Cookie life: key `cookie-life` (⛔ never in `cookie` metadata: the sync tool overwrites it) keyed by the sync's `syncedAt`, entries `{ s, a, r }` merged by max (≤ `COOKIE_LIFE_MAX` syncs; `ended` = `r > a`, so an accept after a rejection heals it) · ETag writes (`onlyIfMatch`/`onlyIfNew`, ≤ `COOKIE_LIFE_TRIES`) · an accept writes ≤ 1 per `COOKIE_LIFE_ACCEPT_GAP_MS` per container (at once for a new sync or after a written rejection) · a rejection writes only at a final `ZTO_AUTH_EXPIRED` whose session is still the current Cookie · writes run inside `cookieRenewTimeoutMs()` beside the renewal flush; a failed write waits `COOKIE_LIFE_RETRY_MS` · env Cookies and syncs without `syncedAt` are not measured · `?diag=1` `cookie.life` = numbers only, read beside the Cookie read | `zto-cookie-store-test` · `zto-budget-test` · `zto-cookie-session-test` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-cookie-life.test.ts` |
| **ZTO "signed" status** | Verdicts `true` · `false` · `null` (missing field ➜ `null`) · misconfiguration disables only this feature (reason in `?diag=1`, values hidden) · flat field paths · production: `ZTO_FIELD_SIGNED` = `billStatus` · `ZTO_SIGNED_VALUES` = `5` (ZTO owns them; picked-up parcels reappearing ➜ check Argus and add the new value before suspecting code) | `zto-signed-status-test` |

Sign evidence: `withSigned=1` runs the `ZTO_LIST_SIGNED_SCAN_TYPE` (`05`) query **in the same invocation, in parallel** (range `from` ➜ max(`to`, today), ≤ 31 days) ➜ `signed` barcodes only on positive proof (code equal and desc not contradicting, or no code and desc equal; `off` or equal to the arrival type ➜ `signed: null`) · a failed companion ➜ `signedOk: false`, the list still answers and is never cached · the companion is awaited **before** `flushCookieRenewal()` (it shares `session`; a `Set-Cookie` arriving after the flush is lost) · requests without `withSigned` stay one upstream call · sign evidence rows are also projected as `signedRows` (list-row shape `barcode·phone·cod·dod·at·from`, `at` = sign scan · latest per barcode · mismatch and other codes never) and a `signedRows` barcode absent from the arrival rows whose sign day lies in [`from`, `to`] imports born closed dated by its sign scan (`signedOnly` · same claim/save/close doors · no phone ➜ skipped · past pickup trash retention, or already in a day's `pickedUpBarcodes` while gone from history and trash (its pickup trash was purged; `classifyZtoListRows(…, dailyPickupData)`) ➜ `too-old-purged` · known in ZoeW ➜ existing, closed if open) · each dialog row shows its arrival and sign time · a row with the sign code but a contradicting text is a `mismatch` (`listRowSignedVerdict()`): never evidence, counted in `otherScans` and surfaced (`signedMismatch` · `signedListMismatch` · `?diag=1` `list.signedMismatch` = distinct barcodes seen (≤ `SIGNED_MISMATCH_KEYS_MAX`, repeated sweeps never inflate it) · the texts of mismatch rows only (≤ `SIGNED_MISMATCH_TEXTS_MAX` distinct, ≤ 64 chars, invisible characters kept: `signedMismatchTexts` · `signedListMismatchTexts` per response, the newest in `?diag=1` `texts`) + `signedDescExpected`/`expected` ➜ ⚠️ in the list dialog and 🩺 with code points · never barcodes or texts of other rows) · `signed=1` = the sign list alone over the **companion's range** (`from` ➜ max(`to`, today)) so the dialog's extra sign pages read the same list as page 1 · `exact=1` = the requested range as is, sent only by `fetchZtoSignedPages()` (`signedExact`: the sweep and its per-day reads); for a Firebase project bound to several branches it reads the sign list of **every bound branch** (`listSignedBranchSites()`: codes and sign rows only, the same shop) and answers only when every branch answered (one failing ➜ the error, never a partial `signedOk`) ⛔ never «complete» from the caller's branch alone (another branch's signed parcels would expire and deduct) · the arrival list and `withSigned` stay on the caller's branch · both cached ≤ `LIST_SIGNED_CACHE_TTL_MAX_MS` (the list keeps its own TTL). Client: page 1 then pages 2..N **in parallel**; a full page whose ZTO `pages` is missing or malformed (`null` · `''` · `false` · `0`), or served at ZTO's own smaller page size (`listServedSize()`), is never a last page (the sign readers — Palm `signedListBody()` · Cookie `listResponseBody()` — follow the candidates reader: `numericCount()` · `listCount()`) · `pages` that contradict the page (rows on a page past `pages` · `total` above `pages` × served size) are not trusted ➜ `pagesUnknown` · a page holding more than `LIST_ROW_MAX` rows fails ⛔ never cut to the ceiling and read as the whole page · an empty page or one past the end (`list: null`; past the end = `total` known ➜ (page − 1) × served size ≥ `total`, else page > `pages`: `listPageIsPastEnd()` · `pageIsPastEnd()`) is an empty page (200, `emptyListContainerOf()`) ⛔ never 502 (a bound branch without signs kept every sweep partial) ⛔ never an empty page that contradicts its own count (`total` 7 · `pages` 0 ➜ failure, never a complete read with 0 signs), whether ZTO sends `null` or an empty array (`listEmptyPageContradictsCount()` · Palm `signedListBody()`): the Function answers `pagesUnknown` with `pages` = that page + 1 (short ➜ that page, unless `total` says rows remain past it ➜ read on · empty ➜ the one before) and the client reads on one page at a time up to `ZTO_LIST_CLIENT_MAX_PAGES` (arrival · companion · sign list); still full there ➜ `truncated` / `partial` ⛔ never `pages: 1`; `fetchZtoListAllPages()` returns sign codes and only the caller notes them **after** `session.current()` (a late reply of a logged-out session never feeds the next pull); verdict order sign evidence ➜ probe ➜ row `ztoClosed` (`ztoPickupVerdictOf()` via `ztoListSignedVerdict()` · same order as the auto-close door: the sign round runs before `/detail` and a `false` never holds it). Returned parcels (owner request, Cookie and Palm alike): `returned=1` = the `ZTO_LIST_RETURN_SCAN_TYPE` (`-710` «ត្រឡប់ការស្កេន», `off` disables) list alone over the companion's range, code-only evidence, latest scan per barcode (`returnedScans` · `sentScans` · `problemScans` join the count: `rows + otherScans + signedScans + returnedScans + sentScans + problemScans` = upstream rows) · the client reads it in parallel with the arrival pages (`fetchZtoReturnedPages()`) and its failure never fails the pull (`returnedState` failed/partial ➜ ⚠️ in the note) · Palm: `tracks=` answers `returnedAt` = own-site action `-710` at or after the latest own-site arrival · a row whose return is at or after its arrival and that ZTO has not closed goes to «↩️ មានក្នុងបញ្ជី តែស្កេនត្រឡប់រួច» (`classifyZtoListRows()` `returned`) ⛔ never imported · one open in ZoeW leaves through row «ZTO returned ➜ trash «ត្រឡប់»» (🗑️ note; switch off ➜ 📌) · a `-710` of another site or before a newer arrival is no return. Preview imports nothing; import (`importZtoListRows`) goes through `claimBarcodeInRegistry()` ➜ `addOrUpdateEntry()` (real `'claimed'` · `releaseBarcodesInRegistry()` · `ZTO_LIST_IMPORT_CONCURRENCY` lanes keyed by customer + scan day (a lane runs its rows in list order so `addOrUpdateEntry()` merges them into one item; lanes run in parallel) · `ZTO_LIST_IMPORT_MAX` · stale view ➜ refuse · `releaseLateBarcodeClaim()` · `armLateWrite()`; a born-closed row whose save lands late or answers `false` (saved, money unconfirmed) still writes pickup stats via `markZtoListRowPickedUp()`; the first hang (claim or save past its ceiling) or going offline stops the loop (no new row starts; lanes already in flight finish their row ➜ ⏳/⚠️ ≤ `ZTO_LIST_IMPORT_CONCURRENCY`) — the rest (rows never started) is reported as «មិនទាន់បញ្ចូល N» and the list stays for a retry; a claim *rejection* is not a hang). Date = ZTO scan day (`ztoScanStampMillis()`). A verdict `true` imports **any age** born closed (`closedAtZto` ➜ `applyBarcodeCloseState()` ➜ pickup stats via `markZtoListRowPickedUp()`, the manual-close door); `false`/`null` young ➜ open. ### Interface, scanning, imports and notifications

| Area | Short rule | Tool |
|---|---|---|
| **Colors COD vs DOD** | COD and DOD in one row look different: COD keeps `money-collected`/`money-pending`; DOD adds `kind-dod` (`--money-dod-collected` / `--money-dod-pending`). Measure **computed color** in a real browser; draw sites derived from `app.js`; reverse: pure COD lines never carry `kind-dod` | `semantic-ui-color-test` · `page-nav-test` |
| **Drawer categories** | `.drawer-group` collapsed by default · state `zoew_drawer_groups_v1` · independent. A category whose items are all hidden hides its header (`refreshDrawerGroups()` single choke point). Measure with `getBoundingClientRect()` (computed `display` resolves under a hidden parent) | `page-nav-test` · `zto-sync-banner-test` |
| **Lookup & scanning** | Table prefetch retries fast but never during a scan · keyboard doesn't jump while a lookup works (ceiling 15s, fail-open) · `lookupCode` survives retries; the Cookie message points to the Windows sync tool ➜ Netlify Blobs · busy is a *wait*, not an *end* · early exits release their queue entry; the ceiling measures real waits only · HTTP 200 with an unreadable body = transient failure (`LOOKUP_BAD_BODY`: one retry · transient cooldown), never «not found» | `lookup-prefetch-test` · `lookup-failure-identity-test` · `lookup-burst-test` |
| **Modals** | `closeModal()` clears only that modal's state (or when the stack is empty) · the last opened is on top (ZoeKeyGen `openModalHelper()` reorders z-index · ZoeW `uiState.modalStack` ➜ `Modal.tsx`) ⛔ never order by fixed z-index or DOM order · a backdrop tap closes every modal except `noDismiss` ones and `BACKDROP_KEEP_MODALS` (Config · lookup API · Excel import — owner decision: long forms · «🗑️ ផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់មុនដកកញ្ចប់» · «📋 បញ្ចូលច្រើន» · «📥 បញ្ជីកញ្ចប់ពី ZTO» — owner decision: a stray tap never drops a check, a scan run or a pulled list; «✍️ Sign · ZTO Palm» follows it: closing drops its photos, name and stage), which still close by their buttons · Back · Escape · keyboard focus stays in the topmost layer (`listenModalFocusTrap()` · `focusTrapLayer()`: the app lock screen while locked, else the topmost modal): a `focusin` outside it (Tab · Shift+Tab · a Bluetooth keyboard or scanner sending Tab) moves to its first focusable element (Shift+Tab ➜ the last) with `preventScroll`, toasts excepted ⛔ never let Tab reach the page behind a modal (☰ · 🔔 · filters answered Enter behind the PIN and Activation modals while the backdrop blocked every tap) · closing the last modal refocuses the hardware scanner on desktop even when the caret sat in that modal's field (`safeFocusScanner()` ignores a focused text field inside `.modal` once no modal is open — `activeElementIsInModal()`; a field outside modals keeps its focus) · the scan phone modal: Enter moves phone ➜ Locker ➜ COD ➜ DOD (`enterKeyHint` next · done) and Enter on DOD only blurs ⛔ Enter never saves (a scanner's trailing Enter must not turn a stray scan into a phone or money) · ⛔ the scan dialog never saves COD or DOD above `SCAN_MONEY_MAX` (`confirmPhone()`: lookup auto-save · «រំលង» · «យល់ព្រម» alike) ➜ toast naming the field and amount, the dialog stays open with the field focused, nothing claimed or written; above 1 ➜ a pinch zoom (desktop trackpad · touchscreen · iPad) is never a keyboard ⛔ never `innerHeight − height` alone (a zoomed desktop sent a hardware scan into COD); one helper for every keyboard (`modalKeyboardViewport()`) ➜ `body.modal-keyboard` · `--modal-kb-pan` · `--modal-kb-line` ➜ the backdrop stays the full layout viewport and its `::after` becomes an opaque layer (the backdrop color over `--body-bg`) from the keyboard line down past the pan + `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` ➜ the translucent iOS keyboard shows one color (measured on screenshot pixels) and no point of the screen reaches the page under a modal ⛔ never shrink or move `.modal` itself · only the topmost modal whose card ends behind the keyboard (or any card while the visual viewport is panned) is fitted (`modalKeyboardFitFor()` ➜ `uiState.modalKeyboardFit` ➜ `Modal.tsx` `.modal-kb` · `--modal-kb-shift` · `--modal-kb-max`, `react-root.css`): left · right never change (measured transform-free before the fit) · a card that fits between the visible top and the keyboard rises only as far as needed so its bottom ends `MODAL_KEYBOARD_GAP_PX` above the keyboard ⛔ never cut a card that fits (owner screenshots: PIN buttons and the «📋 បញ្ចូលច្រើន» title cut while the space above stood empty) · a taller card starts at the visible top (`offsetTop` + max(padding, `--modal-safe-top` + gap): a registered `<length>` holding `env(safe-area-inset-top)` on `.modal`) and ends above the keyboard, scrolling inside ⛔ never above the visible top or under the status bar (owner: a long modal like «API ស្វែងរកអតិថិជន» went off screen) · a card that already ends above the keyboard is untouched; while the visual viewport is panned it keeps its untouched screen position (layout top = untouched top + `offsetTop`) · the focused field is scrolled into view inside its modal scrollers (`revealFocusedModalField()` through `setElementScrollTop()`, margin ≤ `MODAL_FIELD_MARGIN_PX`, a field taller than its scroller keeps its top) on visualViewport `resize`/`scroll` · `focusin` · a modal opening, closing or stacking (deferred one microtask + `commitNow()` so the new modal is rendered) · APK: window `resize` reveals the focused modal field ⛔ never `scrollIntoView()` (it scrolls the document on iOS) ⛔ never a CSS transition on it · Android (`isAndroidDevice()`: APK — `KeyboardOpenHold` keeps the WebView height while the IME animates but the visual viewport shrinks at its start — and Chrome PWA, which also shrinks after the keyboard): the fit is the layout the viewport gets once it shrinks (`modalKeyboardHoldFitFor()`: centered in the visible area · `.modal` padding · max-height scaled by new ÷ old height · `hold` ➜ `.modal-kb-hold` keeps `.si-modal-content` flex) ➜ the card moves once, when the keyboard starts ⛔ never the bottom-anchored iOS fit there (it moves again when the WebView shrinks) · while the keyboard stays open an Android card never moves down (`androidFitStaysUp()`: a lower fit is ignored while the card still fits; through the WebView or page shrink `androidHeldFitView()` — APK `keyboardOpen` or `keyboardLayoutShrank()`), a higher one still lifts it (owner video: the IME first announced the previous QWERTY height, the numeric keyboard settled lower and the card dropped) · Android learns each keyboard's real height by itself (any keyboard app · resized keyboards): `keyboardSession` = the full layout height when the visual viewport first shows a keyboard ➜ height = full − visible, remembered only once it holds (`KEYBOARD_SETTLE_MS` after the layout shrank · `KEYBOARD_TRUST_MS` in the visual viewport outside the APK) ⛔ never the APK hold value ⛔ never a value written on every resize (it kept the stale announcement) · until the keyboard settles | **Push (ZoeW)** | Identity = Activation Key (real ECDSA + License Project Revoke/expiry) · "can't verify" = 503 ≠ 403 · `netlify/lib/push-core.mjs` constants equal `license-verify.js`. Supabase shops: session token ➜ `my_account()` (publishable key only) ➜ key per shop (`supabaseTenantKeyId()`) · `shop-inactive` · `no-account` · 503 · `server-off`; `pushIdentityMissing()` checks synchronously before `requestPermission()`. Web Push aes128gcm (RFC 8291) + VAPID ES256 · real push hosts only (SSRF) · 404/410 ➜ retire the captured subscription revision. APK: FCM via the License Project only · no `google-services.json` (`__FCM_CONFIGURED__`) ➜ don't load the plugin · channel `zoew_notify`. A subscribe whose index update gives up (`index:busy`) rolls its record back (new ➜ conditionally retired · existing ➜ conditionally restored) unless the index already holds it ⛔ never an active `sub/` record outside every index (it escapes `PUSH_SUBS_PER_KEY_MAX`, fills `PUSH_SUBS_TOTAL_MAX` for every shop and eats the notice budget) · Current subscription `keyId` must match the expiry shop even if the bykey index is stale; eviction, rollback and unsubscribe must also preserve a record that has moved to another owner/revision. A stale index is not deletion authority; derive evictions only from the successful CAS attempt, never accumulate them across rejected updater attempts. HTTP deadlines abort headers/body before releasing a worker. Notice claim size is at most send concurrency; reserve read/OAuth/send time before claim and attempt every reserved recipient. At-most-once (ETag ledger `onlyIfMatch`/`onlyIfNew`) · first run = baseline · notices > 24h not sent. Scheduled functions share one budget, `PUSH_RUN_BUDGET_MS` inside Netlify's 30s scheduled-function limit (`runPushCron()`: the 8am reminder before seller notices ➜ a hung notice read or a notice carried in slices never takes the reminder's only hour; a new notice also goes out at once through `op=kick`); out of time ➜ don't lock the reminder · a claim (one shop's reminder · one notice slice) starts only while `PUSH_CLAIM_RESERVE_MS` of the budget is left · the 8am reminder walks shops `PUSH_EXPIRY_CONCURRENCY` at a time and reads the day ledger before the schedule ⛔ never one shop at a time in list order (the tail of a long shop list was never reminded) · a seller notice goes out in `PUSH_NOTICE_CHUNK` slices of the sorted subscriptions, each claimed in `state/notices` `carry` (id + last key) before it is sent ➜ a run out of budget continues at the next cron, at most once per device ⛔ never the whole list in one run (the ledger was locked and the rest never got it). Near-expiry: app sends times only, from `barcodeAbandonIsRipe()`, fresh view only · 8am Asia/Phnom_Penh reminder · schedules > 48h dropped. `Notification.requestPermission()` before any `await` · `busy` is a lock ➜ every wait behind it has a ceiling (`withTimeout(…, PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS)` · `pushStep` · `PUSH_NATIVE_REGISTER_TIMEOUT_MS`) · ⛔ never resolve a promise to a Capacitor plugin (Proxy `then` hangs) ➜ `loadNativePush()` returns `{ PN }`; mocks are Proxies · APK off ➜ send `unsubscribe` (token in `zoew_push_v1`) before `unregister()` · accept tokens only when the user wants it on · status tells the truth · `resyncPush()` re-registers every `PUSH_RESYNC_MS` and, after a refused reply (`failedAt`), after `PUSH_RESYNC_RETRY_MS` ⛔ never on every `visibilitychange` · a registration belongs to the shop it was made in (`zoew_push_v1` `shop` = `shopScope()`): another shop (`pushShopMoved()`) ➜ `resyncPush()` re-registers at once (also from `resumePushAfterSignIn()`) and re-sends the expiry schedule (`syncExpirySchedule(true)`) ⛔ never wait for `PUSH_RESYNC_MS` (the old shop keeps pushing to this device · Firebase keys the schedule by the Activation Key, the same in every shop) · web: the first resync of a page compares the subscription key with `?op=config` ➜ a different key re-subscribes (refused ➜ «off», never a silent «on») · tap ➜ 🔔 panel · 🧹 hides only announcements/notices (`zoew_notify_dismissed_v1`) · Subscription retirement uses the captured owner and revision with a conditional write; Blob delete has no CAS. Eviction victims come only from the successful index CAS attempt. Registration and index revisions prevent same-content ABA; rollback, unsubscribe and gone-endpoint cleanup cannot retire a newer registration. Inert tombstones retain no credentials and do not count toward active capacity; capacity reads are bounded and uncertainty returns retryable failure. | `ZoeW/tests/push-server.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/push-client.test.tsx` · `npm run android:check` · `keygen-notice-test` |

| ZoeKeyGen biometrics: WebAuthn PRF only · discoverable passkey (`residentKey: 'required'`) · decrypted PIN verified · PIN change unbinds · hung admin writes ➜ "⏳ not confirmed" + late ✅ (`armAdminLateWrite()`) · Signing Key idle expiry (`SIGNING_KEY_IDLE_MS`) · admin operations (Generate · Extend · Revoke · seat limit · seat release · notices) are bound to the signed-in admin: `adminOperationIsCurrent()` = same `uid` + same `authGeneration` (logout or re-login drops the result) ⛔ never the sensitive-session generation (a PIN modal close is not a logout ➜ the written Key and the ✅ still show) · every admin read and write, retries included, goes to the database captured when the operation started (`operationDb`) (every retry callback stops with `noRetry` once the operation is no longer current, including seat changes and notices) ⛔ never the live `db` after an `await` or inside a retry (a Reconfig in between writes the Key, or another project's metadata, into another License Project); secret operations (Signing Key load/restore · keypair · biometric enrollment · clipboard) keep `isSensitiveSessionCurrent()` · the Generate lock has an owner (`generateKeyOwner`): a session invalidated mid-generate (Config · logout) drops the result but releases the lock ⛔ never release by session check | `keygen-pin-flow-test.js` · `keygen-session-security-test.js` · `keygen-biometric-test.js` · `keygen-notice-test.js` |
