Allowlists (`ACCEPTED` / `EXPECTED_DIVERGENT` / `IGNORE`) need a written reason per entry.

## 🔒 ZTO status · auto-close · sign-list sweep (locked zone)

> The rules behind the core-table row «ZTO not-closed bar · auto-close · sign-list sweep». Guards: `zto-sync-banner-test` ·
> `ZoeW/tests/zto-signed-sync.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-signed-cadence.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-remind-days.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-sign-evidence-refresh.test.ts` ·
> `ZoeW/tests/zto-sweep-lane.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-final-marks.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-signed-mismatch-age.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/zto-recheck-bar-first.test.tsx`. ⛔ The files live in the locked zone (`LOCK`).

### Verdict store and the not-closed bar

- Verdicts only in `localStorage` (`zoew_zto_pickup_status_v1`) · never touch money/`isDeducted`/Firebase · writes only via
  `applyBarcodeCloseChange()` · bar only on a real `false` · a closed barcode whose server record already carries `ztoSignedAt` (signed at another branch) never enters it and `holdFalse` never keeps its `false` (`ZoeW/tests/zto-sign-evidence-refresh.test.ts`) · reopened ➜ leaves · scans `scanHistory` and pickup trash ≤ `ZTO_STATUS_TTL_MS` (3 days after it entered the trash · owner request: a parcel closed in ZoeW but not yet signed in ZTO Palm stays in the bar 3 days; verdicts in the store live the same 3 days and `ZTO_STATUS_MAX` holds 3 days of verdicts ➜ a reopened App never re-asks them) · non-ZTO
  Lookup ➜ dormant · logout clears.
- Call limits: 1/barcode · 10/round · 20s apart · not while scanning or offline · `null` remembered · failures not remembered but backed
  off (`ZTO_STATUS_FAIL_BACKOFF_MS`) · `resumeZtoStatusSweep()` only touches set timers · a forced round («🔄 ពិនិត្យម្តងទៀត») asks the bar's
  entries first, least recently asked first (`ztoStatusLastAskedAt()`: the later of the verdict `at` and the last ask, failed asks
  included ➜ entries ZTO keeps refusing never hold every press), at most `ZTO_STATUS_SWEEP_BATCH` − 1 of them while the rest of the
  queue holds entries, and its toast counts the bar entries not checked since the first press of the walk («… មិនទាន់ពិនិត្យ — សូមចុចម្តងទៀត»;
  the walk ends once every entry is checked or after `ZTO_OPEN_RECHECK_MS`) · the rotation cursor counts the queue entries a round
  passed (`queuePassed`: asked, or decided by the sign read) ⛔ never the bar entries · a round broken before any queue entry moves it by
  nothing ⛔ never a forced round without a queue slot (both starved the queue: a Palm barcode never got its exact Sign time and its
  2-hour cleanup never ran).
- Sign evidence keeps flowing to the pickup cleanup: a barcode closed in ZoeW whose verdict is `false` is asked again once its verdict is
  older than `ZTO_OPEN_RECHECK_MS`, and an answer that is not a boolean keeps that `false` with a fresh `at` (`holdFalse`) ⛔ never `null`
  (it would leave the bar and every Sign path) · the Palm Sign-time probe asks only closed barcodes and an unclear answer never erases a
  `true` · a positive `signedAt` survives load and save while the server clock is still unknown ⛔ never re-minted from the device clock.
- Eviction (`ZTO_STATUS_MAX`) by value, newest entry never victim, oldest fallback, `while` has `break`; an evicted verdict is never
  re-asked by the sweep (`ztoPickupVerdictSeenAt` · in memory · pruned to the current candidates · open barcodes keep the hourly recheck)
  ➜ more barcodes than `ZTO_STATUS_MAX` never loop `/detail`, and «កំពុងពិនិត្យបន្ត N» counts only barcodes the sweep will still ask
  (`ztoStatusUnmeasuredCount()`).
- Optional `/detail` rounds pass `ztoStatusNetworkAllowed(userAsked)` (`linkIsFrugal()` · `isModalOpen`); the sign-list read passes
  `ztoSignedNetworkAllowed()` (offline · `linkIsFrugal()` unless an abandon hold is active (`ztoAbandonHoldIsActive()`) · lookup in flight) ⛔ never `isModalOpen` (scanning without pause never closed
  from ZTO) · a round whose `/detail` is blocked never stamps `ztoStatusLastSweepAt` · long blocks set no wake timer
  (`ztoStatusBlockIsTransient()`) · "កំពុងពិនិត្យបន្ត N" in `ztoStatusModalSig` · Locker = `barcode.locker || item.locker` · list holds only
  real `false` · barcode = `Code128Svg` from `code128Bars()` (ZXing read-back, `CODE128_QUIET`, ≤ 1.5px/module) ·
  `clearSensitiveModalFields()` clears it · 3 triggers (60s cycle · `visibilitychange` · PIN unlock) ⛔ not from `runScheduledCleanup()`.

### Auto-close

- `zoew_zto_autoclose_v1` (default on) · switches only under `ztoFastModeIsOn()`, hiding disables (`ztoAutoCloseEnabled()`/
  `ztoListSyncEnabled()`) · asks open barcodes only (skip `clearClaim`/restore markers) · hourly recheck (`ZTO_OPEN_RECHECK_MS`) · closes
  only on real `true` via `applyBarcodeCloseChange()` (`silent: true` · `showModal: false`) · one summary per round · queue rotates
  (`rotateZtoSweepQueue(queue, ztoStatusSweepCursor)`).

### Sign-list sweep (ZTO Palm)

- The sweep first reads the sign list (`closeZtoSignedBarcodes()` ➜ `signed=1`) when due (`ztoSignedSweepCadenceMs()` — Netlify quota:
  active (`noteZtoUserActivity()` from document `pointerdown`/`keydown`, ≤ `ZTO_USER_ACTIVE_WINDOW_MS`) `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_ACTIVE_MS` ·
  visible `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_VISIBLE_MS` · hidden `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_GAP_MS`; its own timer only when visible + switch on + open barcodes +
  normal wait (`ztoSignedLivePollWanted()`); idle ➜ active moves a pending timer to the active cadence at once (`noteZtoUserActivity()`,
  only while the live poll is wanted — a `/detail` continuation timer stays); the sign read never waits behind the `/detail` backoff (a
  transient block reschedules at the sign cadence · `scheduleZtoStatusSweep()` runs when the sign read is due); failure doubles the wait
  up to `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_FAIL_MAX_MS` and the cadence never overrides it; not configured / no `signed` ➜ `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_IDLE_MS`.
- Window 7 days, then 1 day after a complete round; a list past the page ceiling (a full page without ZTO `pages` counts as more pages) is re-read **per day** (`ztoListDayKeys()` ·
  ≤ `ZTO_SIGNED_DAY_CONCURRENCY` at a time · the next reads within `ZTO_SIGNED_SPLIT_MEMO_MS` of a range at least as wide
  (`ztoSignedSplitFrom`) go per day at once; on that path a failed day stops the wave and, when no day was measured, its error reaches the
  caller (backoff · `ztoSignedOff`) · the per-day workers stop when the session changes) and a day still past it ➜ `truncated` (never
  complete · backoff · one toast per session).
- A read with `signedMismatch` is never complete either (ambiguous evidence ➜ the 7-day cleanup keeps waiting) but still narrows the window
  to 1 day (`ztoSignedSweepOkAt` = range complete); its range start is remembered (`ztoSignedMismatchFrom`) ➜ no read is complete until a
  mismatch-free read covers it, and the wide range is re-read at most once per `ZTO_SIGNED_FRESH_MS`; the remembered start is clamped to `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_MAX_DAYS` (no open parcel is older ➜ the read stays inside ZTO's 31-day range and a mismatch-free read can still clear it).
- It closes open barcodes found there through `autoCloseBarcodeFromZto()` and flips closed barcodes found there without a server Sign
  stamp to `true` without `/detail` — a sign row older than the barcode's restore (`barcodeSignBasis()`, the basis of the stamp writer
  `applyZtoFinalMarks()`) is no evidence for it: no close, no flip, `/detail` (the current state) decides ⛔ never close or flip on a sign the
  stamp writer refuses (a `true` without a stamp hides the barcode from the bar and locks reopen for good) — saved once per read (`deferSave`: a save per flip serialises the whole store each time): when the
  read carries the sign time (Cookie `signedRows` `at` ➜ an exact stamp) every one without a local `true` verdict (`signedStale` ⛔ never
  only this device's `false` verdicts — a device yielding its turn learns the sign only from the reader's stamp); without it (Palm) only
  this device's `false` verdicts ⛔ never an observation stamp on a barcode this device never measured (the Palm 2-hour cleanup would
  count from the observation, not the sign) (a round without open barcodes reads only yesterday ➜ today and is
  never complete for E1; the flip happens even when the round is cut or the read is partial/truncated; the live poll also runs for pending
  `false` verdicts), ≤ `ZTO_STATUS_SWEEP_BATCH` per round (rest ➜ next round; a pending close stops the round); every definite attempt is
  skipped by the same round's `/detail` loop: a close records verdict `true`; a close that fails while the barcode stays open is retried
  after `ZTO_SIGNED_CLOSE_RETRY_MS` (never every round · never a 1-hour verdict `true` · no starvation of the tail) and keeps the round incomplete for the 7-day wait · a
  recent `true` verdict (closed from ZTO, then reopened by hand) holds the sign close until `ztoOpenRecheckIsDue()` unless forced
  (`ztoSignedCloseIsHeld()`) ⛔ never once its abandon is ripe (`ztoSignedEntryAbandonRipe()`: ZTO evidence closes it as pickup before the
  8-day rule acts); a recent `false` does not hold.
- Branch turns (owner request, option ឃ): devices of one lane take turns for the sign read — lane = `palm_<site code>` · `cookie_<domain of
  the signed-in account's email>` (`ztoSweepLaneOf()`; none ➜ the device reads alone) · Firebase shops only (owner decision): on the Supabase
  adapter (`tenantScope`) `attachZtoSweepLaneListener()` attaches nothing and every device reads alone ⛔ never claim there (each claim is a
  tenant write that broadcasts to every device of the shop and makes each one pull: N devices ➜ ~N² realtime messages and pulls per `ZTO_SIGNED_SWEEP_ACTIVE_MS`) · a non-forced sign read first claims
  `zoew_settings/zto_sweep_lead/<lane>` = `{ device, at }` (`claimZtoSweepLane()`: an advisory CAS transaction · `device` =
  `ztoTrashModeDeviceKey()` · `at` on the server clock) and skips its read while another device claimed within the spacing
  (`ztoSweepLaneTurn()`: own cadence ÷ (1 + other devices seen claiming within `ZTO_SWEEP_LANE_PEER_SPAN` × own cadence), never below
  `ZTO_SWEEP_LANE_FLOOR_MS`), then wakes at its turn (`laneWaitMs`) · each device's own cadence stays its ceiling (`ztoSignedSweepIsDue()`)
  ➜ Function calls never exceed every device reading alone, and N equally active devices see a sign N× sooner ⛔ never a sticky leader
  (it reads at one device's cadence) · a claim stamped more than `ZTO_SWEEP_LANE_FUTURE_MS` ahead holds nothing · a forced read (🔄) never
  waits · a device whose 7-day hold is active (`ztoAbandonHoldIsActive()`) never waits either: its hold is released only by its own
  complete read covering the ripe moment ⛔ never let a yielding device's hold run out unread (a slower device in a lane with a faster one
  gets no turn; once its episode runs out every later ripe parcel is released at once and one signed just before ripening is deducted) ·
  a sign read that closed or flipped schedules the Sign evidence write at once (`scheduleZtoFinalMarks()`) and every history snapshot
  schedules the next `ZTO_FINAL_SWEEP_BATCH` (`renderHistory()`, an empty filtered view included · `ZoeW/tests/zto-empty-view-refresh.test.ts`) ➜ every other device's not-closed bar and reopen lock follow the reader's
  read, not its 60s cycle · fail-open ➜ the device reads alone: lane listener pending/failed/denied
  (rules not published) · untrusted clock or no live connection · no shop · a claim refused or past `ZTO_SWEEP_LANE_CLAIM_TIMEOUT_MS`
  (claims pause `ZTO_SWEEP_LANE_RETRY_MS`) · only the sign read takes turns: `/detail` rounds and the not-closed bar verdicts stay per
  device, the Cookie returned list rides a device's own sign read · rules: lane key 6–47 characters, `device` 8–32, `at` > 0 and
  ≤ now + 600000, `$other` rejected.

---

# 💰 Core business rule — "Delete" vs "Remove"

- **Delete (លុប)** moves a customer row and its barcodes to trash. It preserves COD, DOD, parcel count and each barcode's existing `isDeducted`; it never creates a revenue delta. Delete all follows the same policy.
- **Remove (ដក)** moves the chosen barcode to trash and deducts its COD, DOD and one parcel once if still counted; a closed barcode also leaves «យករួច» (owner decision · rules under «Pickup stats»). A successful restore adds back that deduction (and that pickup key) once; removing the restored barcode is a new deduction. Parent totals reflect only the barcodes left behind.
- `isDeducted` is the **business flag for settled money**, not proof that an interrupted write finished. Between a ledger commit and the flag flip, consult the keyed ledger event and pending journal. Restore finalization marks the event returned together with its revenue change; never increment blindly from a stale flag. The durable protocol and its guards live under **Network · Hang ≠ fail**.
- Diagnose money from fresh barcode/history/trash and ledger evidence. `isFromDeletion`, `trashReason`, labels and trash grouping describe provenance or presentation; none authorizes an extra debit or credit.

## "Revenue" = value of parcels **picked up**

```
ledger = (picked up) + (waiting, < 7 days) + (deleted by hand)
```

Uncollected parcels ➜ `claimAndCleanupItem('abandon')` ➜ `isDeducted: true` ➜ deducted ➜ purged in 2 days
(`RETURNED_TRASH_RETENTION_MS`); they return to the central branch and don't stay in our system.

Uncollected-parcel lifecycle (company policy: over 7 days ➜ back to the central branch · one clock, `ABANDON_AGE_MS`), in order:
1. waits in a Locker · 2. the last `NOTIFY_EXPIRY_HOURS_MAX` hours ➜ 🔔 📦 and the 8 a.m. push tell staff to return-scan ·
3. past the bound ➜ staff take it out and scan «ត្រឡប់» in ZTO · 4. ZoeW removes it to trash «ត្រឡប់» (money and count deducted) on whichever
comes first: the ZTO return (scenario 8b) or the age rule (scenario 8) · 5. 📤 lists it until the purge. `guide.html` shows these
five steps in order; the texts live once in `NOTIFY_RETURN_*` (`notifications.ts`) and the push repeats them ⛔ never a second
day count (`ZoeW/tests/expiry-return-policy.test.tsx`).

- Both stats screens show **"ចំណូល (យករួច)"**, never the raw ledger, plus total parcel value as a separate row.
- One formula: `collectedValueOf(ledgerCod, ledgerDod, uncollected)` with `uncollectedValueByDate()` counting barcodes
  **`!isDeducted && !isClosed`** across `scanHistory` and `deletedItems`. Clamp **per currency**.
- `collected = ledger − open` needs fresh views of both sides: `collectedValueIsMeasurable()` covers
  `DB_LISTENER_KEY_HISTORY` · `DB_LISTENER_KEY_DELETED` · `DB_LISTENER_KEY_DAILY_REVENUE`; otherwise show **`—`**.
- ⚠️ Accepted boundary: Delete doesn't deduct ➜ after purge, revenue is slightly inflated. ⛔ Never make Delete deduct
  (that changes business policy).

## 📋 Scenario table — single source of truth

| # | Action | Function | `trashReason` | `isFromDeletion` | `isDeducted` | **Money** | `closedAt` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Close "picked up" 1 barcode | `toggleIndividualBarcodeClose` | — | — | — | untouched | stamped on that barcode |
| 2 | Close whole parcel | `toggleCloseStatus` | — | — | — | untouched | stamped on every barcode |
| 3 | Reopen (both paths) | same | — | — | — | untouched | stamp removed |
| 4 | **Remove** 1 barcode | `removeSingleBarcode` | `remove` | `false` | **`true`** | **deducted** | kept |
| 5 | **Delete** parcel | `deleteSingleItem` | `delete` | `true` | untouched | untouched | kept |
| 6 | Delete all | `buildClearHistoryTrashItem` | `delete` | `true` | untouched | untouched | kept |
| 7 | Mode-aware pickup cleanup (rules below) | `claimAndCleanupItem('close')` | `pickup` | `true` | untouched | untouched | decider |
| 8 | 7-day cleanup (not picked up) | `claimAndCleanupItem('abandon')` | `returned` | `false` | **`true`** | **deducted** | — |
| 8b | ZTO returned (core-table row «ZTO returned ➜ trash «ត្រឡប់»») | `claimAndCleanupItem('abandon', { codes })` | `returned` | `false` | **`true`** | **deducted** | — |
| 9 | **Restore** | `executeRestoreItem` | removed | removed | **reset** | **added back** if deducted | closed: now; open: removed |
| 10 | Permanent delete | `executePermanentDelete` | — | — | — | untouched | — |

**The table describes completed operations.** Pending deduction/restore follows the evidence rule above. `trashReason` never substitutes for that evidence.

## ⛔ Automatic cleanup constants — never change without a request

| Rule | Measures | Result | Money |
|---|---|---|---|
| ZTO Login: 2 hours (`TWO_HOURS_MS`) | locally closed barcode with real ZTO Sign evidence; `now - ztoSignedAt >= TWO_HOURS_MS` | trash `pickup` | untouched |
| Cookie: 2 hours (`TWO_HOURS_MS`) | locally closed barcode; `now - closedAt >= TWO_HOURS_MS`, whether signed or unsigned | trash `pickup` | untouched |
| Non-ZTO/disabled Lookup: over 2 hours | each barcode's `closedAt`, existing local workflow | trash `pickup` | untouched |
| over 7×24h (`ABANDON_AGE_MS`) | `item.createdAt`; restored-open barcodes use the later of parent and `barcode.restoredAt`, split by `barcode.isClosed` | trash `returned` | deducted |
| 2 days | `returned` trash items | purge | — |
| 30 days (`TRASH_RETENTION_MS`) | other trash items | purge | — |

- The comparison is `now - createdAt > ABANDON_AGE_MS` (**`>`**) ➜ the trash label "ត្រឡប់ (ថ្ងៃទី ៨ …)" (day 8) is correct ⛔ never
  "fix" it to 7. `trash-modal-test.js` derives `ABANDON_LABEL_DAY = ABANDON_DAYS + 1` from `app.js` and asserts `>`;
  stale-day scans cover "៨ ថ្ងៃ" and "៨ថ្ងៃ".
- ZTO auto-close on ➜ the 7-day rule waits for the ZTO signed list (core table row «7-day cleanup ↔ ZTO signed list»).
- `barcode.restoredAt` is optional; never change the parent's or siblings' clocks; one age helper for selection and
  transaction; after partial removal `isClosed` comes from the remaining barcodes.
- Mixed parcels (A closed · B open): the mode-aware pickup rule moves **A only** once eligible (`pickup`, money untouched); 7-day rule moves **B only**
  (`returned`, deduct B).
- ⛔ Every close/open goes through `applyBarcodeCloseState(barcode, closed, at)` (never `b.isClosed = x`). `applyBarcodeCloseChange()` and
  the whole-row `toggleCloseStatus()` keep the server's `closedAt` (barcode and parcel) when the server barcode already holds the desired
  state — a stale view never re-stamps and a collected record never moves day. A whole-row reopen marks every barcode open; a whole-row
  close marks the barcodes that change on the server plus every barcode this device's view marked (`localMarkedKeys`) ⛔ never leave a
  local mark out of the server marks (`reapplyPickupMarks()` would undo it to the stale previous state) · a barcode both views already
  hold closed is left alone (a Reset never comes back); `applyBarcodeCloseChange()` follows the same rule for one barcode (both views
  closed with a stamp ➜ no pickup mark · no new stamp in the view), while its collected-mirror reconcile still runs (a Reset never
  touches the mirror) (`ZoeW/tests/close-restamp-idempotent.test.ts` · `pickup-barcode-identity-test` ៣ឃ · `daily-collected-test` ១៦).
  `barcodeCloseIsRipe(barcode, now, mode)` is the single decider; a closed barcode without `closedAt` is never ripe (no guessed stamp). `executeRestoreItem()`
  resets `closedAt` only on closed barcodes; open barcodes have no close stamp (their age basis is defined above).

## Trash and `trashReason`

Path, reason, flags and money behavior are defined only in the scenario table above. Trash labels are លុប / យករួច / ត្រឡប់ / ដក.

- **Owner policy:** every ZTO Login trash item is non-restorable, including delete/remove/returned/pickup. Cookie trash with any actual signed barcode is non-restorable regardless of reason; unsigned Cookie delete/remove/pickup retains the deduction-aware restore flow. A mixed trash item containing a signed barcode is blocked as a whole.
- `stampTrashPolicy()` persists `trashMode` before the destructive claim/journal and `restoreBlocked: true` for ZTO Login or signed evidence. These travel with recovery; changing device mode cannot weaken them. Both backend validators fence blocked claims/history preparation/finalization and keep the flag monotonic. Unsigned rows without mode provenance cannot be retrospectively identified as ZTO Login; current ZTO Login UI/claim still blocks them.
- `ztoSignedAt` is a positive finite epoch. `ztoSignedExact` identifies a validated upstream Sign timestamp; otherwise the first confirmed observation uses server time conservatively. A bounded existing status sweep enriches missing Palm times. Exact evidence may correct an observation stamp once, never across a later restore generation; dispatch/arrival time is never a Sign clock.
- Cookie Restore checks fresh upstream status for every barcode before claiming, using `verifyZtoRestoreStatus()` and the Function's uncached `restore=1` path. Unknown/offline/old endpoints fail closed; validate the returned barcode, status and freshness marker under the original session. Terminal evidence observed during claim/prepare invokes cancellation below. A fresh `ZTO_NOT_FOUND` (not a ZTO parcel) allows the restore · a record holding the barcode without customer data answers `ZTO_NO_CUSTOMER_DATA` with its sign verdict (`true` ➜ final, `false` ➜ allowed, anything else ➜ unconfirmed) — Cookie status reads send `status=1` too and bypass the scan's «not found» cache, so such a barcode leaves the not-closed bar once ZTO signs it; the scan lookup itself keeps `ZTO_NOT_FOUND` · that answer (`restore=1` · `status=1`) passes the `ZTO_DETAIL_BRANCH_PATHS` check like `/detail` (another branch · no branch field ➜ `ZTO_OTHER_BRANCH`, no verdict) · a Cookie shop seen from a device without a Cookie Lookup ➜ `RESTORE_ZTO_NEEDS_LOOKUP` · shop marker unknown ➜ `RESTORE_SHOP_MODE_UNSYNCED`. ZTO and the customer database do not share an atomic transaction; do not claim a guarantee against an external Sign that has not yet been observed. Guard: `tests/zto-restore-verification.test.ts`.
- Signed trash without the flag is gated immediately in the app and backfilled with the permanent block. Both backends require `restoreBlocked: true` beside every barcode `ztoSignedAt` in trash, with or without `trashMode` ⛔ never an exception for items without `trashMode` (`emu/supabase-rules-parity-test` · `emu/restore-deadlock-test`). The existing authenticated-client trust boundary still applies; these stamps are not cryptographic upstream attestations.

- `trashReasonOf()` fallback: `isFromDeletion === true` ➜ `isClosed ? 'pickup' : 'delete'`; else `'remove'`.
- ⛔ `executeRestoreItem()` deletes `itemToRestore.trashReason`, `trashMode` and `restoreBlocked` before writing to `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod`
  (`$other: { ".validate": false }` would reject the restore).
- Table merging is display-only (key `trashGroupKeyOf()`: reason · phone · scanDate · time, each length-prefixed ⛔ never a separator join — a `~` in a field could merge two customers); 🔄/✖️ act on one `id` ⛔ no bulk
  restore/purge without a request.
- Totals split by `TRASH_REASON_META[reason].deducted` (`remove`+`returned` vs `pickup`+`delete`); search filters table and
  totals; a tapped card filters the table only (core-table row «Trash · `trashReason`»).

## ⛔ Markers never to confuse

| Marker | Belongs to | Strip before writing to |
|---|---|---|
| `restoreClaimId` · `restoreClaimToken` · `restoreAddedCodes` | `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod` | trash (`stripHistoryOnlyMarkers`) |
| `clearClaim` | `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod` | trash |
| `trashClaim` · `trashClaimAt` (on each barcode) | `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod` | trash (`stripHistoryOnlyMarkers`) |
| `restoreClaim` | `zoew_recently_deleted_cod_dod` | history (`executeRestoreItem`) |
| `trashReason` · `trashMode` · `restoreBlocked` · `deletedAt` · `isFromDeletion` | `zoew_recently_deleted_cod_dod` | history (`executeRestoreItem`) |

- Cleanup, delete, remove and price edits skip items being restored (`itemHasRestoreMarkers()` in the transaction).
- A history row carrying restore markers refuses close and reopen (`RESTORE_IN_PROGRESS_CLOSE_TEXT`) ➜ no pickup or collected mark lands on
  codes a cancellation may still remove · a row prepared by another source is never a merge target (`isRestoreMergeTarget()`) · a source
  whose claim lease expired and whose own evidence blocks it (Sign or returned evidence: `trashRestoreBlockOf(item, true)`) is cancelled
  automatically (`restoreAwaitsCancellation()` from `resumeInterruptedCleanups()` · `cancelBlockedRestore(…, { evidenceOnly: true })`; needs
  `cleanupClockIsTrustworthy()` and a fresh trash view (`dbListenerViewIsStale(DB_LISTENER_KEY_DELETED)`), never this device's live claim) ⛔ never on the device or shop mode alone (a Palm view would freeze an
  unsigned Cookie item for good) · a `claimedAt` up to `RESTORE_CLAIM_FUTURE_MS` ahead of now is a live lease (another device's clock), beyond it
  invalid · the background freeze re-checks that lease on the server value inside its transaction ⛔ never on the claimant's own
  `rejectBlockedRestore()` (it freezes on terminal evidence) (`ZoeW/tests/zto-trash-mode-shop.test.ts`) ·
  the purge skips a source whose claim it cannot release (`releaseStaleRestoreClaimForPurge()` ➜ `false`), never an error loop.
- Lease expiry permits taking over a restore, not dropping its prepared history. Keep markers while the source has any claim; a takeover preserves its target inside the same CAS. Purge may release an expired claim only when its target has no matching prepared history, enforced again by both backends; on trash holding Sign evidence without the block flag the release stamps `restoreBlocked` in the same write (owner policy; the rules require the flag beside every `ztoSignedAt`). Normal finalization needs a matching witness and deletes the source atomically with the revenue increment; a target the prepare left without barcodes is deleted by that same update (the witness rule accepts an absent target only when it held no barcodes before the write).
- `restoreAddedCodes` is immutable JSON text listing only codes inserted by this prepare. When terminal Sign is observed, `cancelBlockedRestore()` freezes the source with `restoreBlocked` (a source already frozen is not written again), removes only those inserted codes, preserves pre-existing codes/call state, clears the history markers, then releases the source claim. Cancellation never credits revenue. Provenance matters only to a cancellation: a blocked source whose prepared row lacks valid provenance is held, no code is removed (never guess), the restore answers `RESTORE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED_TEXT` and Sentry money reports it once per row (`resumeBlockedRestoreCancellations()` · `executeRestoreItem()`); a cancellation whose inserted codes were the last ones of the row deletes the row · a restorable source finishes its prepare retry with or without provenance: codes that left the prepared row (`lost`) are never inserted again, the finalize still credits the source's own deduction once (the device that moved the copy settled it as an ordinary barcode), pickup and collected marks cover only codes the row still holds, the toast names the lost codes (`restoreLostCodesText()`) and Sentry money reports them · rules: provenance rows cancel with the source blocked and its claim targeting the row ⛔ no write drops the markers or codes of a row without provenance (neither alone nor beside a release of the source claim) · a target that no longer names the source is left untouched (abort, never a rewrite) · a claim on a restorable item marks the row ⏳🔄 (finish with 🔄). A history token left behind by a takeover whose re-token never landed is still cancellable (same `restoreClaimId` + provenance; both backends' rules accept it). Recovery is fenced by database/auth generation/shop. Guards: `tests/restore-terminal-cancel.test.ts`, `emu/restore-mutation-emu-test.js`, backend rules parity and the money matrix.
- Group purge is atomic ➜ a failed group falls back to one-by-one.

## ⛔ Pickup stats — barcode set as the single basis

```
zoew_daily_pickup_cod_dod/<day>/pickedUpBarcodes/<barcodeKey> = <phoneKey>
packagesPickedUp = number of keys        ← derived mirror
pickedUpPhones   = count per value        ← derived mirror
```

- Customers = distinct phones with ≥ 1 closed barcode; parcels = closed barcodes.
- Persist a desired-close plan before history/pickup effects: direction, exact target membership, positive birth, nonnegative restored marker,
  prior receipt identity including its absence, Sign identity and original server timestamp. Original execution and replay share the same
  claim/restore/session guards and basis comparison; whole-row plans also reject changed membership. An exact prior basis
  permits the desired transition; a complete receipt owned by that intent proves idempotent completion. Never toggle again,
  restamp on replay, or replay a partial/conflicting receipt. Unknown legacy intent supplies no desired action.
- Store the transition timestamp as `serverTransitionAt`; accept an old plan's `at` only through strict v1 shape validation
  with exactly one timestamp field. Preserve that saved server time across offset changes; reject both/neither/invalid fields,
  never substitute the current clock or the outer journal timestamp.
- Before business effects, reject a new valid desired-close plan whose OPEN target is already in captured pickup membership.
  Report Sync/Refresh and remove only its owned, current, prepared entry. Preserve unchanged closed siblings, reopen and clean
  retries; this admission gate does not establish safety for already-issued packets or no-plan legacy live compatibility.
- Retire a losing intent on foreign completion only with complete modern receipt groups, globally unique keys, exact lifecycle,
  Sign/membership and current-phone proof, internally consistent counts, and a stable revision within the original Reset epoch.
  Refuse changed targets already admitted; bind the latest journal context and `allowedAddKeys`, acknowledge the collected
  mirror and re-read an unchanged full proof fingerprint before removal. Retirement must not write history/pickup, grant
  additions or set transition permission. Legacy, partial, claimed, reset-changed or drifting evidence remains HOLD.
- Only the live original operation may retire a first-abort plan after actual `committed:false` and no prior updater proposal;
  a speculative proposal followed by a mismatch remains unresolved. Reset governs counts, not desired history: an exact plan
  may finish history using its captured timestamp after Reset, but cannot re-add older pickup keys. Preserve ambiguous combined
  history/pickup evidence across Reset instead of deleting it as count-only cancellation.
- «យករួច» follows money (owner decision): a closed copy in trash that deducted money (`remove` · `returned`: `trashReasonDeducted()`) and a
  code an unfinished restore inserted (`restorePendingCode()`) do not count ➜ `pickupCopyMarks()` is the one truth for `pickupTruthMarks()`. Remove deletes a closed barcode's key: live beside its mirror drop, durably in cleanup stage
  `collected` (`dropCleanupCollected()`; the entry clears only when both answer `true`); `remarkCleanupCollectedKept()` re-adds the key of a
  barcode a restore brought back closed when a late drop deleted it. Restore of a closed barcode from deducted trash writes its key back through
  a pickup journal entry begun before the finalize ⛔ never after it (a crash between them loses the key). A close/reopen whose server verdict
  excludes a local mark decides that key from fresh server views (`reapplyPickupMarks(…, true)` ➜ `revertPickupMarks(…, true)`) ⛔ never the
  stale local previous state (it re-added a key another device's Remove deleted). The close's own re-write is followed by a read of its row: a key it wrote whose barcode left the row or is open there is decided by fresh truth and only deleted (`healPickupKeysLeftRow()`) ⛔ never re-added (a Reset stays); the row is read again right before the delete, and a key a newer pickup write of this device added after the heal began is never deleted (`pickupKeyAddedSince()`, checked inside the transaction) ⛔ never delete a key a later close wrote (`ZoeW/tests/close-pickup-key-race.test.ts`). Delete and pickup cleanup keep the key (money untouched).
- ⛔ No arithmetic on `packagesPickedUp` or `pickedUpPhones[...]` anywhere: close = write a key · reopen = delete a key ·
  ⛔ never reintroduce delta arithmetic on pickup stats.
- Barcode key = `barcodeRegistryKey(code)`. The server verdict beats the local view (`reapplyPickupMarks()` writes it and
  removes local marks it excludes). A failed write never rolls memory back — toast and let the next snapshot heal.
- A rejected close and pickup-intent recovery read history/trash from the server before reconciling pickup marks; unreadable data retains the intent. An unchanged mark preserves Reset instead of resurrecting older closed barcodes.
- A phone edit must reassign only barcode identities already counted in that day's pickup record; changing an owner must not
  create a missing key or restore a Reset count. Scan merges change nothing.
- `pickupSetFromRecord(record)` reads only `pickedUpBarcodes` (absent ➜ empty set) ⛔ never seed a day from a recount of history or from
  placeholder keys (owner decision: no compat before launch; a recount would bring a Reset back).
- Reset writes `{ packagesPickedUp: 0 }` ⛔ never deletes the node (display would recount history). Day basis:
  `getFilterTargetDateKey()`. Money untouched.

## ⛔ Ledger: "apply ➜ revert" is a true inverse

- `ledgerAppliedDelta(before, after)` is the single revert basis — revert the applied delta.
- Verdict `null` = not applied: `ledgerServerVerdict(serverPromise)` (rejection or `committed: false`) ➜
  `{cod:0,dod:0,count:0}` (an `unknown` rejection, read or unread, adds `unknown: true` ➜ same arithmetic, never `ok`). In-memory revert is idempotent
  (`ledgerMemoryCompensationClaimed`) in both orders.
- Server-side revert waits for the server verdict: `commitDailyRevenueDelta` / `commitMonthlyRevenueDelta` capture
  `serverBefore`/`serverAfter` inside the transaction.
- Every stats-node write clamps before returning (day · month · pickup). `ZoeErrors.capture('… clamped to 0')` is not a
  clamp. `pickedUpPhones/$phoneKey` requires `> 0` ➜ delete keys reaching 0.
- Keep the reverse: `50 - 12.5` writes exactly `37.5`.
- Before the first cleanup debit, capture authoritative server witnesses and verify the intent is durably journaled. Never refresh prior witnesses during ledger recovery; keep unresolved entries across restarts, cap the stored retry counter and suppress repeated warnings rather than clearing unproven work. Later resume events may retry; this is not a total-attempt ceiling or backoff. Guard: `ZoeW/tests/three-device-recovery.test.ts`.
- Cleanup receipts record the actual clamped COD, DOD and count separately in daily and monthly buckets. Restore reads both authoritative receipts, matches the lifecycle stamp, and credits each bucket only its own applied deduction; a completed receipt credits zero on replay.
- Trash requiring deduction proof must remain held when either receipt is absent or pruned; never reconstruct proof from parcel face values.
- Deploy receipt-accepting Business rules and Supabase migrations before releasing the client, and require every client to update before relying on monthly proof: old writers may erase it. Never treat a partial client rollout as protocol safety.

Tools: `ledger-clamp-symmetry-test` · `ledger-failed-apply-revert-test` · `emu/ledger-revert-emu-test` ·
`money-guardian-test` · `price-edit-abort-test` · `revenue-rules-clamp-test` · `revenue-fuzz-test` · `ZoeW/tests/cleanup-ledger-key.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/trash-retry-session.test.ts`.

## ⛔ Duplicate barcodes

Layers: debounce 2.5s · `isBarcodeAlreadyUsed()` · **`claimBarcodeInRegistry()` (the only server layer)** ·
`addOrUpdateEntry()` merge. ⛔ Saving needs a real `'claimed'` verdict ("can't verify" ≠ "no duplicate"). Offline RTDB
hangs ➜ `withTimeout` throws ➜ the save is refused.

## ⛔ Orphan keys in `zoew_barcode_registry`

- Release goes through `dbOp()` + `retryAsync()`, then `pendingRegistryReleases`, rerun when `.info/connected` returns
  **and** via `noteDbListenerAlive()` plus `runScheduledCleanup()`. Callbacks apply the snapshot **before**
  `noteDbListenerAlive()`.
- `registryReleaseVerdict(key)`: `release` · `owned` · `defer` (`dbListenerViewIsStale()`); the immediate path has no
  such gate.
- ⛔ Never auto-repair keys that "look orphaned" (a trap is cheaper than duplicate money). Many keys:
  `node audit-tools/registry-orphan-list.js <dump.json>` emits `{"KEY":null,…}` for a human to review and `curl -X PATCH`
  ⛔ never "Import JSON" in Console (it replaces the whole registry). The tool deletes nothing.

---

# 🏗️ Architecture

- ⛔ Functions are not globals — only the audit build (`VITE_EXPOSE_GLOBALS=1` ➜ `src/expose-globals.ts`) exposes them;
  `window.<fn>` replacement works via plugin `zoew-audit-rebind`; production builds never have these bridges. State lives
  in stores (`src/core/state.ts`).
- `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod` is the single primary source (`onValue(dbRefHistory)` ➜ `scanHistory`); no second
  projection.
- Every parcel edit runs `runTransaction` on the **server** record.

## UI structure (ZoeW)

> Derived from real `index.html` and `app.js` (`doc-scope-test` part 6). Labels are the real Khmer UI text — keep verbatim.

| Part | id | Content |
|---|---|---|
| Page 1 — data | `pageData` | daily management, number search, history table, 2 stats buttons (📅 កញ្ចប់ប្រចាំថ្ងៃ · 💵 ចំណូលប្រចាំថ្ងៃ) |
| Page 2 — scan | `pageEntry` | 3 scan modes (parcel, Locker, remove), camera, hardware scanner, image, «📋 បញ្ចូលច្រើន» (parcel mode only: `batchScanModal` ➜ `startBatchScan()`), `parcelPanel`, `lockerPanel` |
| Bottom tab bar | `pageTabBar` | page switching (`switchAppPage`) |
| Drawer | `sideDrawer` | **4 collapsible categories** (`.drawer-group`, collapsed by default, state `zoew_drawer_groups_v1`): **ការតភ្ជាប់ និងទិន្នន័យ** (Config / Reconfig · API ស្វែងរកអតិថិជន · តារាងអតិថិជន · នាំចូល Excel ទៅ Sheet) · **ZTO** (បិទតាម ZTO ស្វ័យប្រវត្តិ · ទាញបញ្ជីកញ្ចប់ពី ZTO — these and the header appear only with Fast Mode) · **ចាក់សោ និងសុវត្ថិភាព** (ចាក់សោពេលបើក App · ចូលដោយក្រយៅដៃ ឬមុខ) · **ឧបករណ៍** (កំណត់ទូ Locker · ពិនិត្យសុខភាពប្រព័ន្ធ · លុបការសរសេររង់ចាំ — shown only while writes are `blocked`, PIN). ⛔ ចូល/ចាកចេញ sits in `.drawer-foot` with the version |
| Top (...) | `globalMoreMenu` | Export Data · 🖨️ បោះពុម្ពស្លាក (current filter) · របាយការណ៍អាជីវកម្មប្រចាំខែ · កែទឹកប្រាក់/កញ្ចប់ (PIN) · អត្រាប្រាក់ · ធុងសំរាម · Reset ចំនួនយករួច (PIN) · លុបទាំងអស់ (PIN) |
| Per-row (...) | `globalMoreMenu` | កែតម្លៃកញ្ចប់ · កែលេខទូរស័ព្ទ · 🖨️ បោះពុម្ពស្លាក · លុប |
| 🔔 panel (navbar right) | `notifyDrawer` | 📦 near-expiry (24h) · 📤 removed (trash `returned` «ត្រឡប់», unseen count in the badge) — both collapsible groups · 📱 កំណែ App · 📲 Push on/off · 📢 announcements/maintenance (`public/announcements.json` + seller notices · 🧹 សម្អាត) · ⛔ "Powered By ZoeW" in this panel's footer |

- (...) menus close when an outside drag or history scroll starts; taps/scrolls inside still work; the open motion
  (`#globalMoreMenu`) honors Reduce Motion and never delays closing (`history-menu-dismiss-test.js`). A scroll closes the menu
  only when it moves the button (the scroller contains the anchor · the anchor left the DOM) or follows user input made
  **after** opening (`moreMenuScrollDismisses()` · `noteMoreMenuInput()`: input inside the menu or on its button never counts ·
  a `wheel` counts only when it starts a stream after a `MORE_MENU_WHEEL_GAP_MS` pause) ⛔ momentum or wheel inertia left over
  from before the tap never closes it (`ZoeW/tests/more-menu-scroll.test.ts` · `history-menu-dismiss-test.js`).
- **🩺 ពិនិត្យសុខភាពប្រព័ន្ធ** (drawer): **9** rows, read-only — network · database (`databaseHealthLabel()`) · clock · License
  (Supabase: shop status) · storage · Service Worker · customer table · Apps Script version · Lookup. Count derived from
  `runHealthCheck()`; verdicts follow the core-table row «Health check»; the Apps Script path makes no network call.
- `entryScanMode` (`'parcel'` / `'locker'` / `'remove'`) is the single branch point; every scan source goes through
  `triggerScanAction()`; `'remove'` isn't persisted (reload ➜ `'parcel'`).
- `syncHistoryExpandedLock()` sets `history-expanded` on `#appPages` only when the **active page's** `.page-side` is
  `.collapsed` (`activePanelSections()` reads the DOM) — a stuck lock makes a page unscrollable (`page-nav-test.js`).
- Layout: `.page-side` + `.page-main`; phone = flex column; **≥992px** = grid (side column `clamp(340px, 27vw, 460px)` + rest), tab bar under the navbar
  via `order`. A closed drawer has `visibility: hidden`.

## Swipe gestures (<992px) — `setupSwipeGestures()` ➜ `bindPanelSwipe()` ×2

| Page | `.page-side` | `.page-main` | Handle | Scroll container |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| data | `#dataSideSection` | `#dataMainSection` | `#dragHandle` | `#tableResponsive` |
| scan | `#entrySideSection` | `#entryMainSection` | `#entryDragHandle` | `#entryTableResponsive` or `#lockerTableResponsive` |

- Both pages behave the same. `entryScrollerInView()` picks the visible container; list cards need `.panel-section`.
- `.collapsed`/`.search-focus` and the `history-expanded` lock change at `touchend` (never mid-gesture on iOS);
  `touchcancel` drops the intent. ⛔ No `transition` on them (`max-height` from `none` can't animate); collapsed panels
  are `visibility: hidden`.
- Swiping up never closes the panel during a search (`phoneSearchIsActive()`: suggestions open · the field focused with a value · or the search pulled up (`dataPanelSearchFocus`), keyboard closed included) (`ios-panel-glide-test` part 7 · `phone-search-swipe-test`).
- iOS standalone: while the phone search is pulled up (`dataPanelSearchFocus`), a scan-tab search field is focused or a modal is open, a document scroll (window `scroll` · `visualViewport` `scroll`) is a
  keyboard artifact (the root is locked, `#appPages` owns scrolling) ➜ `restoreIOSDocumentScroll()` returns it to 0 (`listenIOSDocumentScroll()` in
  boot, besides the `visualViewport` `resize` reset); other fields keep iOS's scroll-into-view; Android/web untouched (`ZoeW/tests/ios-search-viewport.test.ts`).
  iOS never shrinks the viewport for its keyboard (Android does) ➜ while that search is pulled up (`iosSearchDocumentLocked()`), `visualViewport` `resize` measures
  `visualViewportKeyboardInset()` (≥ `KEYBOARD_MIN_INSET_PX`) into `uiState.iosKeyboardInset` ➜ `body.ios-keyboard` + `--ios-keyboard-inset` ➜ `.app-pages`
  `padding-bottom` (`react-root.css`, `html.ios-standalone` only) so the list card ends above the keyboard and scrolls inside, and a vertical `touchmove` outside a
  scroller with room in that direction is `preventDefault()`ed (`blockIOSKeyboardPan()`: the document pan iOS allows with the keyboard up, which `restoreIOSDocumentScroll()`
  would snap back) ⛔ never block a scroller with room · a horizontal drag · a drag inside another text field · the search fields themselves (`IOS_SEARCH_FIELDS`)
  block vertical drags too: the focused field's text-interaction pan (a `UIPanGestureRecognizer` under the web view) ignores `touch-action` and moved the page
  while every other point stood still (owner report) ➜ only a prevented **first** `touchmove` fails it (WebKit `_touchMoveDeferringGestureRecognizer`); horizontal
  drags stay native (caret) (`ZoeW/tests/ios-keyboard-pan.test.ts` · `gesture-test` focused data and scan search fields).
  ⛔ The touchmove block alone is not enough (owner video: the page and the navbar still moved): WebKit decides the main scroll view's pan in the UI process from the
  `touch-action` at the touch start ➜ while `body.ios-keyboard`, `body` and `.app-pages` get `touch-action: none` (`react-root.css`; `.app-pages`' `overflow-x` computes to
  `auto`, so it restarts the used value at `auto` and needs its own `none`) · scroll containers inside restart at `auto` ➜ the list scrolls · the active list scroller
  (`activePanelSections().scroller`) and the open phone suggestion box (`.phone-suggest`, fixed · `overflow-y: auto`) that cannot scroll (empty · short · few
  suggestions) have no child scroll view ➜ `measureIOSKeyboardListRoom()` (ResizeObserver on the scroller, its content and the suggestion box while the search is locked)
  ➜ `uiState.iosKeyboardListFixed` · `iosKeyboardSuggestFixed` ➜ `body.ios-keyboard-list-fixed` · `body.ios-keyboard-suggest-fixed` ➜ `touch-action: none` on it · both
  get `overscroll-behavior-y: contain` (a scrollable end never chains to the main scroll view) ⛔ every point above the keyboard either refuses the Y pan or lies in a
  scroller with room + contain (`gesture-test` WebKit used-touch-action model on a 22px grid, data and scan pages, few and many suggestions ·
  `ZoeW/tests/ios-search-resnap.test.tsx`). While a modal holds the keyboard (`iosModalKeyboardLocked()`: `uiState.modalViewportHeight > 0`
  on iOS standalone) the same lock applies to the whole modal (`iosKeyboardPanLocked()`): `body` and `.modal` get `touch-action: none`, `.modal-content`
  `overscroll-behavior-y: contain`, and the touchmove block covers the backdrop · a card or a field without room · text fields inside the modal (owner
  video: a drag on the backdrop panned the page and the modal followed `offsetTop` a frame late) ⛔ never block a scroller with room (a long textarea · a
  long card) or a horizontal drag (`ZoeW/tests/ios-keyboard-pan.test.ts` · `gesture-test` batch-scan modal).
  Pulling the search down (`dataPanelSearchFocus` true ➜ false, iOS standalone phone) pauses snap (`beginPanelGlideSnapPause()`) **before** React commits it
  (`settleIOSSearchPullDown()` runs in the store subscriber, inside the immediate commit): while pulled up `.page-main`'s snap offset is 0 = the scroll position, so WebKit
  records it as the snapped box and its re-snap after layout (WebKit `ScrollSnapAnimatorState::resnapAfterLayout` · synchronous, in the first layout after the commit) would scroll to `.page-main`'s new offset — the search card
  under the navbar · with snap off WebKit drops its snap state and rebuilds it at 0 when the pause ends (nearest within proximity: none) ⛔ never check or correct before the
  commit only. That pause covers only the commit's layout (released at once; the search glides keep their own) and a touch inside the pull-down window
  (`IOS_SEARCH_PULLDOWN_SETTLE_MS`) ends any glide pause (`noteIOSKeyboardTouchStart()` ➜ `endPanelGlideSnapPause()`) ⛔ never leave snap off while the user scrolls (the scroll
  rests with the search card half under the navbar). `#appPages` still scrolled after that commit ➜ 0 + a new `panelGlideFrom()` from the visible position of the search
  card and the list card ⛔ never an instant jump · once more after `IOS_SEARCH_PULLDOWN_SETTLE_MS` unless a finger touched meanwhile, the search was pulled up again or the field is focused — Chromium
  clamps to 0 by itself (Android correct) (`ZoeW/tests/ios-search-resnap.test.tsx`: WebKit re-snap model on the real React DOM · `ZoeW/tests/ios-search-pulldown.test.ts`).
  That reset lands mid keyboard animation (the page drops, then slides back) ➜ iOS must not scroll at all: a tap on an unfocused search input (phone search or scan tab · iOS
  standalone · < 992px · one finger · ≤ `IOS_SEARCH_TAP_SLOP_PX` · ≤ `IOS_SEARCH_TAP_MAX_MS`; the scan-tab fields also on Android — APK and Chrome, `isAndroidDevice()`: Chromium scrolls a field focused by the tap into view when the IME shows while the glide still holds it low ➜ the document panned, the glide overshot and the page snapped back (owner video) · the phone search stays native on Android) ➜ `focusIOSSearchWithoutScroll()` prevents the `touchend`,
  on Android first returns a panned visual viewport to the top (`resetVisualViewportPan()`: `documentElement.scrollIntoView` ⛔ never `window.scrollTo` — it moves only the layout
  viewport; a pan left by an earlier field kept the collapsed search above the visible area, owner video), pulls the search up (scan tab: collapses the scan panel) **before** `focus({ preventScroll: true })` (`listenIOSSearchFocus()` in boot, capture, `touchend` non-passive); drags · long
  presses · a focused input · other elements stay native; a refused focus pulls back down and leaves later taps to iOS (`ZoeW/tests/ios-search-tap-focus.test.ts`).
- iOS handoff: `usesIOSPanelHandoff()` (`navigator.standalone === true` + `CSS.supports('-webkit-touch-callout','none')`);
  Android stays passive. On iOS, non-passive `touchmove` calls `preventDefault()` only when `.collapsed` · clearly down ·
  past 8px · `scrollTop <= 1`; the intent latches until the last `touchend`.

### ⛔ Finger-following motion — the 5-point table, all required

The list card (`.page-main`) has the same height in normal and full-screen modes:

| # | What | Where |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `.page-main` `height: calc(100dvh - --chrome-top - --chrome-bottom - 16px)` + `flex: none` | `src/styles/app.css` `@media (max-width: 991px)` |
| 2 | flex chain: `.history-section`/`.panel-section`/`#parcelPanel`/`#lockerPanel` `flex: 1; min-height: 0`; `.table-responsive` `max-height: none; flex: 1; min-height: 0` (scoped to `.page-main`) | same |
| 3 | `.app-pages` `scroll-snap-type: y proximity` + `scroll-padding-top` = `padding-top`; children `scroll-snap-align: start` | same |
| 4 | `panelGlideFrom()` called unconditionally in `applyPanelAction()` and the `#dragHandle` click ⛔ no iOS exception | `src/app/behaviors/panel-motion.ts` |
| 5 | `panelGlideFrom()` pauses snap (`#appPages.panel-gliding`) and releases via `anim.finished.then(release, release)` **and** `setTimeout(…, PANEL_GLIDE_MS + GRACE)`; old callbacks never release a newer pause | `src/app/behaviors/panel-motion.ts` + `src/styles/app.css` |

⛔ Missing `scroll-padding-top` or a stuck `panel-gliding` ➜ PTR dies (it needs `scrollTop <= 1`).
`clearSensitiveModalFields()` calls `endPanelGlideSnapPause()`. `panel-motion-test.js`: `cardHeightDelta`/
`tableHeightDelta` ➜ points 1–2 · `snapRestNearTop` ➜ point 3 · `residualTransform` ➜ point 4.
Auto pull-up (`setPhoneSearchPulledUp()`): the search card must stay the last child of `.page-side`. Focus and blur glide (`glidePhoneSearchPulledUp()`: FLIP of the search card `dataSearchCard` and `.page-main` through `panelGlideFrom()`) ⛔ never an instant jump. Scan-tab search fields (`entryListSearchInput` · `lockerListSearchInput`) collapse the scan panel on focus with the same glide (`entrySearchFocused()`) and open it again when the empty field blurs, only when the focus collapsed it (any other expansion clears that: `listenEntrySearchPanel()` · only the shown mode's field counts) · the Locker filter select (`lockerListFilter`) belongs to the Locker search (`entrySearchInUse()`): focus moving to it or a chosen position keeps the panel collapsed, its blur reopens only when the search is empty and «ទីតាំងទាំងអស់» is chosen ⛔ never reopen when the focus moves from the search to the select (owner report: the list dropped and left only the select above the iOS picker) ⛔ never while the camera scans (a hidden camera would keep adding parcels); `entrySearchMovesField()` is the single decider for the focus, the iOS tap interception and the iOS document-scroll reset ➜ a field that does not move (camera live, panel open) keeps iOS's native tap and scroll-into-view (`history-window-check` · `ZoeW/tests/ios-entry-search-tap.test.ts`). Search glides pass `PANEL_SEARCH_GLIDE` (gentle curve · start paused at the old position and play only once two consecutive frames are ≤ `PANEL_GLIDE_FLOW_FRAME_MS` apart, ceiling `PANEL_GLIDE_HOLD_MAX_MS` — iOS shows no frames while it prepares the keyboard, a running clock would skip the glide) ⛔ swipe/handle glides keep the default motion; a new glide on an element cancels its old one (`stopPanelGlide()`). `positionPhoneSuggestBox()` and its scroll/resize scheduler return before any React flush or rAF while `phoneSuggestOpen` is false; an open list still coalesces to one frame. Phone suggestions stay closed while the search card glides (`phoneSearchGlideRunning()`) and drop in once the glide `settled()` (`dropPhoneSuggestBox()`) ⛔ never shown at the card's old position. A scan-tab search in a collapsed panel (`uiState.entrySearchActive` ➜ `.entry-search-open`) keeps the empty message under the table header ⛔ never at the card bottom (the WebView/Chrome shrinks after the keyboard ➜ it would jump) (`ZoeW/tests/search-glide-hold.test.ts`).

## ⛔ `--chrome-bottom` and safe-area

```js
tabbar.offsetHeight + Math.max(0, document.body.getBoundingClientRect().height - window.innerHeight)
```

⛔ Never `tabbar.offsetHeight` alone, `body.getBoundingClientRect().bottom`, or a transformed tabbar rect. Only
`html.ios-standalone` extends the body by the inset and locks the root ⛔ never remove it, never add a generic
`@media (display-mode: standalone)`. Chromium has `env(safe-area-*) = 0` ➜ tests simulate (`min-height:
calc(100dvh + 34px)`) and re-run `measureAppChromeSize()`. Changes here need a deploy preview and a real iPhone.
iOS standalone also re-measures when the body or a bar's border-box resizes (`listenIOSPageSize()`): WebKit can apply `100dvh` and
`env(safe-area-*)` after the rotation's resize event (owner photo: iPhone PWA landscape, list card 0px) ⛔ never rely on resize events alone ·
`panel-motion-test` (stale rotation both ways · SE inset-only).

## Pull-to-refresh — `setupIOSPullToRefresh()`

- `boot-flags.js` sets `html.ios-standalone` before the stylesheet; `#appPages` is the single scroll owner.
- PTR only when every container is at the top (`scrollTop <= 1`), one touch, clearly down; modals/drawer/inputs/bars are
  not targets.
- ⛔ WebKit decides native panning on the **first** `touchmove` only (`m_touchMovePreventionState`); once UIKit's pan begins
  (~10pt) with no `touchmove` prevented, later ones are uncancelable ➜ PTR parks · the page only bounces ➜ a tracked pull claims
  every downward, mostly vertical `touchmove` before `AXIS_SLOP` (`claimsBeforeSlop()`), on iOS standalone and Android native ⛔ never
  wait for the slop before the first `preventDefault()` · iOS keeps row buttons (`button, a` inside the table) unclaimed before the
  slop (a tap stays a tap) · upward · sideways · nested scrolled · overlays · controls stay native (`gesture-test` WebKit model).
- ⛔ Owner rules: start in the **top 40%** (`PTR_START_ZONE_RATIO`) · any open layer ➜ no PTR (`ptrBlockedByOverlay()`,
  state remembered at `pointerdown` on `window` capture) · one haptic tick at the threshold (`hapticTick()`: APK ➜ Capacitor Haptics · web ➜
  `navigator.vibrate` only; iPhone web has no vibration API and the `<input type="checkbox" switch>` trick is closed — since iOS 26.5 WebKit plays that haptic
  only on a trusted tap, and a script `label.click()` inside the pull gesture broke PTR itself (owner report: iOS PTR fired only sometimes) ⛔ never call
  `.click()` on anything from the PTR path · accepted boundary: no PTR haptic on iPhone web).
- ⛔ Panel `.collapsed`/`.search-focus` or `history-expanded` ➜ PTR fully off; the non-passive listener is armed ahead and
  removed via `MutationObserver` ⛔ never installed after `touchstart`.
- PTR layout (`react-root.css` only): below 992px, `.ptr-indicator` uses `top: calc(var(--chrome-top, 51px) - 41px)`
  so the measured navbar height keeps the ready indicator gap stable on phones/tablets and after navbar resizing.
  ≥992px keeps `top: calc(var(--chrome-top, 65px) + var(--tabbar-height, 45px) - 41px)`. Gesture thresholds stay unchanged.
- `iosTouchArbiter` (`Touch.identifier`): 0–30px nothing · ~56–212px spring back · ~213px+ refresh · second finger
  cancels; panel swipes need vertical ratio 1.6.
- Reload: marker `zoew_ptr_reload_pending` · `history.scrollRestoration='manual'` · clear offsets ⛔ never a bare
  `location.reload()` · 5s watchdog · `beforeunload` cancels the watchdog without clearing markers.

## Bar hiding — `setupChromeAutoHide()`

- Only the **bottom tab bar** hides ⛔ never add `body.chrome-hidden .app-navbar`.
- ⛔ Showing/hiding never changes any scroll container's height or padding: the bar is `position: fixed`, moved only by
  `translate3d`.
- Android: the list's bar space and `clip-path` follow «Locked CSS invariants» (`--tabbar-height`).
  Card bottom matches iOS: `react-root.css` in `@supports (not (-webkit-touch-callout: none))
  and selector(:has(*))` clips `.app-card` at `--tabbar-height` (8px above the bar) and paints edges (`::before` table
  edge + `--card-bg` shadows · `::after` card edge catching taps) ⛔ paint only; `chrome-hidden` removes clip +
  `visibility: hidden` ⛔ never change `position`/`content` by bar state. Cards ending in `.empty-state`:
  `margin-bottom: var(--tabbar-height)`, no padding, no `::before`. Checkers simulating iOS drop that block
  (`dropAndroidOnlyCss`). Guard: `panel-motion-test` part 9.
- iOS: `@supports (-webkit-touch-callout: none)` — real card height, grows to fill (`clip-path` doesn't work on Safari);
  Chromium never applies it ➜ `panel-motion-test.js` injects it.
- ⛔ The 180ms delay and `chrome-space-released` stay removed.
- The new-version banner (`.app-update-banner`) sits above the bottom tab bar below 992px (`bottom: var(--tabbar-height)`, `react-root.css`) and drops to the screen bottom only with `chrome-hidden` ⛔ never over the tab bar (`layout-check`).
- rAF-coalesced scroll handler; `SHOW_AFTER` (48px) > `HIDE_AFTER` (36px) on purpose.
- ⛔ The bar moves only on **user** scrolling: a delta counts only during a touch or ≤ `CHROME_SCROLL_INTENT_MS` after touch ·
  wheel · key · pointer input; after the last input at most one flip; nothing counts for `CHROME_FLIP_SETTLE_MS` after any flip.
  Programmatic scroll writes (virtualizer corrections · clamps · scroll anchoring) never drive it — on the APK they formed a
  show/hide loop on a still list. Reaching the top zone still shows it. Guards: `ZoeW/tests/chrome-autohide-intent.test.tsx` ·
  `gesture-test`.
- ⛔ APK soft keyboard: the WebView resizes in one step when the IME starts opening ➜ a fixed bar would jump above it. Inside the
  same `resize` handler `noteKeyboardViewport()` (native Android only) sets `uiState.keyboardOpen` (immediate commit) when the
  height drops ≥ `KEYBOARD_MIN_INSET_PX` below the **last height before the keyboard** (any other same-width resize re-bases
  it: split-screen) while a text field is focused **and** a text field got `focusin`/`pointerdown` within `KEYBOARD_INTENT_MS`;
  it clears only on a resize (back within the inset of that base, or ≥ the inset above the lowest open height) ⛔ never on blur
  (the IME is still closing). Body gets `keyboard-open` + `chrome-hidden` (bar hidden with no transition, list uses the
  hidden-bar layout); `uiState.chromeHidden` (scroll state) is never touched, so the bar returns to the state it had. Web/iOS
  unchanged (the keyboard overlays). Anything meaning "bar hidden" reads both fields. Capacitor `SystemBars` pads the decor by the
  full IME height when the show animation starts ➜ Android 11+ `MainActivity` (`KeyboardOpenHold`, platform
  `WindowInsetsAnimation` on the decor) keeps the WebView at its height from `onPrepare` (parents unclipped) until `onEnd` or
  `KEYBOARD_HOLD_MAX_MS`, so the keyboard slides over the page and the WebView shrinks once at the end ⛔ never `ViewCompat`
  (below Android 11 it replaces Capacitor's insets listener). Any strip left shows the window background ➜ the bridge theme's
  `android:windowBackground` = `--body-bg`. Guards: `ZoeW/tests/keyboard-tabbar.test.tsx` · `ZoeW/scripts/history-window-check.mjs`
  · `npm run android:check`.
- ⛔ Modals and drawers (☰ · 🔔) cover the bar (z-index) ➜ they never move it: `openModalHelper()` and `openSideDrawer()`
  never call `showAppChrome()`, and scrolls inside a `.modal` or `.side-drawer` never drive it. On Android a bar toggle
  flips the list's `clip-path` and `.table-responsive` `padding-bottom` (`app.css`) ➜ the whole rendered list
  repaints, cost grows with rows. A page scroller reaching the top while an overlay is open still shows it; page switches
  still show it. Guards: `ZoeW/tests/modal-chrome-state.test.tsx` · `gesture-test` · `perf-check` (modal and drawer open
  with the bar hidden ≤ 1.8× PrePaint+Paint of the bar shown, CPU ÷4).

## Frame rate and lite mode

- `sampleFramePace()`: median of 90 rAF intervals, clamped `[10, 120]`. `longFrameThresholdMs()` = frame budget × 1.6
  (min 12ms) ⛔ no fixed number.
- `setupAdaptivePerformance()` measures twice (1.5s and 10s); `body.perf-lite` only when both drop; never touches
  business features. Each round measures the rate and the pace in **one** window (`sampleFramePace()`: budget = that
  window's median) ⛔ never in separate windows — an LTPO display switching 120 ➜ 60 between them counts every normal
  60Hz frame as long.
- High-refresh devices are learned, not guessed: `sampleScrollHz()` (passive capture `scroll` · ≤ 1 per
  `SCROLL_HZ_GAP_MS` · `DISPLAY_HZ_SAMPLES` frames · median) raises `uiState.displayHzPeak` (max only · `zoew_display_hz_peak_v1` per
  device); ≥ `HIGH_REFRESH_HZ` ➜ never `perf-lite` and an existing one is cleared (`noteDisplayHzPeak()`). Guard:
  `ZoeW/tests/adaptive-refresh.test.ts`.

## Barcode scanner

- ZXing-WASM in the repo (`vendor/zxing-wasm.js` + `vendor/zxing_reader.wasm`, both in `CORE_SHELL`) via
  `ZXingWASM.readBarcodes(imageData, options)` ⛔ CSP needs `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`.
- **Code 128 + QR only** (`SCAN_FORMAT_NAMES = ['Code128']` for the live band · `SCAN_QR_FORMAT_NAMES` / `NATIVE_SCAN_QR_FORMAT_NAMES` in
  `src/services/scan-formats.ts`) ⛔ never add other formats (ITF · CODABAR · CODE_39 have no mandatory check digit ➜ wrong numbers read
  "successfully"; QR carries Reed-Solomon). Owner request: the scan tab (parcel · remove · Locker), its image picker and the Sign dialog read the QR
  printed beside the waybill. With no expected list (scan tab · image) a read keeps its Code128 and takes a QR only when no Code128 was read and
  its text has the waybill shape (`pickScanCode()` · `ztoBarcodeShapeIsValid()`) ⛔ never a promo QR (a URL) as a parcel; the Sign dialog takes only
  a text equal to one of its barcodes (`palmSignPickCode()`). ZXing live loop: every `LIVE_SCAN_QR_EVERY`-th decode reads an unsquashed frame
  (≤ `LIVE_SCAN_QR_DIM`, `decodeLiveQrFrame()`) ⛔ never QR in the squashed band. Android: the boot `BarcodeDetector` is replaced by a
  `code_128` + `qr_code` one only when `getSupportedFormats()` lists both (`upgradeNativeDetectorForQr()`, never over a detector changed meanwhile) (`ZoeW/tests/scan-qr.test.ts` · `ZoeW/tests/scan-image-qr.test.ts` · `scan-engine-test`).
- `confirmLiveScan()`: same number on `SCAN_CONFIRM_REPEATS` (2) consecutive frames for both live paths (image/hardware
  are single-frame). `takeFreshVideoFrame()` prevents decoding one frame twice.
- `LIVE_SCAN_WIDTH_STEPS = [640, 800, 1024, 1280]` start at the highest; `noteLiveScanCost()` (EMA) steps down above
  `LIVE_SCAN_SLOW_MS` (22ms), up below `LIVE_SCAN_FAST_MS` (9ms) ⛔ never pin the width. Band height
  `LIVE_SCAN_MAX_BAND_PX` (240px). Interval `LIVE_SCAN_MIN_FPS` 10 ➜ `LIVE_SCAN_MAX_FPS` 120.
- Config QR scanning is a separate reader (`configQrReader`).
- zxing-wasm caches the module promise ➜ a failed WASM load (fetch · compile) rejects every later `readBarcodes()`.
  `noteScanEngineLoadFailed()` is the single failure path: `purgeZXingModule()` · decode gated by `scanEngineDown` ⛔ never
  `readBarcodes()` between purge and re-prepare (the library's default `locateFile` is a CDN) · re-prepare from `./vendor/`
  on `SCAN_ENGINE_RETRY_STEPS_MS` (last step repeats) · Sentry once per page · toast once at `SCAN_ENGINE_FAIL_TOAST_AFTER`.
  Guards: `ZoeW/tests/scan-engine-recovery.test.ts` · `scan-engine-test` (real library, WASM 404).

## Clock

Retention/revenue timestamps (`createdAt` · `closedAt` · `deletedAt` · `lockerUpdatedAt` and the compared "now") use
**`getServerNow()`**. Local cosmetic timers (PIN lockout · salt id · scan debounce · script deadlines · cache TTL) keep
raw `Date.now()` on purpose.

- Search argument-less `new Date()` too; `clock-hygiene.js` scans the AST in 4 directions with no dead allowlist entries.
- Business calendar `Asia/Phnom_Penh`: `appZoneParts()` (Intl + fixed UTC+7 fallback) · `getZoneDateKey(ms, dayOffset)`
  ⛔ never `d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1)` · `getFormattedClockTime(ms)` for `item.time`. ⛔ Old data never recomputed;
  stored format `HH:MM:SS (YYYY-MM-DD)` never changes; sorting via `formatScanStamp(raw)` (fail-open).
- `elapsedSince(mark)`: `!mark` or `delta < 0` ➜ `Infinity` (fail-open); shared byte-identical in both apps. Deadline
  comparisons (`Date.now() < lockoutUntil`) stay.
- Destructive cleanup: `cleanupClockIsTrustworthy()` = `serverClockTrusted && isDatabaseConnected` ⛔ never drop
  `isDatabaseConnected`; the gate sits inside `runAutomaticCleanupRules()` and `runAutomaticDeletedCleanup()` ⛔ no
  `performance.now()` jump detection.
- `serverClockOffsetIsFromServer(offsetMs)` = `offsetMs !== 0 || isDatabaseConnected || hasEverConnectedToDatabase`
  ⛔ never simplify; non-number values `return` at once. `.info/serverTimeOffset` applies a value only once it holds; a
  backend switch resets `serverClockTrusted` and the offset (the old backend never vouches for the new one).

## ⛔ CSP and `data-act`

`script-src` has no `'unsafe-inline'` in both apps ➜ `on*=` attributes and inline `<script>` are silently refused.

| What | How |
|---|---|
| no-arg call | `data-act="openSideDrawer"` |
| constant args | `data-act="filterDataByDate" data-args='["today"]'` |
| data args | `data-a1="${sanitizeInput(item.id)}"` |
| event | `data-evt="1"` ➜ `event` prepended |
| element | `data-self="1"` ➜ element first |
| other events | `data-on="change"` (`input`/`submit`) |

1. The allowlist is the boundary ⛔ never plain `window[name]`: ZoeKeyGen `ACTION_ALLOWLIST` (`app.js`) · ZoeW `ACTION_REGISTRY`
   through `lookupAction()` (`src/core/action-registry.ts`); ZoeW `ACTION_ALLOWLIST` (`src/core/runtime.ts`, never imported by the
   app) only mirrors its names for checkers (equality: `tests/batch-scan.test.ts`).
2. New button ➜ add its name (ZoeW: `ACTION_REGISTRY` **and** the mirror) (`wiring.js` · `csp-enforced-test.js` assert both directions).
3. `event.currentTarget` is `document` ➜ use `data-self`.
4. ⛔ Never a second listener (incl. `el.onclick = …` from JS) on an element with `data-act` — use one `data-act` deciding
   by state (`navAuthFlow()`); `page-nav-test` counts 1 real call; `action-binding-test` locks it in both apps.
5. `<head>` scripts live in `boot-flags.js` (`<script src>` before the stylesheet, in the SW core shell via
   `serviceWorkerPlugin` ➜ `__CORE_SHELL__`).
6. ZoeW `style-src` keeps `'unsafe-inline'` ⛔ don't remove without request and measurement. ZoeKeyGen has **no** `'unsafe-inline'` in `style-src` (owner request): no `style=` attribute in `index.html` (utility classes · `modal-w-*` carry `--modal-w`) · QR as SVG · CSSOM writes only; `csp-enforced-test` part 3 loads it under the real header (0 violations · reverse probe) ⛔ never add an inline style there.
7. ⛔ Scanner and SheetJS scripts stay vendored; never add their CDN origins to `script-src`. Other remote scripts
   remain restricted to the reasoned Firebase/Sentry allowlist (core-table Public repo row); `sw.js` never caches cross-origin requests.

## ⛔ Cross-origin resources in `<head>`

They never enter the cache ➜ if render-blocking, a dead network shows a white screen. No cross-origin script sits in `<head>`
(the Sentry SDK loads later from `error-reporting.js` with SRI; `sentry-load-race-test.js`); cross-origin stylesheets use `media="print"` switched to `all` by
`boot-flags.js` on `DOMContentLoaded` ⛔ never `onload="this.media='all'"`; `<noscript>` fallback.

## Locked CSS invariants (`src/styles/*.css`)

`ZoeW/src/styles/app.css` is pinned by `forbidden-zone-lock` (no comments); React-only CSS goes to
`react-root.css` · `native.css`.

| Selector | Rule | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `.status-dot::after` | animate `transform`/`opacity` · online blinks 3 times then stops (`animation-iteration-count: 3`) · "connecting" `infinite` | no endless paint while idle (LTPO Hz · battery) |
| `.call-btn-recall` | blinks 5.5 times then stays `--action-danger` ⛔ never `infinite` | infinite background color repaints the main thread |
| `.app-navbar` | no `backdrop-filter` · `transform` · `transition` | blur cost; the top bar never hides |
| `.modal` | plain dark backdrop | opens on every scan |
| `.scan-line` | animate `transform`, not `top` | no layout per frame while decoding |
| `.page-main` · `.table-responsive` | 5-point table rules (scoped to `.page-main`) | equal card height · modals keep 62vh |
| `.mrep-table` | `width: max-content; min-width: 100%` ⛔ not `width: 100%` | otherwise `nowrap` text overlaps instead of scrolling |
| `.app-pages` | point 3 of the 5-point table; its two declarations live in **two separate** `@media (max-width: 991px)` blocks ➜ edit one, check the other ⛔ never merge the blocks | PTR dies otherwise |
| `#appPages.panel-gliding` | `scroll-snap-type: none` | snap fights the glide |
| data search pulled up (`.page-side.search-focus`, `react-root.css`) | `.app-pages` `overflow-y: hidden` · `> .app-page.active` `height: 100%` · `.page-side.search-focus + .page-main` `flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; height: auto` (the list card takes only the space under the search card, like `history-expanded`) ⛔ never switch `.app-pages` `display` | the full-height card overflowed by the search card ➜ a short result list chained the drag to `#appPages` and snapped the search card under the navbar · a long list's last row sat below the screen |
| `.app-pages.history-expanded` | `display: block` + `> .app-page.active { height: 100% }` ⛔ never switch `.app-pages` `display` by mode | rebuilding the whole layout tree on every drag (`panel-motion-test` part 8) |
| `.table-responsive` | `padding-bottom: var(--tabbar-height)` | bar space inside the scroll container |
| `.page-main` / `.app-card` clip | `--tabbar-height`, not `--chrome-bottom`; Android card edges are paint only | safe-area sits below the viewport |
| `@supports (-webkit-touch-callout: none)` | separate iOS path | no `clip-path` on Safari |
| `.boot-splash` | `pointer-events: none` | never swallows taps |
| `.boot-splash-bar > span` | moves inside the bar (`width: 40%`, `translateX(0 → 150%)`) | no overflow at 320px |
| every `font-size` | `calc(N * var(--fs-unit))`, no fixed `px` (except `#pdfExportPrintArea`) | consistent scaling |
| `:focus-visible` · text inputs | 2px outline ring; `outline: none` needs a `:focus` replacement | keyboard/hardware-scanner users |
| `.modal-content` · `.modal-btns button` | 46px tall; non-text controls reset `min-height: 0; padding: 0; flex: none` | tap targets without stretched checkboxes |
| overrides of `.modal-content` and children | sit **after** the general rule (or raise specificity); guards compare `getComputedStyle()` with values derived from real CSS | equal specificity ➜ order decides ➜ silent death |
| `.btn-biometric` · `.bio-ico` · `.bio-label` | white background, red border, `margin-bottom`; label in 2 spans | not mistaken for an error box; flex `gap` needs elements |
| ZoeKeyGen `.app-container` · `#appContainer` | 2-column grid at `≥900px`, `max-width: 1180px`; show/hide by class `hidden`, not `style.display` | inline display beats the media query |

Type scale: phone `< 700px` (clamp to 1.1px) · tablet `700–991px` (floor 1.2px) · desktop `>= 992px` (up to 1.35px), two
declarations only ⛔ `< 700px` and `>= 992px` never change ⛔ ZoeKeyGen unchanged (`tabletStep: null`). Show/hide via
class, column widths in CSS. `--fs-unit` is registered (`@property --fs-unit { syntax: '<length>' }`, `react-root.css`) ➜ it computes to px
at `:root` and a height-only resize (APK keyboard) never recalcs every element ⛔ never unregister it (`perf-check` keyboard).
