# 🌐 Network · Service Worker · License

## ⛔ Hang ≠ fail — 5 failure modes

| Mode | Happens | Needed |
|---|---|---|
| reject | promise rejects | `.catch()` |
| absent | synchronous `TypeError` | presence check first |
| hang | neither answers nor rejects | a ceiling that settles structurally |
| slow success | resolves after the ceiling | post-commit work runs when it arrives |
| rejected but applied | `disconnect` after a server commit | read the real server first (`runTransactionResolved()`) |

- `withTransactionOutcomeResolution()` wraps `fb` once in `initFirebase()`: every `fb.runTransaction` reads REST with the
  ID token and compares with the sent/pre-send value (`applied` ➜ success · `not-applied` ➜ reject · `unknown` ➜ reject +
  Sentry money). Every move of barcodes out of history (cleanup · Remove · Delete) first claims them inside the history item
  (`beginTrashClaim()` ➜ `claimTrashBarcodes()`: a transaction on `zoew_scan_history_cod_dod/<id>` stamps `trashClaim` = a token unique to the
  operation + `trashClaimAt` on the server clock) and then moves only the barcodes that carry its own token, in a second transaction on the same path ➜
  only a claim's owner can remove that barcode, so a lost reply is decided by the token on the server (claim: our token on every stamped barcode ➜ applied ·
  move: our token still there ➜ not applied, gone ➜ applied) ⛔ never by comparing a value another device could have produced · a barcode another
  operation holds is skipped by cleanup, answered «busy» by Remove and Delete, and keeps Delete all off its row (`itemHasLiveTrashClaim()` ➜
  `CLEAR_HISTORY_IN_PROGRESS`) · a claim older than `trashClaimPolicy().staleMs` or stamped past `futureMs` holds nothing (taken over); a move
  never uses an own claim older than `refreshMs` (the next round re-stamps it) and releases, in the same write, its own claims on barcodes no longer
  eligible · an operation that ends without consuming its claims releases them through the database, SDK and generation it captured
  (`trashClaimOwner()`) ⛔ never through the live session · claim startup awaits its device journal before sending (`zoew_trash_claims_v2`,
  shop scope + document identity; the key disappears when empty). With Web Locks, the claim holds a token-specific lock until settlement.
  `ownLeftoverTrashClaim()` permits immediate reuse only within the same document and shop, after the live operation has ended.
  `resumeInterruptedCleanups()` releases another document's recorded claim only after acquiring its available Web Lock, or after the server lease
  expires when that lock cannot establish ownership. Legacy v1 journals remain untouched; older tabs cannot read new claim records.
  Recovery still writes through the captured server owner, retaining the journal until a commit or a fresh history view proves the row gone;
  stale local absence never proves release. Shared local storage is not transactional. Trash copies omit claim fields (`stripHistoryOnlyMarkers()`),
  and history writers retain barcode fields outside their own operation.
  Accepted boundary: a device whose lost move reply stays unread longer than the stale bound while another device takes over its claim.
  ➜ **every** cleanup trash write — whole or partial, acknowledged or `applied` — goes through `claimCleanupTrashSlot()`: a create-if-absent transaction on
  `zoew_recently_deleted_cod_dod/<id>` — committed, or the slot already holds our copy (same `deletedAt`: a retry after our own landed write) = ours (ledger
  follows) · another copy holding any of our claimed barcodes (`trashSlotSharesClaim()`) = another device's copy (journal cleared · no ledger) · a copy
  sharing none = another **lifecycle** of the id (an item recreated under it by «Merge into a gone item» while its old trash copy lives) ➜ the claim moves
  once to `cleanupPartialTrashId(id, <trashReason>|slot)` (journal entry + live marker re-keyed) ⛔ never «elsewhere» (the barcodes would vanish undeducted)
  ⛔ never a plain overwrite of the slot (the overwriting device deducts again · an old copy is lost) — Delete (`deleteSingleItem()`) writes through the same
  slot · Delete all's finalize fence needs `trash/<id>` absent (rules) ➜ a taken slot (our claim still on history + trash present ➜ `CLEAR_HISTORY_SLOT_TAKEN`)
  releases our own `clearClaim` (`releaseOwnClearHistoryClaim()`) and deletes through `deleteSingleItem(id, { confirmed, quiet })` ⛔ never retry the fence
  (refused every time · the claim blocks cleanup · Remove · Delete of the item) ⛔ never a REST read (none exists on Supabase · a failed read dropped the parcel). Identical claims meet at one slot: whole = item id · partial =
  `cleanupPartialTrashId()` (item timestamp + the claim token of item id · reason · the claimed codes with their `closedAt`/`restoredAt` ➜ a restored barcode
  claimed again gets a new id; fixed length ≤ 64 for `ded/<trashId>`) ⛔ never a random id for a partial claim · a definitive `not-applied` clears the
  prepared journal entry (live · late · Remove) so no resume deducts what the transaction refused (`ZoeW/tests/cleanup-applied-foreign-op.test.ts`: two devices,
  stale caches, lost replies, a foreign trash write landing after this device finished ➜ one copy, money once). Accepted boundary: a stale copy left in the
  slot by an earlier lifecycle (a trash write that failed every retry yet landed, then restored to history) makes the next identical claim skip its deduction
  (revenue stays high, never deducted twice). Until the slot answers, the journal says stage `slot` (ownership unknown) ⛔ never
  `moved` (a resume would deduct a claim that was another device's): resume resolves it with `resolveCleanupSlot()` — trash with our `deletedAt` ➜ ours ·
  another `deletedAt` sharing our barcodes ➜ elsewhere (sharing none ➜ claim, which moves) · absent ➜ the item back in history (its id holding our barcodes, or
  restored barcodes, fresh views only) ➜ elsewhere, else claim the slot.
  ⛔ `fb.get()` isn't evidence (cached).
- Cleanup evidence uses per-operation `zoew_cleanup_journal_v2:` keys scoped to shop and actor; never bulk-rewrite the journal array. Capture stable operation identity outside retryable updaters and preserve its first timestamp. Repeated updater calls replace only that operation’s speculative payload. Legacy v1 stays untouched/HOLD; switching shop/account releases stale in-memory owners but preserves their evidence. Storage refusal aborts admission; unknown pickup/mirror outcomes never hit a retry ceiling that erases them.
- Destructive history claims (Delete · Remove · automatic cleanup) persist their cleanup before-image **inside the transaction updater before returning the destructive value**; unreadable/full storage aborts before the write. Recovery runs after fresh history snapshots as well as on resume. Live ownership prevents recovery from racing the original operation. Completion releases only its captured gate token and owner object; a newer session reusing that item ID keeps its own gate. A shared Web Lock remains held until every registered operation finishes. Asynchronous journal clear/patch/stage/rekey also matches the captured deletedAt and canonical barcode lifecycle; a reused trash ID never authorizes changing a newer claim (tests/cleanup-journal-claim-race.test.ts). Releasing an owner with an unfinished journal waits for its Web Lock and any active recovery pass, then retries recovery only in the original database/auth generation and shop. Recovery freshly reads history before claiming a slot, recreating missing trash or resolving a missing flip; `zoew_restore_finalizations/<trashId>.targetId` is only a hint to a freshly read merge target, never restore proof by itself. Remove uses the keyed cleanup ledger path after securing its trash slot; Restore marks that key returned in its atomic finalization. A session fence separates monthly and daily commits. Guard: `tests/cleanup-applied-ownership.test.ts`. Other guards: the shared harness `money-cookie-blob.js` (Cookie Blob · Palm Login/shared session · real Function · both backends · interrupted pages) run by `emu/money-multidevice-emu-test` · `supabase-money-multishop-test` · `tests/money-multidevice-oracle.test.ts` · `tests/trash-retry-session.test.ts` · `tests/session-switch-fuzz.test.ts`.
- Restore persists its claim token/target under the shop scope before claiming; retry/reload must reuse that operation identity
  and recognize its already-prepared target. It must not rebuild revenue additions or overwrite the before-image from its own
  prepared result; an unknown finalization is reconciled before any new credit. One in-flight restore per trash id per device;
  terminal success or proven foreign/lost ownership clears the saved entry. A resync reply only replaces a data array if no
  listener replaced that array while the read was pending. Related guards: `tests/trash-retry-session.test.ts` · `tests/restore-prepared-retry.test.ts` (a retry whose prepared target lost codes finishes with the rest ⛔ never re-inserted);
  also measure interruption after prepare and before finalization, not only an ordinary retry.
- `dbOp(promise, msg)` (`withTimeout(…, DB_OP_TIMEOUT_MS)`, 15s) for Firebase calls behind locks, incl. via helpers ⛔ no
  direct `await fb.<dataOp>(…)`.
- RTDB queues offline writes ➜ `armLateCommit(promise, onCommitted, onFailed, label)` finishes post-commit work;
  `committed: false` and rejections go to `onFailed`; never claim a rollback while the transaction lives.
  `armLateWrite(promise, onDone)` for key releases; `notifyIfSlow(promise, ms, message)` returns the **same** promise.
- ⛔ No `dbOp()` on trash writes (core-table row «Locks ↔ hung trash writes»). ⛔ Exception: `claimBarcodeInRegistry` has no inner ceiling (call
  sites have one). The `isDeducted` flip (`markCleanupTrashDeducted()`) may sit in `dbOp()`: it is idempotent and its catch only keeps the journal
  at stage `flip` (a late landing is the wanted state; without the ceiling a hung flip would block every later resume).
- Batch work aborts after the first hang (`dbOpStalled(e)`).
- `sw.js` `timedFetch()` is the single network path (`NETWORK_TIMEOUT_MS` 20s, `AbortController` + race); `networkOnly()`
  uses it; the ceiling covers headers only.
- `loadScriptOnce()` has a ceiling and forgets on failure (identity `=== pending`); messages say "too long", not "load
  failed".

## Listener and connection recovery

- `handleDbListenerError(err, pathKey)` always gets the path key ⛔ never pass bare `handleDbListenerError`.
- `dbListenerViewIsStale(key)` is the single "untrustworthy view" basis ⛔ never `dbListenerPendingPaths.has()` directly.
- Keys come from shared constants (`DB_LISTENER_KEYS` · `DB_LISTENER_KEY_DELETED`); `initDatabaseListeners()` builds a
  `listenerRefs` map.
- `.info/*` follows the same rules (`infoListenerFailedPaths` · `noteInfoListenerAlive(pathKey)`); only `.info/connected`
  dying may announce a network drop; fix both apps.
- Progress is a timestamp (`dbListenerProgressAt`) written in `noteDbListenerAlive()` ⛔ never overwrite evidence in a
  poll.
- `canCycleDatabaseConnection()`: cycling only when `hasEverConnectedToDatabase` or `networkJustReturned`.
- `retryFirebaseSdkNow()` runs on `online` **and** `visibilitychange` in both apps (assert the events directly).
- `FIREBASE_SDK_RETRY_MIN_GAP_MS` (3s) < the ladder's first step (5s); `clearFirebaseSdkRetry()` never resets
  `lastFirebaseSdkAttemptAt`.
- SDK recovery = page reload (`reloadForFirebaseSdk()`): `firebaseSdkUnavailable` · `navigator.onLine !== false` · no
  open modal (`anyModalIsOpen()`) · `FIREBASE_SDK_RELOAD_MAX` (3) · `FIREBASE_SDK_RELOAD_MIN_GAP_MS` (20s) · reachability probe first
  (`probeFirebaseSdkHost()` · `FIREBASE_SDK_PROBE_URL` · `firebaseSdkProbeInFlight`; unreachable ➜ don't spend the
  ceiling; gate re-checked after the probe) · CSP allows the probe origin · both apps. `recoverFirebaseSdk()` exits when
  `!firebaseSdkUnavailable`. Cap spent (`firebaseSdkNeedsRefresh()`: a failed module import stays failed for the page) ➜
  the ladder · `online` · `visibilitychange` stop retrying and the status + toast say «សូម Refresh ទំព័រ» (a late SDK still
  arrives through `armLateFirebaseSdkListener()`) ⛔ never reload past the cap, not even when the probe turns reachable.
- `dbListenerResyncIsProgressing()` delays (`DB_LISTENER_PROGRESS_GRACE_MS` 20s), never blocks.
- `pendingHistoryPatches` rerun on reconnect; only `disconnect` or SDK `already deleted` may queue; opt-in per call site
  (`saveEditedPhone()` never) ⛔ never flush from the 60s cycle (`HISTORY_PATCH_RETRY_MAX`).

## Short rules

- Reconfig during SDK load: `initFirebase()` reads config before `await waitForFirebaseSDK()`; `finally` reruns only if
  the stored config changed ⛔ never unconditionally (stacks with `scheduleFirebaseSdkRetry()`). Both apps
  (`saveFirebaseConfig()` writes `zoew_firebase_config`). ⛔ Guards for two-app rules must read both apps.
- `rememberCustomerTableRow()` feeds network lookups back ⛔ never insert empty rows for "not found".
  `customerTableIsPartial`: partial miss ➜ network; fresh complete miss ➜ nothing scheduled; scheduled pulls give up
  after `CUSTOMER_TABLE_SOON_MAX_WAIT_MS`.
- `armLookupFocus()`: focus after `LOOKUP_FOCUS_GRACE_MS` (250ms) once, unless the user typed (`scanState.phoneModalTyped`
  or the phone field holds text), a text field inside the modal has focus, the modal closed or `pendingBarcode` changed ⛔ never
  pull the caret out of a field being typed (the next digit landed in the phone field). Lookup auto-save
  (`applyLookupFillToModal()` ➜ `confirmPhone(false)`) runs only while nobody typed in the scan dialog: `scanState.phoneModalTyped`
  = any `input` on its four fields (`notePhoneModalInput()`; reset by `triggerScanAction()`; lookup fills write without an
  event) ➜ typed: fill empty fields only + ℹ️ with the lookup COD/DOD, the person saves ⛔ never save a value mid-typing
  (`ZoeW/tests/scanner-modal-queue.test.tsx` · `lookup-prefetch-test`; `auto-lookup.ts` is in the ZTO lock).
- `pickupResetInFlight` releases in `resetClearHistoryOperationState()`.
- `PIN_PROMPT_MESSAGES` / `requestPinBeforeConfig(targetAction, promptKey)`: every PIN-protected action has its own entry
  ⛔ never reuse another action's key; `promptKey` changes text only (`applyPinPromptText()`) · ⛔ a target (and `openConfigModal`, the
  `null` default) is never a name buttons can call (`ACTION_REGISTRY` · `ACTION_ALLOWLIST`) unless it re-gates itself with
  `requestPinBeforeConfig(itself, …)` · `pinSetupModal` opens only while no PIN is stored (`pin-prompt-test.js`). The PIN dialog
  submits on Enter (`<form onSubmit>` ➜ `submitSecurityPinForm`) and focuses its field on desktop only (`isMobileDevice()`: a phone keeps the
  keyboard closed) (`layout-check`).
- Biometrics wrap the PIN, never replace it: `deriveLookupSecretKey(pin)` · enabling needs the real PIN · decrypted PINs
  are verified against `zoew_security_pin_hash` · PIN change unbinds · `completePinUnlock()` is the single success path ·
  web biometrics in both apps accept WebAuthn PRF only ⛔ never a `device` mode (a raw key stored beside the wrapped PIN = the PIN
  in plain sight): no PRF ➜ no enrollment (use the PIN) · the APK uses native biometrics · enrollment creates a discoverable passkey in both apps (`residentKey: 'required'`: Android
  Google Password Manager gives PRF only to passkeys) · PRF results from `create()` are used at once, `prf: {}` (no `enabled`) asks `get()`, only
  `enabled: false` means unsupported · a cancelled second scan is a cancel, never «unsupported» (`biometricPrfEval()` throws; `biometricPrfFirst` ·
  `biometricPrfEval` · `biometricPrfBytes` byte-identical in both apps) (`biometric-no-device-mode.test.tsx` · `biometric-android-prf.test.ts` ·
  `biometric-unlock-test` · `keygen-biometric-test`).
- `linkIsFrugal()` fails open and skips only optional work.

## Service Worker

- Cache-first, `CACHE_VERSION` `<app>-vN`; cleanup filter scans only its own prefix ⛔ never widen it.
- `CORE_SHELL` uses atomic `cache.addAll()` ⛔ no whole-install ceiling on it (a slow but progressing download of the shell must
  finish; a hung CORE request leaves install to the browser's own event limit). Each `OPTIONAL_SHELL` entry settles within
  `OPTIONAL_INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS` (abort + structural resolve) ➜ a hung optional never holds install or `skipWaiting()`; both apps.
- Activate cache cleanup failure still reaches `clients.claim()`; prefix filters stay unchanged. Cache API failure is never fatal: `networkOnly(request)` in 2 places (`cache.match()` second `.then` argument ·
  `caches.open()` outer `.catch`) resolves to `Response.error()` on a network failure; the `./index.html` fallback resolves it directly. Navigation miss or
  Cache API rejection fetches the same shell `cacheKey` with `request.signal` ⛔ never cache JS under the `index.html` key.
- `/.netlify/functions/` ➜ `networkOnly()` (never cached).
- Cached HTML stays with its SW version; `shellDeployIsCurrent()` and `revalidateShell()` settle within 6s
  (`ZoeW/tests/sw-revalidation-timeout.test.ts`).

## `license-verify.js` — the third network path

REST-only with its own helpers ➜ apply every network rule separately (`network-pressure-test.js` stubs it fully).

- Every request goes through `sharedRequest(key, priority, run)` (dedup + `NET_MAX_IN_FLIGHT` 2; release on both paths).
- `checkOnline()` and `syncServerTime()` check `networkLooksDown()`.
- ⛔ A skip returns `{ ok: null }`, never `{ ok: false }` (`getStatus()` deletes only on `false`).
- `activate()` uses `{ priority: true }`.
- `getStatus()` binds verdicts to the record snapshot and re-checks after `await`; a changed record is re-read, never
  deleted (`license-record-race-test.js`).
- `license-grace-test.js` extracts functions by name ➜ add new helpers to its list.

**"Can't verify" ≠ "wrong" on every axis** — outcomes `valid` · `invalid` · `unverified`; only `invalid` deletes;
`unverified` keeps but grants nothing new.

| Axis | Rule |
|---|---|
| network | skip ➜ `{ ok: null }` |
| crypto | WebCrypto failure ≠ forged signature |
| clock | deletion needs `serverTimeSynced` or a server verdict |
| rollback | `monotonicNow(record) = max(getServerNow(), record.seenMax)` |

- `activate()` needs `checkOnline().ok === true` plus `serverTimeSynced` (tries `syncServerTime({ priority: true })`).
- DB `online.expiresAt` beats the signed `exp` (Extend works); Revoke · missing · bad signature still refuse.
- Deletion needs a real `checkOnline()` verdict; server verdicts heal `seenMax`.
- Messages tell the truth: `network` · `clock-unverified` ➜ "ភ្ជាប់ Server មិនបានទេ!" ⛔ not "Key មិនត្រឹមត្រូវទេ!".

## Toasts · XSS · secrets · storage

- 4 toast classes by prefix; `renderConnectionStatus()` is the single broadcaster; toast registration lives in the DOM.
  Optimistic UI ≠ durable commit (`toast-action-truth-test.js`).
- Every HTML value via `sanitizeInput()` (`html-sink-escaping.js` scans template literals and concatenation).
- `redactDeep()` walks the whole event: Bearer/Basic before colon splitting · whole Cookie header values · JSON
  credentials and encoded param names · separators `, ; { |` besides `?&#` and spaces · values stop at separators ·
  depth ceiling truncates (`'[truncated]'`) · redaction by key name for any value type · writes verified
  (`if (target[key] === next) return;`), copy frozen/getter objects once · `seen` is a `Map` · cycles ➜ `[circular]` ·
  keep `barcode` · `keyId` · `id` · `count` visible.
- `appLocalStore` / `appSessionStore` are the single storage path (read `window.localStorage` in a `try` once; may be
  `null`) ⛔ `safeStoreSet(localStorage, …)` isn't enough (the getter throws at the call site) ⛔ never judge by
  `typeof <fn> === 'function'`.
- `clearSensitiveModalFields()` is the single credential cleaner; new state ➜ add it to `setup-link-logout-test.js`.

## ⛔ App lock

- Never touches the 4-hour session (no `fb.signOut()`, keep `zoew_login_time`/`remembered_email`).
- `lookupSecretKey` survives reload: `CryptoKey` in IndexedDB (`zoew_lookup_key_v1`, `extractable: false`); restore
  refuses when `appLockShouldArm()` or the record isn't a CryptoKey; every IndexedDB path fails open with a 3s ceiling.
  Before assigning a completed read, recheck captured auth generation and key identity, `appIsLocked`, and
  `appLockShouldArm()`; late reads must not resurrect a cleared key or replace a newer key (`tests/lookup-key-restore-idb.test.ts`).
  Boot alone may request one fresh read after initial auth arrives: same restore owner, saved backend config and
  unlocked session, with no initial auth object. The retry uses the strict default and fences auth/user identity,
  generation, key and config again; forget/session cleanup invalidate the owner. Never reuse the first read's key.
- Unlock flag in `sessionStorage` (`zoew_app_unlocked`) ⛔ not `localStorage` (PTR reloads).
- Leave/return: (1) `hidden` ➜ cover with `showAppLockScreen(true)` without touching the flag · (2) `visible` ➜ lock (clear
  the flag) + silent `runAppLockBiometric(true)` · (3) intentional exits excused via `noteAppLockExcuse()` +
  `APP_LOCK_EXCUSE_WINDOW_MS` (60s, one round) ⛔ never `blur`/`focus` ⛔ never remove the excuse. A permission prompt is an intentional exit
  (Android pauses the Activity): `noteAppLockExcuse()` right before it — Config QR camera (`getUserMedia`) · APK notification permission only
  when a prompt can appear (`checkPermissions()` neither granted nor denied) (`tests/native/app-lock-away.test.tsx` · `tests/push-client.test.tsx`).
- `body.app-locked` hides `.modal` · `.more-menu` · `.phone-suggest` · `.toast-container` and the rest, with
  `visibility: hidden` + opaque `var(--body-bg)`.
- `safeFocusScanner()` returns when `appIsLocked`; `pullTargetBlocked()` counts `appIsLocked`.
- Locks only when `zoew_security_pin_hash` exists ⛔ never force PIN setup at boot; "ភ្លេច PIN?" exit required.
- `initAppLock()` runs in boot (`src/app/lifecycle/boot.ts`, `oncePerPage`) before data rendering.
- Toasts shown while locked (`.toast-container` is hidden) keep their lifetime: `armToastDismiss()` holds the timer and `hideAppLockScreen()` ➜
  `releaseHeldToasts()` starts it (live toasts re-read the truth first) ⛔ never let them expire unseen.

## ZTO Lookup

- `ZoeW/netlify/functions/zto-order-detail.mjs` is the sole modern default entry; its official Lambda adapter wraps
  `_shared/zto-order-detail-core.cjs` with a fixed static Blobs SDK. Only privately marked modern events use zero-config
  `getStore()` without `connectLambda`; legacy events stay explicit and fail closed. No request-supplied SDK/context or
  global context copying. The public function route stays unchanged;
  explicit Palm login/lookup uses server-only `_shared/zto-palm.js` (core-table row «ZTO Palm lookup»). ⛔ `puppeteer-core` and
  `@sparticuz/chromium` banned ⛔ no auto-login without proof the IdP accepts foreign servers.
- Budgets: `ZTO_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS` (6s) per request · `ZTO_REQUEST_BUDGET_MS` (9s) total (app budget; Netlify sync
  functions allow 60s, streaming 10s) · client `ZTO_AUTO_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_MS` 13s · `ZTO_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS` 11s. Budgets via
  `elapsedSince()`; settling structural.
- "Not found" = 200 `{ found: false }` without `error` (an `error` field triggers the 30s cooldown).
- Server cache key `config.fingerprint + '|' + barcode` (no Cookie); single-flight key `config|barcode|cookie`; cache
  check before `resolveCookieCredential()`; `notFound` cached 15s; transient failures (401 · 5xx · 429 · timeout) never
  cached; negative TTL capped by `cacheTtlMs` (`ZTO_CACHE_TTL_MS=0` disables both; `ZTO_NOT_FOUND_CACHE_TTL_MS=0` only
  negative).
- `resolveCookieCredential()`: `ZTO_AUTHORIZATION` / `ZTO_TOKEN` ➜ never touch the store; else blob ➜ `ZTO_COOKIE` env.
  Blobs is never a single point of failure. `connectLambda(event)` sets only `edgeURL` ➜ `consistency: 'strong'` throws
  ⛔ never "fix" with a PAT.
- `getStore('zto-auth')` = store `site:zto-auth`; the helper writes `/api/v1/blobs/{siteID}/site:zto-auth/cookie`;
  checkers read the name from the Function.
- A ZTO 401 clears that cache (`invalidateCookieCache()`); `?diag=1` reports the reason (8-char fingerprint only).
- Auth verdicts belong to the Cookie ZTO measured (new Sync ➜ "unverified"; late answers never judge a newer Cookie);
  messages say "rejected"; timeouts aren't auth verdicts.
- Renewals: `getWithMetadata()` + `set()` with `onlyIfMatch`/`onlyIfNew`; `modified:false` = conflict; `modified:true`
  without ETag isn't proof; a changed `BOS-MAN-SESSION` writes at once; pending survives budgets/timeouts/stale reads; a
  failed fresh read never deletes a working Cookie.
- Consecutive scans: capped parcels go to `autoLookupQueueRetries`; every early exit calls
  `dropAutoLookupQueueEntry(lookupKey)` (except the requeue path); skip scheduling only when `existing && existing.timer`
  ⛔ never on `waiting`; concurrency ceiling stays 2.
- `lookupResponseError(status, body, retryable)` is the single error builder (`lookupCode` · `lookupReason`) ⛔ no bare
  `new Error('HTTP ' + status)`; `err.noRetry` honored; cooldown transient 6s · permanent 30s.
- `customerTablePrefetchAllowed()` gate (no modal, no lookup in flight); the ladder's first step > 
  `CUSTOMER_TABLE_FAIL_COOLDOWN_MS`; busy ➜ reschedule.
- `seedCustomerTableFromImport()` fills (never clears) and mirrors server import modes (`replace`/`upsert`/`newOnly`, last
  row wins); `runSheetImportClear()` still clears; `fresh=1` only for forced pulls; server key `customer_rows_v2_<lastRow>`.
- ⛔ Apps Script requests are simple requests (`Content-Type: text/plain;charset=utf-8`, no custom headers) on every path;
  `lookupApiSendsHeader(cfg)` skips header and PIN for Apps Script (hostname match, not substring); tell the user, no
  silent drops.
- `fetch()` doesn't reject on HTTP errors ➜ 408/425/429/5xx become rejections inside `retryAsync()`; 401/403 stay outside.

## ZTO Cookie Sync on Windows (`tools/zto-cookie-sync-windows/`)

Chain: `sync-zto-cookie.cmd ➜ Edge/Chrome ➜ Netlify Blobs (site:zto-auth, key cookie)`, no redeploy. Fallback trigger:
Scan Management ➜ Arrival Scan ➜ a Waybill (`POST https://aargus-api.ztoglobal.com/scan/get/order/detail`).

- Start at `gate.ztoglobal.com` (keeps the session); `argus.ztoglobal.com` stays printed as fallback ⛔ never remove.
  `BOS-MAN-SESSION` belongs to `aargus-api.ztoglobal.com`; `argus` Cookies (`__zcat_uuid__` · `ZTO_INTL_BOS_MAN_TOKEN`)
  can't replace it ➜ `validateCookieHeader()` requires it.
- `openArgusFromPortal()` opens a new tab to `ARGUS_URL` directly (no DOM link scan) after 3 gates: capture not done · no
  Argus tab (`isArgusHost()` ⛔ not a loose `endsWith`) · gate page settled (`waitForLoadState` with a ceiling); fully
  fail-open; opened tabs are left alone. `watchApiTraffic()` reports counts only on failure (outside the capture path,
  no URLs, no Cookies).
- Cookies shown on cmd by user request ⛔ PAT and `ZTO_PROXY_KEY` never (AST scan of value flow).
- cmd text ASCII English; `.cmd` pure ASCII + CRLF.
- Unverifiable pairs skipped (names only); CR/LF/NUL ➜ reject the jar; no valid `BOS-MAN-SESSION` ➜ reject; the server
  applies the same rules.
- PAT via `Read-Host -AsSecureString`, DPAPI/CurrentUser in `%LOCALAPPDATA%`, stdout pipe only (`shell:false`) ⛔ never on
  command lines or Netlify env ⛔ never sent to the signed-URL host; the DPAPI child finishes within 30s and cleans up.
- Setup reruns with Enter = keep · no silent drops (report typed vs required length) · gates measure the decrypted value.
- `--auto` opens a browser only when `status === 'ok' && healthy === false`; `mismatch` isn't `error`.
- Netlify timeouts cover the whole body; streams over 1 MB refused; broken EOF retryable; 401/403 never retried
  (`zto-network-boundaries-test.js`).
- Capture waits for a response proving the session (HTTPS `GET`/`POST` on the real host, **any path**); 401/403 ·
  redirects/IdP · OPTIONS/HEAD · bad JSON never prove it; read fully, merge matching `Set-Cookie`, keep the ceiling, remove
  listeners ⛔ never lock a specific path (`zto-cookie-capture-test.js` · `zto-cookie-sync-test.js`).
- Same fingerprint proves storage only (accepted/rejected/not measured).
- ⛔ Old extension env stays deleted: `ZTO_COOKIE_UPDATE_KEY` · `NETLIFY_AUTH_TOKEN` · `NETLIFY_ACCOUNT_ID` ·
  `NETLIFY_SITE_ID`.

## Excel import · `zto-import` · Export · Setup Link · Backup

- Replace grows the grid and writes new data before clearing extra rows; rejected writes never delete (`zto-import/test.js`).
- The dialog opens only via `requestPinBeforeConfig(openSheetImportModal, 'sheetImport')` ⛔ not in `ACTION_ALLOWLIST`.
- Salts never change: `zoew_sheet_import_secret_v1` · `zoeadmin_pin_verify_v2` · `zoeadmin_lookup_api_secret_v1` ·
  `LICENSE_APP_CODE` = `'ZOE'` (inside signatures `payload.a`, a Firebase path and key `zoe_license_activation_` + code ➜
  changing it breaks every Key; `license-app-code-test`).
- Wrong PIN ➜ setup form, never delete the record; logout ➜ `clearSheetImportSession()` keeps the encrypted connection.
- `sheetImportReadOptions(bytes)`: `raw: true` only for non-containers (ZIP `50 4b` · OLE `d0 cf 11 e0`, by bytes)
  ⛔ never for `.xlsx`.
- SheetJS is vendored (`ZoeW/public/vendor/xlsx.full.min.js`) ⛔ no CDN ⛔ no plaintext `IMPORT_PASSWORD` in storage.
- `zto-import/` (standalone, writes) and `zto-import/google-sheets-api/` (bound, reads) are separate projects with separate
  `CacheService`; `Code.gs` fails closed without ScriptProperty `API_KEY`.
- PDF export uses browser print-to-PDF ⛔ no jsPDF (can't shape Khmer).
- Phones and barcodes are TEXT: `XLSX.writeFile(..., { bookSST: true })` + `forceExportTextCells()`
  (`EXPORT_TEXT_COLUMN_INDEXES = [1, 2]`); check the emitted XML.
- `csvSafeText()` prefixes `'` to `=` `+` `-` `@` ⛔ never on all of `csvEscape` (`sheetsText()` starts with `=` on purpose).
- Setup Link: `applySetupLinkFromUrl()` goes through the PIN gate, no auto-save (destination card `configPendingLink` +
  one tap ⛔ never auto-save a URL link — it can come from anyone); in-modal QR/image/paste save at once via
  `connectSetupPayload()` (a rejected config ➜ not saved, manual section opens); ZoeKeyGen emits the payload in the fragment
  (`#setup=` ➜ never in server logs, `Referer` or Service Worker requests) and ZoeW reads `#setup=` first, still accepts `?setup=`
  (provisioning tool · printed QR) and removes both via `history.replaceState`; a link opened in a tab that already runs ZoeW changes only the fragment ➜ the same read runs on `hashchange` (`listenSetupLinkNavigation()`); abandoned links don't survive logout, active PIN flows keep them (`isPinFlowPending()`).
  Lookup-only links require an existing shop; combined links accept Firebase/Supabase. Lookup metadata (including HTTPS URL and optional Supabase project/tenant `shop` target)
  is authenticated with AES-GCM; `keyEnc` (`v: 2`, AAD prefix `zoe-setup-lookup-v2`) uses PBKDF2-SHA256 and a separately delivered
  cryptographically random 16-character Crockford code (80 bits, `XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`; `lookupSetupCodeText()` in both apps
  normalizes case · spaces · hyphens and Unicode dashes · invisible format characters · Khmer digits ០–៩ · O/I/L) ⛔ never a short numeric code (an offline guess of a leaked Link) · `v: 1` is refused.
  Wrong code/cancel/session change writes nothing. Re-encrypt Header under the PIN `lookupSecretKey`, bind to
  `shopScope()` (untargeted Supabase links bind once tenant is known; targeted links keep their authenticated tenant and reject project mismatches), clear customer/ZTO caches and refresh shop UI. Never persist or
  report plaintext Header/code; seller logout clears them. A lookup URL never carries a secret-named query parameter (`lookupSetupSecretName()`,
  byte-identical in both apps: long words anywhere in the name — `token` · `secret` · `password` · `apikey` · … —, short words only as a whole
  part of it — `key` · `pin` · `auth` · …; the ZoeKeyGen alert and README list its words; both apps' `lookupSetupMetadata()` refuse it, and so
  does ZoeW's lookup dialog (`lookupUrlSecretParams()` ➜ put the secret in the encrypted Header); the Sentry redactor hides every such
  name in strings (`SECRET_PARAM_INFIX_RE` + `SECRET_PARAM_PATTERN` ⊇ its words, `secret-hygiene` derives them from the function) · an
  Apps Script `key=` is moved by ZoeKeyGen into the code-sealed secret (`secretParam: 'key'`, part of the AAD ➜ an App that does not know it
  fails closed) · ZoeKeyGen removes the key from its URL field only after a successful
  generation (the field still holding the URL it read; logout and the session lock scrub it too) · an Apps Script lookup without a non-empty
  `key` is refused with its own alert, and one with `key` plus a Header name or secret too (ZoeW never sends a Header to Apps Script).
  ZoeW keeps an Apps Script `key` only encrypted under the PIN key like the Header (owner decision): `urlKeyEnc`, with the stored and shown URL
  without it — Setup Link · the lookup dialog (a pasted `&key=…` is split off by `lookupUrlSplitKey()`; a keyless Apps Script URL keeps the
  stored key; another API drops it) ·
  every request attaches it when sent (`lookupKeyedUrl()` ➜ `lookupUrlWithKey()`: scan lookup · customer table · 🧪 test), percent-encoded completely
  (`'()*!~` included ⛔ never a raw «'»: the redactor ends a query value there and the rest of the key reached Sentry) · locked PIN
  (`lookupSecretLocked()`, Header or URL key) ➜ the scan asks for the PIN and the table is not pulled ⛔ never a request without its key.
  ZoeKeyGen's session lock wipes the code together with the Link and QR it seals (a Link without its code opens nothing).
  Guards: `tests/setup-lookup.test.tsx` · `tests/apps-script-key-encrypted.test.tsx` · `tests/apps-script-key-redaction.test.ts` · `tests/setup-link-hashchange.test.tsx` ·
  `setup-link-browser-test` (open tab) · `setup-link-roundtrip-test` (README form) · `secret-hygiene` · `lookup-config-secret-test` · `keygen-session-security-test`.
- `firebase-backup/`: Node.js 18+ CLI, no dependencies; `config.json` · `secrets/` · `backups/` gitignored ⛔ never commit
  service-account keys; `.partial` ➜ `renameSync()`; runs by hand/Task Scheduler and via `backup.yml` (cron `0 19 * * *`):
  `ci-config.js` ➜ `backup.js` ➜ `crypt.js seal` (AES-256-GCM via scrypt) ⛔ artifacts never hold plaintext (seal deletes
  `.json.gz`, verification fails before upload, decrypt-verify before writing) ⛔ keys never in the checkout (`if: always()`
  cleanup, `contents: read`) · `ZOE_BACKUP_TARGETS` array (separate folders; customer keys with role
  `Firebase Realtime Database Viewer`; artifact quota = round size × `ZOE_BACKUP_RETENTION_DAYS`) · `continue-on-error`
  on pulls and a final failing step **after** upload ⛔ never move it up · GitHub pauses schedules after 60 quiet days
  (📝-type: check Actions first).

## Firebase rules — current shape

- Root default-deny; `auth != null` is the only permission (Business).
- Schema validation and `$other: { ".validate": false }` ⛔ never remove.
- Claim/witness fences on `zoew_restore_finalizations` / `zoew_clear_history_finalizations` ⛔ never remove; a witness
  never requires `!data.exists()` (3-way deadlock). Replay is blocked by "claim token in the trash item before the update,
  item deleted in the same update".
- `license_keys/$appCode/$keyId` publicly readable (`expiresAt` · `revoked` · `maxDevices` · `releasedAt`); `license_seats/$appCode/$keyId` is also publicly readable by key ID, including device metadata.
  Keep signing keys and seller notes out of these public nodes; seller metadata belongs in `license_keys_meta` (admin only).
- Object nodes require objects (`newData.hasChildren()`) ⛔ never remove; `.validate` doesn't run on deletes.
- `ZoeKeyGen/firebase-database.rules.json` keeps `user_roles` and `admin` on purpose.

## ⛔ Netlify: 2 sites, one repo

- Base directory per site: «Accepted by design»; "base directory does not exist" ➜ check its case in the Netlify UI first.
- ⛔ No root `netlify.toml` and no `base` key anywhere.
- Config matches what each app ships (derived): `.wasm` ➜ `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` + `Content-Type = "application/wasm"` ·
  `netlify/functions/*.{js,cjs,mjs}` ➜ `functions = ` to a real dir · `script.google.com` in `connect-src` when used · reverse:
  apps without them aren't forced.
- Every config production reads has a checker reading it. ⛔ Bot-proposed fixes get the same scrutiny: verify the claimed
  cause with `grep` (`netlify-config-scope-test.js`).

# 🔬 Checker discipline

## 13 questions before believing a checker

1. Which files does it scan/run?
2. Which syntax forms (template literals vs concatenation)?
3. Which directions (in **and** out)?
4. Can it fail? ➜ `node audit-tools/checker-coverage.js` (empty `*_APP_DIR`)
5. Can it hang? ➜ `node audit-tools/hang-guard.js`
6. Does failure reach the exit code? ➜ `node audit-tools/exit-code-integrity.js`
7. Does any tool see the seam between 2 files? (a stubbed seam is an untested seam)
8. What state is the system in before asserting? (scenarios must enter the buggy state, not avoid it by timing)
9. Which failure modes do dependencies get (reject · absent · hang · slow success)?
10. Does it see promise rejections (`addInitScript` converters before `.goto()`)?
11. Real failure or measurement artifact? ➜ reverse probe + minimum floor.
12. `PASS` or `SKIP`? ⛔ `SKIP` = couldn't measure ➜ eliminate before claiming green.
13. Does the measurer point at the right tree? `grep -n 'process.env\.[A-Z_]*APP_DIR' audit-tools/<checker>.js` and make
    it fail on purpose once.

## Core tool rules

- Static checkers lock names; behavioral checkers lock outcomes ➜ browser-measurable classes deserve behavioral checkers.
- Absence assertions need minimum floors (count files too).
- Every checker supports `*_APP_DIR` and is called in the `run-all.sh` baseline.
- ⛔ Never exit when a function isn't found — stub it (exiting masks every later assertion).
- ⛔ No single `try` around all scenarios; `await scenario()` for async ones.
- ⛔ Every scenario starts from original stubs; add precondition assertions.
- ⛔ `listen(0, '127.0.0.1')`; `emu/*` namespaces from `emu/ns.js` (`emuNamespace()`).
- ⛔ Label-only `ok()` never called conditionally; count arguments with a real parser.
- ⛔ `checker-coverage.js` skips part 5 under `EXITCODE_CHILD`; hash snapshots skip `.tmp-poison-*`.
- ⛔ A SKIP marker starts its line (`run-all.sh` judges `^\s*SKIP`; a mid-line «— SKIP» reads as a full PASS) ➜ `checker-coverage`.
- ⛔ Poison shadow files (`.tmp-poison-<pid>-<name>.js`), never originals.
- ⛔ Depth parameters default to at least the depth measured as necessary.
- ⛔ Ceiling assertions use the real ceiling (`cap + N`).
- ⛔ Fuzz floors derive from `RUNS` with a reserved share per state (never a fixed number).
- ⛔ Doc assertions measure facts, not wording; default numbers in docs are read from code; code-text assertions derive the
  function that provides the guarantee instead of hard-coding helper names, and still fail when nothing provides it.
- ⛔ A sandbox constant (ceiling · timeout · view-key list · cleanup constant) is read from the shipped code, never hand-copied: a
  copied value keeps the checker green when the app's value changes; an owner-fixed value (concurrency 2 · 2 hours · 7×24h) is also
  pinned. A storage failure is injected at the storage layer, never by replacing the helper that is supposed to survive it.
- ⛔ Never write assertions that lock a bug in.
- ⛔ Fuzz asserts after **every** operation, not only at the end.
- Mutation testing asks **who** catches it. Count detection only after completed behavioral assertions with coherent
  summary counts; startup, dependency, syntax and timeout failures are infrastructure failures. Keep mutated aggregate
  source and extracted module views equivalent. A real-types claim needs an invalid-API control against those types,
  not an unrelated declaration import or permissive shim.
- ⛔ Measure a suspected gap before building a tool for it; delete tools that duplicate existing coverage.
- Sandbox breaks after adding a helper are a good sign (the checker runs real code).

---

# 📘 Runbook

## Step 0 — setup (once per session)

Verify the checkout and remote first (`git status --short --branch`, `git remote -v`, `git fetch origin main`).
Keep existing local changes intact; compare the fetched commit before updating and pin the revision under review.
Read the latest pending items in `docs/HISTORY.md` before relying on a rule as implemented.
The shell examples below use Bash on Linux/WSL; browser and emulator prerequisites must match the host.
A Linux container whose preinstalled Playwright Chromium is another revision: link it where the checkers look, as `audit.yml`
does (`/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux` ➜ the installed `chrome-linux*` folder · `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium` ➜ its
`chrome`) ⛔ otherwise browser checkers report a missing browser (SKIP).
Windows without WSL (Git Bash): checkers default to `/opt/pw-browsers/…` ➜ export every `ZOEW_TEST_BROWSER`/`*_CHROME`
variable named in those `process.env.X || '/opt/pw-browsers…'` defaults to an installed Chrome before building the measure
tree · `@embedded-postgres/linux-x64` has no Windows build ➜ real-Postgres Supabase guards SKIP there · start the emulator jar as
a background task (`setsid`/`/tmp` are Linux-only) · a system proxy that swallows `127.0.0.1` makes every browser checker time out on `page.goto` ➜ launch Chrome with `--no-proxy-server` (a `NODE_OPTIONS=--require` preload that adds it to `chromium.launch`; nothing in the repo changes) ⛔ a local SKIP is never evidence; the 4 CI shards are.

⛔ The full run belongs to GitHub CI (owner request: the session has few CPUs, a local full run is slower than CI and
competes with the work). Finish the round ➜ the fast checks of Step 3 in the session ➜ **only when publication is authorized**, push ➜ wait for
`.github/workflows/audit.yml`: **4 parallel shards** (`RUNALL_SHARD=k/4`, an emulator per shard, STRICT flags below) ➜
CI green = all 4 green ⛔ never report a round done before every shard is green. A red shard ➜ read its log, reproduce
only that checker in the session (`RUNALL_ONLY` or the measure tree below), fix, and publish again within the authorized scope. A full local run (below)
is a fallback only when CI cannot run (outage · runner mode without runners); reproducing a red shard normally stays targeted.
An audit-only report must name its inspected commit, completed checks and missing coverage. Cite verified CI with its exact SHA;
never present stale CI or unrun checks as validation of a changed tree. Without publication permission, stop at the local result and disclose that CI on the changed tree remains unverified; this runbook grants no permission to push, open a PR, merge or deploy.

```bash
npm ci --prefix ZoeW          # ZoeW React: vite · acorn · playwright-core · firebase (real SDK for emu/tx-disconnect · emu/app-network-e2e)
npm ci --prefix supabase      # Supabase guards: real Postgres (@embedded-postgres) · pg · supabase-js · typescript
npm ci --prefix tools/firebase-provision --ignore-scripts   # real firebase-tools for firebase-provision-test
```

- `run-all.sh` builds the measure tree itself; use that tree for guards that consume generated app text/markup. One such checker:
  `M=$(ZOE_MEASURE_ONLY=1 bash audit-tools/run-all.sh | tail -1)` then `(cd "$M" && node audit-tools/<x>.js)`.
- Full runs include real network interruption matrices and can take longer than ordinary checks. Run in the background and inspect the complete final verdict before reporting success.
- Parallel and resumable ([`audit-tools/README.md`](audit-tools/README.md) section 2): `RUNALL_JOBS` lanes · state in
  `<git-dir>/zoe-runall-state.tsv` · `RUNALL_RESUME=1` with the same `*_STRICT` flags carries only full PASS and retries failed/unfinished/skipped/partial
  checkers ⛔ never stitch logs · a changed tree ➜ RESUME refuses · `RUNALL_ONLY` / `RUNALL_SHARD` are incomplete runs ·
  one run-all per repo (lock `<git-dir>/zoe-runall-measure.lock`, exit 2; copy the repo to measure in parallel) · new
  emulator/shadow checkers enter `runall_lane()` · `RUNALL_JOBS=1` for serial order.

⛔ **No RTDB emulator ➜ every `emu/*` checker degrades — `SKIP` is not `PASS`.** Count from the real directory
(`audit-tools/emu/*-test.js` plus `crud-rules-flow.js`). Two shapes: `emu/restore-deadlock` · `emu/ledger-revert` ·
`emu/license-seat-rules` · `emu/tx-disconnect` · `emu/app-writes-rules` · `emu/app-network-e2e` · `emu/money-multidevice` ·
`emu/supabase-rules-parity` · `emu/supabase-adapter-parity` print **`SKIPPED`**; `emu/crud-rules-flow` and
`emu/restore-mutation` print **`PARTIAL PASS (… SKIP …)`** — it contains the word `PASS` while the real-rules assertions
never ran. Always read the "partial" and "skipped" numbers of the summary. A local run that must count (CI unavailable ·
reproducing a red shard) runs like CI:

```bash
./tools/firebase-provision/node_modules/.bin/firebase setup:emulators:database
setsid nohup java -jar ~/.cache/firebase/emulators/firebase-database-emulator-*.jar \
    --port 9000 --host 127.0.0.1 > /tmp/emu.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &
cp firebase-database.rules.json audit-tools/emu/real.rules.json
curl -s "http://127.0.0.1:9000/.json?ns=x"    # must answer null — otherwise the emulator is dead
CRUD_FLOW_STRICT=1 VERSIONSCOPE_STRICT=1 MONEYGUARD_STRICT=1 SUPABASE_STRICT=1 FBPROVISION_STRICT=1 bash audit-tools/run-all.sh
```

- The emulator can die when its shell call ends ➜ run it as a long-lived background task and `curl` again before run-all.
- `money-guardian-test.js` runs only inside run-all (`MONEYGUARD_STRICT=1`).
- ⚠️ **Never `pkill -f <pattern>`** — any pattern appearing in your own command kills your shell. Let processes finish, use
  `timeout`, or `kill <pid>` after checking the PID isn't your shell.

## Step 1 — read real code, try uncovered scenarios

Read real paths (`await` points · session switches · late callbacks) and try behavior. Priorities: new tool classes ·
newly shipped code (`git log`) · real user reports (Sentry, videos).

## Step 2 — rules for every test

Tests extract **real** code into `vm` or run a real browser — never a copy — and must fail on the pre-fix tree:

```bash
git fetch origin main
BASE_REF=$(git rev-parse origin/main)     # or a verified commit before your change
BASE_DIR=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/zoe-baseline.XXXXXX")
git archive "$BASE_REF" | tar -x -C "$BASE_DIR"
RUNALL_ONLY='<checker-name>' bash audit-tools/run-all.sh "$BASE_DIR"
```

Archive the **pre-fix** tree, not `HEAD`. Replace the checker placeholder with the relevant guard; a focused run is not a full-suite verdict.
Keep the unique baseline directory until its evidence is recorded. Mutations must be real — `grep` that they landed in the real file.

## Step 3 — before commit

```bash
node --check <edited .js files>
node audit-tools/strip-comments.js --check                     # documentation-only; omit --check after source edits
npm --prefix ZoeW run typecheck                                # builds the referenced app project, not the empty solution config
npm --prefix ZoeW run lint
(cd ZoeW && npx vitest run tests/<files of the area>)          # never the whole suite: CI runs it
M=$(ZOE_MEASURE_ONLY=1 bash audit-tools/run-all.sh | tail -1)    # then the checkers of the area: (cd "$M" && node audit-tools/<x>.js)
git diff "$BASE_REF" -- ZoeW/src ZoeW/public ZoeKeyGen | grep '^-'   # unexplained deletions = regressions
```

Apply non-negotiable rules 6 and 8 for version scope and the history entry; documentation-only work never needs an app bump.
`version-check` · `version-bump-scope` · `doc-scope-test` run on the measure tree, never directly on the repo (they fail
falsely there); `doc-scope-test` also needs `ZOE_REPO_ROOT=<repo>` (the measure tree has no `ZoeW/tests`). Follow the publication and CI conditions in Step 0.
If the audit build stops before browser prerender, its generated source view is incomplete: name that limited input and each failed or unrun stage, not a full measure-root PASS.

### ⛔ Impact verification — end of every task (user request)

| # | Question | How |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | What was deleted? | `git diff "$BASE_REF" -- <shipped dirs> \| grep '^-'` — every line explained |
| 2 | How much existing code changed? | `git diff "$BASE_REF" -U3` — separate new code from edits to old code |
| 3 | Who uses what I changed? | `grep` every user of changed shapes/return values/fields |
| 4 | Did behavior change? | run the function **before** (`git show "$BASE_REF":<file>`) and **after** in `vm` on the same cases |
| 5 | Can a new hot-path helper throw? | run it on `undefined` · `null` · `NaN` · object · array |
| 6 | Shared helper? | `error-reporting.js` · `license-verify.js` ship in both apps ➜ bump both |

Green `run-all.sh` is not the answer — step 4 is the strongest proof.

## Harness traps

- `snapshot.val()` returns a deep copy.
- Read store state as `window.<field>` only in the audit build after `waitAuditBridge()`.
- ⛔ `src/services/supabase-*.ts` never import `domain/**` or `ui/**`: Vite reassigns the `supabase-backend` chunk's closure, the audit text view's module order changes and slice-based checkers (`clear-history-claim-test`) read another module's constants ➜ hand helpers to the adapter through `supabaseEnv()` (`ledgerCompanion`).
- ⛔ In TS sources never declare a local named like a store field (`const fb = firebaseState.fb;` becomes `const fb = fb;`
  in the text view) — `build-audit.mjs` refuses it. The view starts with `\n`.
- The fake SDK supports `fb.increment()` and should enforce real `.validate`.
- Race tests put the other device's write **between** read and write.
- New SW control needs empty `installing`/`waiting` **and** old caches deleted (`OPTIONAL_SHELL` installs after
  `CORE_SHELL`).
- Clear `tr.dataset.sig` before measuring `renderHistory` performance.
- Cleanup fuzz ages stamps per barcode.
- `vm` sandboxes need `setTimeout`: use `(fn, ms) => ms >= 10000 ? setTimeout(fn, ms) : (fn(), 0)`; `Date` must be a real
  constructor.
- A throwing `extractConst()` makes `|| fallback` useless.
- Environment-dependent coverage is accidental ➜ write both paths on purpose.
- `String.replace(from, to)` interprets `$$` ➜ use `replace(from, () => to)`.
- Playwright `page.clock` (`setFixedTime` · `install`) also fakes `requestAnimationFrame` and `performance.now()`: a callback is a
  timer tick, not a painted frame ➜ frame-by-frame motion is measured per real frame (`document.timeline.currentTime`, the clock
  that drives Web Animations: samples of one frame merge · steps only between frames ≤ 20ms apart) ⛔ never on the rAF timestamp
  (under load two ticks land in one frame ➜ a 0 step, then a double step).
- React commits a dialog in a microtask ➜ a fuzz op or checker that reads the dialog right after opening it finds nothing and becomes a silent no-op ➜ wait for the commit (`commitNow()` + a bounded poll) and floor on real effects (saved · landed), never on «the op was picked».
- A test proxy that cuts a server reply records the request ids (`r`) it targets and cuts only their reply ⛔ never the first
  `"s":"ok"` it sees (a listen ack that arrives late under load is one too ➜ the cut lands before the write).
- Windows timers round `setTimeout(fn, 1)` up to ~16ms ➜ a fixed wait for a chain of fake 1ms round trips passes on Linux and
  flakes on Windows ➜ wait for the precondition itself (`vi.waitFor` with a ceiling), never a fixed `sleep()`.

## RTDB emulator for rules changes

Start it per **step 0** (never copy those commands elsewhere), then:

```bash
cp firebase-database.rules.json audit-tools/emu/real.rules.json
bash audit-tools/emu/rules.sh
```

- CLI `emulators:start` can't upload rules through the proxy — run the jar.
- `.settings/rules.json` and `auth_variable_override` need `-H "Authorization: Bearer owner"`.
- `Bearer owner` without `auth_variable_override` bypasses rules ➜ unauthenticated tests send no Authorization header.
- Assert a known-bad write is refused before trusting results.

---

# ⚠️ Expected error patterns — don't "fix"

- `"<X> timed out"` — intentional `withTimeout()`; the Error is built synchronously at the call site.
- `permission_denied` at `repoRerunTransactionQueue` / reconnect — offline transactions replayed after auth/rules changes.
- `"Daily/Monthly revenue underflow clamped to 0"` — safety net (frequent for one day/month ➜ suspect duplicate deduction).
- "⚠️ ទិន្នន័យនេះលែងមានក្នុងប្រព័ន្ធ!" — item gone on the server (frequent ➜ check the device clock).
- False positives: `ResizeObserver` (guarded) · `navigator.credentials` (`biometricPlatformAvailable()` + `try`) ·
  `crypto.subtle` (absent only where the app can't run).
- `waitForZXingThenInitScanEngine()` uses the raw clock on purpose.
- All 8 ZoeW `setInterval` timers run while hidden ⛔ never add a `document.hidden` gate (exception inside a cycle:
  `probeDatabaseLivenessIfIdle()` skips while `hidden`; the Sign outbox wakes and refreshes its view but starts network jobs only while visible, unlocked and online).
- `revenue-fuzz` doesn't cover "7 days + mixed parcel" — `partial-pickup-cleanup-test` does.
- ZTO ⛔ no global circuit breaker (`ZTO_AUTH_EXPIRED` is already `noRetry`).
